
// ==================================================
// File: client\ChestScreenOverlay.java
// ==================================================

package com.example.examplemod.client;

import com.example.examplemod.ExampleMod;
import dev.shadowsoffire.apotheosis.Apoth;
import dev.shadowsoffire.apotheosis.socket.gem.Purity;
import io.redspace.ironsspellbooks.api.spells.ISpellContainer;
import io.redspace.ironsspellbooks.api.spells.SpellData;
import io.redspace.ironsspellbooks.api.spells.SpellRarity;
import io.redspace.ironsspellbooks.item.Scroll;
import net.minecraft.client.gui.GuiGraphics;
import net.minecraft.core.component.DataComponents;
import net.minecraft.nbt.CompoundTag;
import net.minecraft.world.inventory.Slot;
import net.minecraft.world.item.ItemStack;
import net.minecraft.world.item.component.CustomData;
import net.neoforged.api.distmarker.Dist;
import net.neoforged.bus.api.SubscribeEvent;
import net.neoforged.fml.common.EventBusSubscriber;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.client.event.ContainerScreenEvent;

import java.util.Locale;

@EventBusSubscriber(modid = ExampleMod.MODID, value = Dist.CLIENT)
public class ChestScreenOverlay {

    // 推荐的 Z 轴深度：高于物品贴图(150~200)，低于 Tooltip(400)
    private static final int OVERLAY_Z = 300;

    @SubscribeEvent
    public static void onRenderGuiForeground(ContainerScreenEvent.Render.Foreground event) {
        var guiGraphics = event.getGuiGraphics();
        var screen = event.getContainerScreen();

        for (Slot slot : screen.getMenu().slots) {
            if (!slot.isActive() || !slot.hasItem()) {
                continue;
            }

            ItemStack stack = slot.getItem();
            int x = slot.x;
            int y = slot.y;

            // =========================================================================
            // 1. 神化装备 RARITY 指示点（左上角）
            // =========================================================================
            if (stack.has(Apoth.Components.RARITY)) {
                var rarityHolder = stack.get(Apoth.Components.RARITY);
                if (rarityHolder != null && rarityHolder.isBound()) {
                    int argbColor = rarityHolder.get().color().getValue() | 0xFF000000;
                    renderPip(guiGraphics, x + 1, y + 1, argbColor);
                }
            }

            // =========================================================================
            // 2. 铁魔法卷轴稀有度指示点（左上角，支持未鉴定卷轴 + 原版已鉴定卷轴）
            // =========================================================================
            if (stack.getItem() instanceof Scroll) {
                Integer scrollColor = getScrollRarityColor(stack);
                if (scrollColor != null) {
                    renderPip(guiGraphics, x + 1, y + 1, scrollColor);
                }
            }

            // =========================================================================
            // 3. 神化宝石 PURITY 指示点（右上角，完美品质动态变色）
            // =========================================================================
            if (stack.has(Apoth.Components.PURITY)) {
                Purity purity = stack.get(Apoth.Components.PURITY);
                if (purity != null && purity.getColor() != null) {
                    int argbColor = purity.getColor().getValue() | 0xFF000000;
                    int size = 3;
                    int rightX = slot.x + 16 - size - 1; // 距右边界 1 像素
                    renderPip(guiGraphics, rightX, y + 1, argbColor);
                }
            }
        }
    }

    /**
     * 获取铁魔法卷轴的品质颜色（支持未鉴定卷轴与已鉴定成品卷轴）
     */
    private static Integer getScrollRarityColor(ItemStack stack) {
        // A. 检查是否为未鉴定卷轴
        CustomData customData = stack.getOrDefault(DataComponents.CUSTOM_DATA, CustomData.EMPTY);
        CompoundTag tag = customData.copyTag();
        if (tag.getBoolean("is_unidentified") && tag.contains("spell_rarity")) {
            try {
                SpellRarity rarity = SpellRarity.valueOf(tag.getString("spell_rarity").toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT));
                Integer color = rarity.getChatFormatting().getColor();
                return color != null ? (color | 0xFF000000) : null;
            } catch (Exception ignored) {}
        }

        // B. 检查是否为原版已鉴定卷轴
        if (ISpellContainer.isSpellContainer(stack)) {
            ISpellContainer container = ISpellContainer.get(stack);
            if (container != null && !container.isEmpty()) {
                SpellData spellData = container.getSpellAtIndex(0);
                if (spellData != null && spellData != SpellData.EMPTY) {
                    SpellRarity rarity = spellData.getRarity();
                    Integer color = rarity.getChatFormatting().getColor();
                    return color != null ? (color | 0xFF000000) : null;
                }
            }
        }

        return null;
    }

    /**
     * 绘制带 1 像素暗色边框的指示点（保证在浅色物品背景下依然清晰可见）
     */
    private static void renderPip(GuiGraphics guiGraphics, int x, int y, int argbColor) {
        int size = 3;
        // 1. 先铺一层 1 像素微阴影衬底（黑色半透明），防贴图反色吃字
        guiGraphics.fill(x - 1, y - 1, x + size + 1, y + size + 1, OVERLAY_Z - 1, 0x60000000);
        // 2. 绘制 3x3 彩色指示点
        guiGraphics.fill(x, y, x + size, y + size, OVERLAY_Z, argbColor);
    }
}

// ==================================================
// File: client\ItemCopyHandler.java
// ==================================================

package com.example.examplemod.client;

import com.example.examplemod.ExampleMod;
import net.minecraft.client.Minecraft;
import net.minecraft.client.gui.screens.Screen;
import net.minecraft.client.gui.screens.inventory.AbstractContainerScreen;
import net.minecraft.core.registries.BuiltInRegistries;
import net.minecraft.network.chat.Component;
import net.minecraft.resources.ResourceLocation;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.player.Player;
import net.minecraft.world.inventory.Slot;
import net.minecraft.world.item.ItemStack;
import net.neoforged.api.distmarker.Dist;
import net.neoforged.bus.api.SubscribeEvent;
import net.neoforged.fml.common.EventBusSubscriber;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.client.event.InputEvent;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.client.event.ScreenEvent;
import org.lwjgl.glfw.GLFW;

@EventBusSubscriber(modid = ExampleMod.MODID, value = Dist.CLIENT)
public class ItemCopyHandler {

    // 场景 1：在任意 GUI 界面中（背包、箱子等），鼠标悬停物品时按 Ctrl + C
    @SubscribeEvent
    public static void onScreenKeyPressed(ScreenEvent.KeyPressed.Pre event) {
        // 判断按键是否是 C，且按住了 Ctrl (Mac 下自动适配 Command)
        if (event.getKeyCode() == GLFW.GLFW_KEY_C && Screen.hasControlDown()) {
            if (event.getScreen() instanceof AbstractContainerScreen<?> containerScreen) {
                // 获取当前鼠标悬停的槽位
                Slot slot = containerScreen.getSlotUnderMouse();
                if (slot != null && slot.hasItem()) {
                    copyItemId(slot.getItem());
                    event.setCanceled(true); // 拦截事件，防止与原版操作冲突
                }
            }
        }
    }

    // 场景 2：游戏中不打开任何界面，手持物品时按 Ctrl + C
    @SubscribeEvent
    public static void onGameKeyPressed(InputEvent.Key event) {
        if (event.getAction() == GLFW.GLFW_PRESS && event.getKey() == GLFW.GLFW_KEY_C && Screen.hasControlDown()) {
            Minecraft mc = Minecraft.getInstance();
            // 确保没有打开任何界面，且玩家存在
            if (mc.screen == null && mc.player != null) {
                ItemStack mainHand = mc.player.getMainHandItem();
                if (!mainHand.isEmpty()) {
                    copyItemId(mainHand);
                }
            }
        }
    }

    // 统一复制与提示工具方法
    private static void copyItemId(ItemStack stack) {
        ResourceLocation id = BuiltInRegistries.ITEM.getKey(stack.getItem());
        String idStr = id.toString();

        // 1. 复制到系统剪贴板
        Minecraft.getInstance().keyboardHandler.setClipboard(idStr);

        // 2. 在快捷栏上方展示提示消息（不污染聊天框）
        Player player = Minecraft.getInstance().player;
        if (player != null) {
            player.displayClientMessage(
                    Component.literal("§a[已复制 ID] §f" + idStr),
                    true // true 表示在 Action Bar 显示
            );
        }
    }
}

// ==================================================
// File: compat\curios\ModLootCategories.java
// ==================================================

package com.example.examplemod.compat.curios;

import dev.shadowsoffire.apotheosis.Apoth;
import dev.shadowsoffire.apotheosis.loot.LootCategory;
import dev.shadowsoffire.apothic_attributes.api.ALObjects.BuiltInRegs;
import dev.shadowsoffire.apothic_attributes.compat.CurioEquipmentSlot;
import dev.shadowsoffire.apothic_attributes.modifiers.EntityEquipmentSlot;
import dev.shadowsoffire.apothic_attributes.modifiers.EntitySlotGroup;
import net.minecraft.core.Holder;
import net.minecraft.core.HolderSet;
import net.minecraft.resources.ResourceLocation;
import net.minecraft.tags.ItemTags;
import net.minecraft.world.item.ItemStack;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.registries.RegisterEvent;
import top.theillusivec4.curios.api.CuriosApi;

import java.util.HashMap;
import java.util.Map;

public class ModLootCategories {

    public static final String MODID = "examplemod";

    public static final Map<String, EntitySlotGroup> REGISTERED_GROUPS = new HashMap<>();

    public static final String[] SLOTS = {
            "ring", "necklace", "belt", "charm", "head", "body", "bracelet", "back", "hands", "feet", "curio",
            "artifact_belt", "artifact_body", "artifact_bracelet", "artifact_head", "artifact_necklace"
    };

    public static void onRegister(RegisterEvent event) {

        // 1. 注册每个 Curios 槽位对应的 EntityEquipmentSlot
        if (event.getRegistryKey().equals(BuiltInRegs.ENTITY_EQUIPMENT_SLOT.key())) {
            event.register(BuiltInRegs.ENTITY_EQUIPMENT_SLOT.key(), helper -> {
                for (String slotType : SLOTS) {
                    helper.register(
                            ResourceLocation.fromNamespaceAndPath(MODID, slotType),
                            new CurioEquipmentSlot(slotType)
                    );
                }
            });
        }

        // 2. 注册对应的 EntitySlotGroup（持有刚才的 slotHolder！）
        if (event.getRegistryKey().equals(BuiltInRegs.ENTITY_SLOT_GROUP.key())) {
            event.register(BuiltInRegs.ENTITY_SLOT_GROUP.key(), helper -> {
                for (String slotType : SLOTS) {
                    ResourceLocation id = ResourceLocation.fromNamespaceAndPath(MODID, slotType);

                    Holder<EntityEquipmentSlot> slotHolder = BuiltInRegs.ENTITY_EQUIPMENT_SLOT.getHolder(id).orElse(null);
                    if (slotHolder != null) {
                        EntitySlotGroup group = new EntitySlotGroup(id, HolderSet.direct(slotHolder));
                        helper.register(id, group);
                        REGISTERED_GROUPS.put(slotType, group);
                    }
                }
            });
        }

        // 3. 注册自定义 LootCategory
        if (event.getRegistryKey().equals(Apoth.BuiltInRegs.LOOT_CATEGORY.key())) {
            event.register(Apoth.BuiltInRegs.LOOT_CATEGORY.key(), helper -> {
                for (String slotType : SLOTS) {
                    EntitySlotGroup group = REGISTERED_GROUPS.get(slotType);
                    if (group != null) {
                        helper.register(
                                ResourceLocation.fromNamespaceAndPath(MODID, slotType),
                                new LootCategory(
                                        stack -> stack.is(ItemTags.create(ResourceLocation.parse("curios:" + slotType))),
                                        group,
                                        500
                                )
                        );
                    }
                }
            });
        }
    }

    /**
     * 根据物品实际支持的 Curios 槽位，获取对应的 EntitySlotGroup
     */
    public static EntitySlotGroup getGroupForStack(ItemStack stack) {
        var slots = CuriosApi.getItemStackSlots(stack, true);
        if (!slots.isEmpty()) {
            String firstSlot = slots.keySet().iterator().next();
            if (REGISTERED_GROUPS.containsKey(firstSlot)) {
                return REGISTERED_GROUPS.get(firstSlot);
            }
        }
        // 兜底返回通用 curio 组，或者默认项链组
        return REGISTERED_GROUPS.getOrDefault("curio", REGISTERED_GROUPS.get("necklace"));
    }
}

// ==================================================
// File: compat\curios\SocketsRuleHandler.java
// ==================================================

package com.example.examplemod.compat.curios;

import com.example.examplemod.util.CurioMimicHelper;
import dev.shadowsoffire.apotheosis.event.GetItemSocketsEvent;
import dev.xkmc.l2artifacts.content.core.RankedItem; // 莱特兰遗物基类（若已有依赖）
import net.minecraft.world.item.ItemStack;
import net.neoforged.bus.api.SubscribeEvent;
import net.neoforged.fml.common.EventBusSubscriber;

@EventBusSubscriber(modid = "examplemod")
public class SocketsRuleHandler {

    @SubscribeEvent
    public static void onGetItemSockets(GetItemSocketsEvent event) {
        ItemStack stack = event.getStack();
        if (stack.isEmpty()) return;

        // 1. 【L2 遗物】：按 Rank 限制最大孔数
        if (stack.getItem() instanceof RankedItem artifact) {
            int rank = artifact.rank; // 或从组件读取 rank
            int max = (rank == 5) ? 3 : (rank == 4) ? 2 : (rank >= 2) ? 1 : 0;
            if (event.getSockets() > max) {
                event.setSockets(max);
            }
            return;
        }

        // 2. 【普通 Curios 饰品】：最多 1 孔
        if (CurioMimicHelper.isCurio(stack) && event.getSockets() > 1) {
            event.setSockets(1);
        }
    }
}

// ==================================================
// File: compat\jei\CuriosCompat.java
// ==================================================

package com.example.examplemod.compat.jei;

import net.minecraft.client.Minecraft;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.player.Player;
import net.minecraft.world.item.ItemStack;
import net.minecraft.world.level.Level;
import net.neoforged.api.distmarker.Dist;
import net.neoforged.api.distmarker.OnlyIn;
import net.neoforged.fml.ModList;
import top.theillusivec4.curios.api.CuriosApi;
import top.theillusivec4.curios.api.type.ISlotType;
import top.theillusivec4.curios.api.type.capability.ICuriosItemHandler;
import top.theillusivec4.curios.api.type.inventory.IDynamicStackHandler;

import java.util.Map;

// 1. 显式声明仅客户端使用，防止服务端意外加载崩溃
@OnlyIn(Dist.CLIENT)
public final class CuriosCompat {
    private static final boolean LOADED = ModList.get().isLoaded("curios");

    private CuriosCompat() {}

    public static boolean isCurio(ItemStack stack) {
        if (!LOADED || stack.isEmpty()) return false;
        return CuriosImpl.isCurio(stack);
    }

    public static boolean tryEquipCurio(Player player, ItemStack stack) {
        if (!LOADED || stack.isEmpty() || player == null) return false;
        return CuriosImpl.tryEquip(player, stack);
    }

    private static class CuriosImpl {
        private static boolean isCurio(ItemStack stack) {
            Level level = Minecraft.getInstance().level;
            if (level == null) return false;

            Map<String, ISlotType> slotTypes = CuriosApi.getItemStackSlots(stack, level);
            return slotTypes != null && !slotTypes.isEmpty();
        }

        private static boolean tryEquip(Player player, ItemStack stack) {
            // 2. 核心修正：优先使用传入实体自带的世界对象
            Level level = player.level();

            Map<String, ISlotType> slotTypes = CuriosApi.getItemStackSlots(stack, level);
            if (slotTypes == null || slotTypes.isEmpty()) return false;

            var curiosOpt = CuriosApi.getCuriosInventory(player);
            if (curiosOpt.isEmpty()) return false;

            ICuriosItemHandler handler = curiosOpt.get();

            // 3. 彻底清空假人残留：同时清空装备栏与时装外观栏
            handler.getCurios().forEach((slotId, stacksHandler) -> {
                // 清空装备栏
                IDynamicStackHandler stacks = stacksHandler.getStacks();
                for (int i = 0; i < stacks.getSlots(); i++) {
                    stacks.setStackInSlot(i, ItemStack.EMPTY);
                }
                // 清空时装栏（防止残留外观遮挡新饰品）
                IDynamicStackHandler cosmeticStacks = stacksHandler.getCosmeticStacks();
                for (int i = 0; i < cosmeticStacks.getSlots(); i++) {
                    cosmeticStacks.setStackInSlot(i, ItemStack.EMPTY);
                }
            });

            // 4. 将饰品放入第一个可用槽位
            for (String slotId : slotTypes.keySet()) {
                var stacksHandler = handler.getCurios().get(slotId);
                if (stacksHandler != null && stacksHandler.getStacks().getSlots() > 0) {
                    stacksHandler.getStacks().setStackInSlot(0, stack.copy());
                    return true;
                }
            }

            return false;
        }
    }
}

// ==================================================
// File: compat\l2hostility\traits\AutoSpellTrait.java
// ==================================================

package com.example.examplemod.compat.l2hostility.traits;

import dev.xkmc.l2hostility.content.traits.base.MobTrait;
import io.redspace.ironsspellbooks.api.magic.MagicData;
import io.redspace.ironsspellbooks.api.spells.AbstractSpell;
import io.redspace.ironsspellbooks.api.spells.CastSource;
import io.redspace.ironsspellbooks.api.spells.CastType;
import io.redspace.ironsspellbooks.api.util.Utils;
import io.redspace.ironsspellbooks.capabilities.magic.TargetEntityCastData;
import io.redspace.ironsspellbooks.capabilities.magic.TelekinesisData;
import io.redspace.ironsspellbooks.spells.eldritch.TelekinesisSpell;
import io.redspace.ironsspellbooks.spells.ender.BlackHoleSpell;
import io.redspace.ironsspellbooks.spells.ender.StarfallSpell;
import net.minecraft.ChatFormatting;
import net.minecraft.core.RegistryAccess;
import net.minecraft.core.particles.DustParticleOptions;
import net.minecraft.core.particles.ParticleTypes;
import net.minecraft.network.chat.Component;
import net.minecraft.network.chat.MutableComponent;
import net.minecraft.server.level.ServerLevel;
import net.minecraft.world.effect.MobEffectInstance;
import net.minecraft.world.effect.MobEffects;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.LivingEntity;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.Mob;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.Vec3;
import org.joml.Vector3f;

import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.WeakHashMap;
import java.util.function.Supplier;

public class AutoSpellTrait extends MobTrait {

    private static final Map<Mob, CastSession> SESSIONS = new WeakHashMap<>();

    private final Supplier<? extends AbstractSpell> spellSupplier;
    private final int cooldownTicks;
    private final Map<Mob, Integer> lastCastPerMob = new WeakHashMap<>();

    public AutoSpellTrait(Supplier<? extends AbstractSpell> spellSupplier) {
        super(() -> SpellTraitHelper.getDynamicColor(spellSupplier.get()));
        this.spellSupplier = spellSupplier;
        AbstractSpell spell = spellSupplier.get();
        this.cooldownTicks = spell != null ? SpellTraitHelper.getSafeCooldown(spell) : 60;
    }

    @Override
    public void tick(LivingEntity entity, int traitLevel) {
        if (entity.level().isClientSide()) return;
        if (!(entity instanceof Mob mob)) return;

        AbstractSpell spell = spellSupplier.get();
        if (spell == null || !spell.isEnabled()) return;

        CastSession session = SESSIONS.get(mob);
        if (session != null) {
            if (session.owner == this) tickSession(mob, session);
            return;
        }

        Integer last = lastCastPerMob.get(mob);
        if (last != null) {
            int ownNext = last + Math.max(40, this.cooldownTicks);
            if (mob.tickCount < ownNext) return;
        }

        LivingEntity target = mob.getTarget();
        if (target == null || !target.isAlive()) return;

        int maxTraitLv = this.getMaxLevel(mob.level().registryAccess());
        int spellLevel = SpellTraitHelper.scaleSpellLevel(traitLevel, maxTraitLv, spell);
        CastType castType = spell.getCastType();
        int windupTicks = (castType == CastType.INSTANT) ? 4 : 20;
        int channelTicks = (castType == CastType.CONTINUOUS)
                ? Math.max(40, spell.getCastTime(spellLevel)) : 0;
        int total = windupTicks + channelTicks + 10;

        CastSession newSession = new CastSession(
                this, spell, mob.tickCount, total,
                castType == CastType.CONTINUOUS,
                target, spellLevel, windupTicks, channelTicks);

        SESSIONS.put(mob, newSession);
        lastCastPerMob.put(mob, mob.tickCount);
        mob.getLookControl().setLookAt(target, 45.0F, 45.0F);
    }

    private void tickSession(Mob mob, CastSession s) {
        int elapsed = mob.tickCount - s.startTick;
        MagicData magicData = MagicData.getPlayerMagicData(mob);
        if (magicData == null) {
            SESSIONS.remove(mob);
            return;
        }
        if (elapsed < s.windupTicks) {
            mob.addEffect(new MobEffectInstance(
                    MobEffects.MOVEMENT_SLOWDOWN, 5, 2, false, false, false));
            if (elapsed % 4 == 0) {
                spawnCastingParticles((ServerLevel) mob.level(), mob, s.spell);
            }
            if (elapsed == s.windupTicks - 1 && s.target.isAlive()) {
                mob.getLookControl().setLookAt(s.target, 45.0F, 45.0F);
                if (s.spell instanceof BlackHoleSpell) {
                    aimAtGround(mob, s.target);
                }
            }
            return;
        }

        if (elapsed == s.windupTicks) {
            if (!s.target.isAlive()) {
                finishSession(mob, s, magicData, true);
                return;
            }

            injectCastData(mob, s.target, s.spell, magicData);

            // 【关键修复】如果是持续施法，通知 magicData 开始引导施法
            if (s.continuous) {
                magicData.initiateCast(s.spell, s.spellLevel, s.channelTicks, CastSource.MOB, "trait");
            }

            s.spell.onCast(mob.level(), s.spellLevel, mob, CastSource.MOB, magicData);

            if (!s.continuous) {
                try {
                    s.spell.onServerCastComplete(mob.level(), s.spellLevel, mob, magicData, false);
                } catch (Throwable ignored) {}
                SESSIONS.remove(mob);
            }
            return;
        }

        if (s.continuous && elapsed < s.windupTicks + s.channelTicks) {
            if (!s.target.isAlive()) {
                finishSession(mob, s, magicData, true);
                return;
            }

            // 关键：每 tick 递减施法剩余时间，并调用法术 tick
            magicData.handleCastDuration();
            s.spell.onServerCastTick(mob.level(), s.spellLevel, mob, magicData);
            return;
        }

        if (s.continuous && elapsed >= s.windupTicks + s.channelTicks) {
            try {
                s.spell.onServerCastComplete(mob.level(), s.spellLevel, mob, magicData, false);
            } catch (Throwable ignored) {}

            // 关键：结束施法状态
            magicData.resetCastingState();
            SESSIONS.remove(mob);
            return;
        }

        if (elapsed >= s.duration) {
            finishSession(mob, s, magicData, true);
        }
    }

    private void finishSession(Mob mob, CastSession s, MagicData magicData, boolean cancelled) {
        try {
            s.spell.onServerCastComplete(mob.level(), s.spellLevel, mob, magicData, cancelled);
        } catch (Throwable ignored) {}

        magicData.resetCastingState(); //
        SESSIONS.remove(mob);
    }

    private void injectCastData(Mob mob, LivingEntity target, AbstractSpell spell, MagicData magicData) {
        if (magicData.getAdditionalCastData() != null) {
            magicData.resetAdditionalCastData();
        }

        if (spell instanceof StarfallSpell) {
            Vec3 ground = Utils.moveToRelativeGroundLevel(mob.level(), target.position(), 12);
            magicData.setAdditionalCastData(new StarfallSpell.StarfallCastData(ground));
            return;
        }
        if (spell instanceof TelekinesisSpell) {
            magicData.setAdditionalCastData(
                    new TelekinesisData(mob.distanceTo(target), target, 6));
            return;
        }
        if (spell instanceof BlackHoleSpell) {
            aimAtGround(mob, target);
            return;
        }
        magicData.setAdditionalCastData(new TargetEntityCastData(target));
    }

    private void aimAtGround(Mob mob, LivingEntity target) {
        double ex = mob.getX(), ey = mob.getEyeY(), ez = mob.getZ();
        double tx = target.getX(), ty = target.getY() + 0.1, tz = target.getZ();
        double dx = tx - ex, dy = ty - ey, dz = tz - ez;
        double horiz = Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dz * dz);

        float yaw   = (float)(Math.toDegrees(Math.atan2(dz, dx)) - 90.0);
        float pitch = (float)(-Math.toDegrees(Math.atan2(dy, horiz)));

        mob.setYRot(yaw);
        mob.setXRot(pitch);
        mob.yHeadRot = yaw;
        mob.yBodyRot = yaw;
    }

    private void spawnCastingParticles(ServerLevel level, Mob mob, AbstractSpell spell) {
        Vector3f color = spell.getTargetingColor();
        DustParticleOptions dust = new DustParticleOptions(color, 1.2F);

        double x = mob.getX();
        double y = mob.getY() + (mob.getBbHeight() * 0.5);
        double z = mob.getZ();

        level.sendParticles(dust, x, y, z, 3, 0.3, 0.3, 0.3, 0.02);
        level.sendParticles(ParticleTypes.ENCHANT, x, y + 0.5, z, 2, 0.2, 0.2, 0.2, 0.5);
    }

    // ============================================================
    // 词条名 = 法术名。SpellTraitSymbol 靠这个拿名字，lang 用 Iron's 自带
    // ============================================================
    @Override
    public String getDescriptionId() {
        AbstractSpell spell = spellSupplier.get();
        return spell != null ? spell.getComponentId() : super.getDescriptionId();
    }

    // ============================================================
    // 详情：显示 cost / weight / min_level / max_rank，全部从 DataMap 读
    // ============================================================
    @Override
    public void addDetail(RegistryAccess access, List<Component> list) {
        AbstractSpell spell = spellSupplier.get();
        if (spell == null) { super.addDetail(access, list); return; }

        try {
            for (MutableComponent line : spell.getUniqueInfo(1, null)) {
                list.add(Component.literal(" • ").append(line).withStyle(ChatFormatting.GRAY));
            }
        } catch (Throwable ignored) {}
    }

    private static final class CastSession {
        final AutoSpellTrait owner;
        final AbstractSpell spell;
        final int startTick;
        final int duration;
        final boolean continuous;
        final LivingEntity target;
        final int spellLevel;
        final int windupTicks;
        final int channelTicks;

        CastSession(AutoSpellTrait owner, AbstractSpell spell, int startTick, int duration,
                    boolean continuous, LivingEntity target, int spellLevel,
                    int windupTicks, int channelTicks) {
            this.owner = owner;
            this.spell = spell;
            this.startTick = startTick;
            this.duration = duration;
            this.continuous = continuous;
            this.target = target;
            this.spellLevel = spellLevel;
            this.windupTicks = windupTicks;
            this.channelTicks = channelTicks;
        }
    }
}

// ==================================================
// File: compat\l2hostility\traits\SpellTraitGenCommand.java
// ==================================================

package com.example.examplemod.compat.l2hostility.traits;

import com.google.gson.Gson;
import com.google.gson.GsonBuilder;
import com.google.gson.JsonObject;
import com.mojang.brigadier.CommandDispatcher;
import com.mojang.brigadier.arguments.StringArgumentType;
import io.redspace.ironsspellbooks.api.registry.SpellRegistry;
import io.redspace.ironsspellbooks.api.spells.AbstractSpell;
import io.redspace.ironsspellbooks.api.spells.CastType;
import net.minecraft.commands.CommandSourceStack;
import net.minecraft.commands.Commands;
import net.minecraft.network.chat.Component;
import net.minecraft.resources.ResourceLocation;
import net.neoforged.bus.api.SubscribeEvent;
import net.neoforged.fml.common.EventBusSubscriber;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.event.RegisterCommandsEvent;

import java.io.IOException;
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.nio.file.Files;
import java.nio.file.Path;
import java.nio.file.Paths;

@EventBusSubscriber(modid = "examplemod")
public class SpellTraitGenCommand {

    /** 开发环境默认路径：项目根/src/main/resources/data/examplemod/data_maps/l2hostility/trait */
    private static final String DEFAULT_OUT =
            "../src/main/resources/data/l2hostility/data_maps/l2hostility/trait";

    private static final Gson GSON = new GsonBuilder().setPrettyPrinting().create();

    @SubscribeEvent
    public static void onRegisterCommands(RegisterCommandsEvent event) {
        CommandDispatcher<CommandSourceStack> d = event.getDispatcher();
        d.register(Commands.literal("spelltrait")
                .requires(s -> s.hasPermission(2))
                .then(Commands.literal("gen")
                        .executes(ctx -> gen(ctx.getSource(), DEFAULT_OUT))
                        .then(Commands.argument("outDir", StringArgumentType.greedyString())
                                .executes(ctx -> gen(ctx.getSource(),
                                        StringArgumentType.getString(ctx, "outDir"))))));
    }

    private static int gen(CommandSourceStack src, String outDirStr) {
        Path outDir = Paths.get(outDirStr).toAbsolutePath();
        try {
            Files.createDirectories(outDir);
        } catch (IOException e) {
            src.sendFailure(Component.literal("创建目录失败: " + e));
            return 0;
        }

        JsonObject values = new JsonObject();
        int ok = 0, fail = 0;

        for (String idStr : SpellTraitRegistrar.WHITELIST) {
            ResourceLocation spellId = ResourceLocation.tryParse(idStr);
            if (spellId == null) { fail++; continue; }

            AbstractSpell spell = SpellRegistry.REGISTRY.get(spellId);
            if (spell == null || !spell.isEnabled()) { fail++; continue; }

            ResourceLocation traitId = SpellTraitRegistrar.traitIdOf(idStr);
            if (traitId == null) { fail++; continue; }

            try {
                values.add(traitId.toString(), buildConfig(spell));
                ok++;
            } catch (Throwable t) {
                fail++;
            }
        }

        JsonObject root = new JsonObject();
        root.addProperty("replace", false);
        root.add("values", values);

        Path file = outDir.resolve("trait_data.json");
        try {
            Files.writeString(file, GSON.toJson(root), StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
        } catch (IOException e) {
            src.sendFailure(Component.literal("写文件失败: " + e));
            return 0;
        }

        int fOk = ok, fFail = fail;
        src.sendSuccess(() -> Component.literal(
                "生成完成: 成功 " + fOk + "，失败 " + fFail + "\n文件: " + file), true);
        return ok;
    }

    private static JsonObject buildConfig(AbstractSpell spell) {
        int rarity = spell.getRarity(1).getValue();

        // —— 数值算法：想改平衡就改这里 ——
        int minLevel = switch (rarity) {
            case 0 -> 20;
            case 1 -> 50;
            case 2 -> 100;
            case 3 -> 200;
            default -> 400;
        };
        int weight  = Math.max(10, 100 - rarity * 15);
        int maxRank = Math.max(1, spell.getMaxLevel());

        int rawMana = spell.getManaCost(1);
        boolean continuous = spell.getCastType() == CastType.CONTINUOUS;
        double effectiveMana = continuous ? rawMana * 20.0 : Math.max(10, rawMana);
        int cost = (int) Math.round(
                Math.sqrt(effectiveMana)
                        * (1.0 + rarity * 0.4)
                        * (1.0 + Math.sqrt(maxRank) * 0.4));
        if (cost < 1) cost = 1;

        JsonObject o = new JsonObject();
        o.addProperty("cost", cost);
        o.addProperty("weight", weight);
        o.addProperty("max_rank", maxRank);
        o.addProperty("min_level", minLevel);
        return o;
    }
}

// ==================================================
// File: compat\l2hostility\traits\SpellTraitHelper.java
// ==================================================

package com.example.examplemod.compat.l2hostility.traits;

import io.redspace.ironsspellbooks.api.spells.AbstractSpell;
import io.redspace.ironsspellbooks.api.spells.SchoolType;
import org.joml.Vector3f;

public class SpellTraitHelper {

    /**
     * 1. 动态颜色计算：
     * 从 SchoolType 的 targetingColor 向量转换提取 0xRRGGBB。
     * 完美兼容未来所有附属模组自定义的新学派。
     */
    public static int getDynamicColor(AbstractSpell spell) {
        if (spell == null) return 0xFFFFFF;
        SchoolType school = spell.getSchoolType();
        if (school == null) return 0xFFFFFF;

        Vector3f colorVec = school.getTargetingColor();
        if (colorVec == null) return 0xFFFFFF;

        int r = Math.clamp((int) (colorVec.x() * 255.0F), 0, 255);
        int g = Math.clamp((int) (colorVec.y() * 255.0F), 0, 255);
        int b = Math.clamp((int) (colorVec.z() * 255.0F), 0, 255);

        return (r << 16) | (g << 8) | b;
    }

    /**
     * 2. 等级自适应算法：
     * 保证词条等级（不管是 1~5 还是 1~10）与法术自身的 [minLevel, maxLevel] 线性对齐。
     */
    public static int scaleSpellLevel(int traitLevel, int maxTraitLevel, AbstractSpell spell) {
        int minSpellLv = spell.getMinLevel();
        int maxSpellLv = spell.getMaxLevel();
        if (minSpellLv >= maxSpellLv) {
            return minSpellLv;
        }

        int maxT = Math.max(1, maxTraitLevel);
        float progress = (float) (traitLevel - 1) / (float) Math.max(1, maxT - 1);
        int result = Math.round(minSpellLv + progress * (maxSpellLv - minSpellLv));
        return Math.clamp(result, minSpellLv, maxSpellLv);
    }

    /**
     * 3. 施法周期计算：
     * 基于法术自身的 Cooldown 动态配置，并约束在合理防卡顿区间 [40 ticks, 200 ticks]。
     */
    public static int getSafeCooldown(AbstractSpell spell) {
        int cd = spell.getSpellCooldown();
        return cd <= 0 ? 60 : Math.clamp(cd, 40, 200);
    }
}

// File: compat\l2hostility\traits\SpellTraitItemRegistrar.java
// ==================================================

package com.example.examplemod.compat.l2hostility.traits;

import com.example.examplemod.ExampleMod;
import dev.xkmc.l2hostility.init.registrate.LHBlocks;
import net.minecraft.core.registries.Registries;
import net.minecraft.resources.ResourceLocation;
import net.minecraft.world.item.Item;
import net.minecraft.world.item.ItemStack;
import net.neoforged.bus.api.SubscribeEvent;
import net.neoforged.fml.common.EventBusSubscriber;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.event.BuildCreativeModeTabContentsEvent;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.registries.RegisterEvent;

import java.io.BufferedWriter;
import java.io.IOException;
import java.nio.file.Files;
import java.nio.file.Path;
import java.nio.file.Paths;
import java.time.LocalDateTime;
import java.time.format.DateTimeFormatter;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.LinkedHashMap;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;

@EventBusSubscriber(modid = ExampleMod.MODID)
public class SpellTraitItemRegistrar {

    private static final Map<ResourceLocation, Item> REGISTERED = new LinkedHashMap<>();

    // ============================================================
    // 调试文件路径：<项目根>/run/spelltrait_debug.txt
    // ============================================================
    private static final Path DEBUG_FILE =
            Paths.get("spelltrait_debug.txt").toAbsolutePath();

    private static final List<String> DEBUG_LINES = new ArrayList<>();

    private static void dbg(String line) {
        DEBUG_LINES.add(line);
        System.out.println("[SpellTraitItem] " + line); // 顺便打一份到 stdout
    }

    private static void flushDebug() {
        try (BufferedWriter w = Files.newBufferedWriter(DEBUG_FILE)) {
            w.write("=== SpellTraitItemRegistrar DEBUG ===\n");
            w.write("时间: " + LocalDateTime.now()
                    .format(DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern("yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss")) + "\n");
            w.write("文件: " + DEBUG_FILE + "\n\n");
            for (String l : DEBUG_LINES) {
                w.write(l);
                w.write("\n");
            }
        } catch (IOException e) {
            System.err.println("[SpellTraitItem] 写调试文件失败: " + e);
        }
    }

    // ============================================================
    // 1) item 注册
    // ============================================================
    @SubscribeEvent
    public static void onRegisterItems(RegisterEvent event) {
        if (!event.getRegistryKey().equals(Registries.ITEM)) return;

        dbg("=== onRegisterItems 被调用 ===");
        dbg("WHITELIST 大小 = " + SpellTraitRegistrar.WHITELIST.size());

        int ok = 0, fail = 0, nullId = 0;
        List<String> okIds = new ArrayList<>();
        List<String> failIds = new ArrayList<>();

        for (String idStr : SpellTraitRegistrar.WHITELIST) {
            ResourceLocation itemId = SpellTraitRegistrar.traitIdOf(idStr);
            if (itemId == null) {
                dbg("  [NULL] traitIdOf 返回 null: " + idStr);
                nullId++;
                continue;
            }

            // 检查是否已经注册过（重复 id 检测）
            if (REGISTERED.containsKey(itemId)) {
                dbg("  [DUP] itemId 已被注册过: " + itemId);
                fail++;
                failIds.add(itemId + "  <-- DUP (来自 " + idStr + ")");
                continue;
            }

            try {
                final String capturedId = idStr;
                event.register(Registries.ITEM, itemId, () -> {
                    SpellTraitSymbol symbol = new SpellTraitSymbol(new Item.Properties());
                    REGISTERED.put(itemId, symbol);
                    dbg("  [OK-SUPPLIER] supplier 执行: " + itemId + "  (来自 " + capturedId + ")");
                    return symbol;
                });
                dbg("  [OK-REG] 注册成功: " + itemId);
                ok++;
                okIds.add(String.valueOf(itemId));
            } catch (Throwable t) {
                dbg("  [FAIL] 注册异常: " + itemId + " -> " + t);
                fail++;
                failIds.add(itemId + "  <-- " + t);
            }
        }

        dbg("");
        dbg("=== 汇总 ===");
        dbg("WHITELIST 总数 = " + SpellTraitRegistrar.WHITELIST.size());
        dbg("成功 ok = " + ok);
        dbg("失败 fail = " + fail);
        dbg("id 为 null = " + nullId);
        dbg("REGISTERED map 大小 = " + REGISTERED.size());
        dbg("");
        dbg("--- 成功的 id ---");
        for (String s : okIds) dbg("  " + s);
        dbg("");
        dbg("--- 失败的 id ---");
        for (String s : failIds) dbg("  " + s);

        flushDebug();
    }

    // ============================================================
    // 2) 创造栏
    // ============================================================
    @SubscribeEvent
    public static void onBuildCreativeTab(BuildCreativeModeTabContentsEvent event) {
        if (!event.getTabKey().equals(LHBlocks.TAB.key())) return;

        dbg("");
        dbg("=== onBuildCreativeTab 被调用 ===");
        dbg("REGISTERED map 大小 = " + REGISTERED.size());

        int added = 0;
        for (Item item : REGISTERED.values()) {
            event.accept(new ItemStack(item));
            added++;
        }
        dbg("加入创造栏数量 = " + added);

        flushDebug();
    }
}

package com.example.examplemod.compat.l2hostility.traits;

import dev.xkmc.l2hostility.init.registrate.LHTraits;
import io.redspace.ironsspellbooks.api.registry.SpellRegistry;

import java.util.Set;

@EventBusSubscriber(modid = "examplemod")
public class SpellTraitRegistrar {
    private static final Logger LOGGER = LogManager.getLogger();

    /**
     * 白名单：所有适合给敌对怪物使用的法术。
     * 排除规则：
     *   1. 自增益/自治疗类（怪物会变得过强）
     *   2. 自位移类（AI 会跑偏、脱战）
     *   3. 需要玩家交互类（怪物无法触发）

     */

            "irons_spellbooks:blaze_storm",
            "irons_spellbooks:fire_arrow",
            "irons_spellbooks:fire_breath",
            "irons_spellbooks:fireball",

            // 🔮 神圣

            "irons_spellbooks:divine_smite",
            "irons_spellbooks:guiding_bolt",
            "irons_spellbooks:healing_circle",
            "irons_spellbooks:sunbeam",
            "irons_spellbooks:wisp",

            // ============================================================
            // 🔮 自然
            //
            // ============================================================
            "irons_spellbooks:acid_orb",
            "irons_spellbooks:blight",
            "irons_spellbooks:earthquake",
            "irons_spellbooks:firefly_swarm",
            "irons_spellbooks:poison_arrow",
            "irons_spellbooks:poison_breath",
            "irons_spellbooks:poison_splash",
            "irons_spellbooks:root",
            "irons_spellbooks:stomp",
            "irons_spellbooks:oakskin",
            "irons_spellbooks:spider_aspect",
            // ============================================================
            // 🔮 邪术（全部保留）
            // ============================================================
            "irons_spellbooks:eldritch_blast",
            "irons_spellbooks:sculk_tentacles",
            "irons_spellbooks:sonic_boom",
            "irons_spellbooks:telekinesis",

            // ============================================================
            // 🔮 雷霆
            //
            // ============================================================
            "irons_spellbooks:ball_lightning",
            "irons_spellbooks:chain_lightning",
            "irons_spellbooks:electrocute",
            "irons_spellbooks:lightning_bolt",
            "irons_spellbooks:lightning_lance",
            "irons_spellbooks:shockwave",
            "irons_spellbooks:charge",
            "irons_spellbooks:volt_strike",

            // ============================================================
            // 🔮 魂灵（iss_magicfromtheeast，全部保留）
            // ============================================================
            "iss_magicfromtheeast:anchoring_kunai",
            "iss_magicfromtheeast:bone_hands",
            "iss_magicfromtheeast:calamity_cut",
            "iss_magicfromtheeast:soul_burst",
            "iss_magicfromtheeast:soul_catalyst",
            "iss_magicfromtheeast:spirit_challenging",
            "iss_magicfromtheeast:splitting_shuriken"
    );
    public static ResourceLocation traitIdOf(String idStr) {
        ResourceLocation spellId = ResourceLocation.tryParse(idStr);
        if (spellId == null) return null;
        return ResourceLocation.fromNamespaceAndPath(
                "examplemod",
                spellId.getNamespace() + "_" + spellId.getPath() + "_spelltrait"
        );
    }
    @SubscribeEvent
    public static void onRegisterTraits(RegisterEvent event) {
        if (!event.getRegistryKey().equals(LHTraits.TRAITS.key())) {
            return;
        }

        LOGGER.info("[ExampleMod] 开始扫描铁魔法法术并注入 L2Hostility 词条（白名单模式）...");

        Registry<AbstractSpell> spellRegistry = SpellRegistry.REGISTRY;
        if (spellRegistry == null) {
            LOGGER.error("[ExampleMod] 铁魔法法术注册表为空或尚未初始化！");
            return;
        }

        int registered = 0;
        int missing = 0;

        for (String idStr : WHITELIST) {
            ResourceLocation spellId = ResourceLocation.tryParse(idStr);
            if (spellId == null) {
                LOGGER.warn("[ExampleMod] 白名单中存在非法 ID: {}", idStr);
                continue;
            }

            AbstractSpell spell = spellRegistry.get(spellId);
            if (spell == null) {
                LOGGER.warn("[ExampleMod] 白名单中的法术不存在（可能是版本差异或拼写错误）: {}", idStr);
                missing++;
                continue;
            }

            if (!spell.isEnabled()) {
                LOGGER.warn("[ExampleMod] 白名单中的法术已被铁魔法配置禁用，跳过: {}", idStr);
                continue;
            }
            event.register(
                    LHTraits.TRAITS.key(),
                    traitIdOf(idStr),
                    () -> new AutoSpellTrait(() -> spell)
            );
            registered++;
        }

        LOGGER.info("[ExampleMod] 白名单注册完成：成功 {} 个，缺失 {} 个。", registered, missing);
    }


}

// ==================================================
// File: compat\l2hostility\traits\SpellTraitSymbol.java
// ==================================================

package com.example.examplemod.compat.l2hostility.traits;

import dev.xkmc.l2hostility.content.item.traits.TraitSymbol;
import dev.xkmc.l2hostility.content.traits.base.MobTrait;
import net.minecraft.world.item.Item;

public class SpellTraitSymbol extends TraitSymbol {

    public SpellTraitSymbol(Item.Properties props) {
        super(props);
    }

    @Override
    protected String getOrCreateDescriptionId() {
        MobTrait trait = this.get();
        if (trait != null) {
            return trait.getDescriptionId();
        }
        return super.getOrCreateDescriptionId();
    }
}

package com.example.examplemod;

import net.neoforged.neoforge.common.ModConfigSpec;

public final class Config {
    private static final ModConfigSpec.Builder BUILDER = new ModConfigSpec.Builder();

    // 核心开关
    public static final ModConfigSpec.BooleanValue ENABLED;

    // 提示框内容模块开关 (已彻底剔除战斗看板与比对)
    public static final ModConfigSpec.BooleanValue SHOW_DURABILITY;
    public static final ModConfigSpec.BooleanValue SHOW_TOOL_SPEED;
    public static final ModConfigSpec.BooleanValue SHOW_FOOD;
    public static final ModConfigSpec.BooleanValue SHOW_EFFECTS;
    public static final ModConfigSpec.BooleanValue SHOW_FUEL;
    public static final ModConfigSpec.BooleanValue SHOW_COMPOST;
    public static final ModConfigSpec.BooleanValue SHOW_COOLDOWN;
    public static final ModConfigSpec.BooleanValue SHOW_CONTAINER_PREVIEW;
    public static final ModConfigSpec.BooleanValue SHOW_MODEL_STAGE;

    // 数据驱动规则
    public static final ModConfigSpec.BooleanValue SHOW_CUSTOM_RULES;

    // HUD 与功能
    public static final ModConfigSpec.BooleanValue SHOW_HELD_HUD;
    public static final ModConfigSpec.BooleanValue SHOW_SLOT_DEBUG;

    // 排版与样式
    public static final ModConfigSpec.BooleanValue RARITY_THEME;
    public static final ModConfigSpec.IntValue PINNED_PAGE_SIZE;
    public static final ModConfigSpec.IntValue MAX_LINES;
    public static final ModConfigSpec.IntValue MAX_TOOLTIP_WIDTH;
    public static final ModConfigSpec.LongValue BACKGROUND_COLOR;
    public static final ModConfigSpec.LongValue BORDER_COLOR;

    static {
        BUILDER.push("general");
        ENABLED = BUILDER.comment("启用 Tooltip 增强层").define("enabled", true);
        BUILDER.pop();

        BUILDER.push("features");
        SHOW_DURABILITY = BUILDER.comment("显示耐久度平滑色彩条").define("showDurability", true);
        SHOW_TOOL_SPEED = BUILDER.comment("显示工具采掘倍率").define("showToolSpeed", true);
        SHOW_FOOD = BUILDER.comment("显示饱食度与饱和度静态图标").define("showFood", true);
        SHOW_EFFECTS = BUILDER.comment("显示药水/迷之炖菜效果").define("showEffects", true);
        SHOW_FUEL = BUILDER.comment("显示熔炉燃料燃烧耗时与份数").define("showFuel", true);
        SHOW_COMPOST = BUILDER.comment("显示堆肥概率百分比").define("showCompost", true);
        SHOW_COOLDOWN = BUILDER.comment("显示物品冷却指示条").define("showCooldown", true);
        SHOW_CONTAINER_PREVIEW = BUILDER.comment("显示容器与收纳袋网格预览").define("showContainerPreview", true);
        SHOW_MODEL_STAGE = BUILDER.comment("在 Tooltip 侧边显示 3D 模型舞台 (可随时关闭)").define("showModelStage", true);
        BUILDER.pop();

        BUILDER.push("rules");
        SHOW_CUSTOM_RULES = BUILDER.comment("加载 JSON 自定义规则文本").define("showCustomRules", true);
        BUILDER.pop();

        BUILDER.push("hud");
        SHOW_HELD_HUD = BUILDER.comment("在快捷栏上方显示主手物品概览").define("showHeldHud", true);
        SHOW_SLOT_DEBUG = BUILDER.comment("显示槽位索引调试信息").define("showSlotDebug", false);
        BUILDER.pop();

        BUILDER.push("appearance");
        RARITY_THEME = BUILDER.comment("边框颜色跟随物品稀有度颜色").define("rarityTheme", true);
        PINNED_PAGE_SIZE = BUILDER.comment("固定 Tooltip 每页行数").defineInRange("pinnedPageSize", 12, 4, 64);
        MAX_LINES = BUILDER.comment("最大附加行数").defineInRange("maxLines", 32, 0, 128);
        MAX_TOOLTIP_WIDTH = BUILDER.comment("Tooltip 最大像素宽度").defineInRange("maxTooltipWidth", 320, 120, 640);
        BACKGROUND_COLOR = BUILDER.comment("背景 ARGB").defineInRange("backgroundColor", 0xF0100010L, 0L, 0xFFFFFFFFL);
        BORDER_COLOR = BUILDER.comment("边框 ARGB").defineInRange("borderColor", 0xFF5050A0L, 0L, 0xFFFFFFFFL);
        BUILDER.pop();
    }

    public static final ModConfigSpec SPEC = BUILDER.build();

    public static int getBackgroundColor() {
        return (int) BACKGROUND_COLOR.get().longValue();
    }

    public static int getBorderColor() {
        return (int) BORDER_COLOR.get().longValue();
    }

    private Config() {}
}

// ==================================================
// File: datagen\ModDataGen.java
// ==================================================

package com.example.examplemod.datagen;

import com.example.examplemod.ExampleMod;
import net.neoforged.bus.api.SubscribeEvent;
import net.neoforged.fml.common.EventBusSubscriber;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.data.event.GatherDataEvent;

@EventBusSubscriber(modid = ExampleMod.MODID)
public class ModDataGen {

    @SubscribeEvent
    public static void onGatherData(GatherDataEvent event) {
        var gen = event.getGenerator();
        var out = gen.getPackOutput();
        var helper = event.getExistingFileHelper();

        if (event.includeClient()) {
            gen.addProvider(true, new SpellTraitItemModels(out, helper));
        }
    }
}

// ==================================================
// File: datagen\SpellTraitItemModels.java
// ==================================================

package com.example.examplemod.datagen;

import com.example.examplemod.ExampleMod;
import com.example.examplemod.compat.l2hostility.traits.SpellTraitRegistrar;
import net.minecraft.data.PackOutput;
import net.minecraft.resources.ResourceLocation;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.client.model.generators.ItemModelProvider;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.client.model.generators.ModelFile;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.common.data.ExistingFileHelper;

public class SpellTraitItemModels extends ItemModelProvider {

    /** 从 LH jar 借用的背景层 */
    private static final ResourceLocation BG =
            ResourceLocation.fromNamespaceAndPath("l2hostility", "item/bg");

    public SpellTraitItemModels(PackOutput output, ExistingFileHelper helper) {
        super(output, ExampleMod.MODID, helper);
    }

    @Override
    protected void registerModels() {
        for (String idStr : SpellTraitRegistrar.WHITELIST) {
            ResourceLocation spellId = ResourceLocation.tryParse(idStr);
            if (spellId == null) continue;

            String itemPath = spellId.getNamespace() + "_" + spellId.getPath() + "_spelltrait";

            // 铁魔法 / 两仪统一走 gui/spell_icons/<path>
            ResourceLocation icon = ResourceLocation.fromNamespaceAndPath(
                    spellId.getNamespace(),
                    "gui/spell_icons/" + spellId.getPath()
            );

            getBuilder(itemPath)
                    .parent(new ModelFile.UncheckedModelFile("item/generated"))
                    .texture("layer0", BG)
                    .texture("layer1", icon);
        }
    }
}

// ==================================================
// File: event\AnimalKillRewardHandler.java
// ==================================================

package com.example.examplemod.event;

import com.example.examplemod.ExampleMod;
import net.minecraft.core.Holder;
import net.minecraft.world.effect.MobEffect;
import net.minecraft.world.effect.MobEffectInstance;
import net.minecraft.world.effect.MobEffects;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.LivingEntity;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.animal.Chicken;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.animal.Cow;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.animal.Pig;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.animal.Sheep;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.player.Player;
import net.neoforged.bus.api.SubscribeEvent;
import net.neoforged.fml.common.EventBusSubscriber;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.event.entity.living.LivingDeathEvent;

import java.util.List;
import java.util.Random;

// 使用 @EventBusSubscriber 自动注册到 NeoForge 游戏事件总线
@EventBusSubscriber(modid = ExampleMod.MODID)
public class AnimalKillRewardHandler {

    // 预定义的正面效果列表
    // 注意：在 1.21+ 中，MobEffects 字段返回的是 Holder<MobEffect>
    private static final List<Holder<MobEffect>> REWARD_EFFECTS = List.of(
            MobEffects.MOVEMENT_SPEED,    // 迅捷 (Speed)
            MobEffects.DAMAGE_BOOST,       // 力量 (Strength)
            MobEffects.DAMAGE_RESISTANCE,  // 抗性提升 (Resistance)
            MobEffects.ABSORPTION,         // 吸收 (Absorption)
            MobEffects.DIG_SPEED           // 急迫 (Haste)
    );

    private static final Random RANDOM = new Random();

    @SubscribeEvent
    public static void onLivingDeath(LivingDeathEvent event) {
        LivingEntity victim = event.getEntity();

        // 1. 仅在服务端处理逻辑，避免客户端产生幽灵效果或不同步
        if (victim.level().isClientSide()) {
            return;
        }

        // 2. 判断受害者类型
        // 方案 A：精确匹配指定的常见农场动物（猪、牛、羊、鸡）
        boolean isTargetAnimal = victim instanceof Pig 
                              || victim instanceof Cow 
                              || victim instanceof Sheep 
                              || victim instanceof Chicken;

        // 方案 B：如果你希望包含游戏中所有的动物（包括兔子、马等），可以直接用：
        // boolean isTargetAnimal = victim instanceof Animal;

        if (isTargetAnimal) {
            // 3. 检查直接/间接击杀者是否为玩家
            if (event.getSource().getEntity() instanceof Player player) {
                // 4. 从列表中随机选择一个 Effect
                Holder<MobEffect> chosenEffect = REWARD_EFFECTS.get(RANDOM.nextInt(REWARD_EFFECTS.size()));

                // 5. 设置持续时间和等级
                // 持续时间单位为游戏刻（20 ticks = 1秒），例如 20 秒为 20 * 20
                // amplifier: 0 代表 I 级，1 代表 II 级
                int duration = 20 * 20; 
                int amplifier = 0;

                // 给予玩家药水效果
                player.addEffect(new MobEffectInstance(chosenEffect, duration, amplifier));
            }
        }
    }
}

// ==================================================
// File: event\ApothicAttributeDifficultyHandler.java
// ==================================================

package com.example.examplemod.event;

import com.example.examplemod.registry.ModDataComponents;
import dev.shadowsoffire.apothic_attributes.modifiers.StackAttributeModifiers;
import dev.shadowsoffire.apothic_attributes.modifiers.StackAttributeModifiersEvent;
import net.minecraft.core.Holder;
import net.minecraft.core.registries.BuiltInRegistries;
import net.minecraft.resources.ResourceLocation;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.ai.attributes.Attribute;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.ai.attributes.AttributeModifier;
import net.minecraft.world.item.ItemStack;
import net.neoforged.bus.api.EventPriority;
import net.neoforged.bus.api.SubscribeEvent;
import net.neoforged.fml.common.EventBusSubscriber;

import java.util.List;
import java.util.UUID;

@EventBusSubscriber
public class ApothicAttributeDifficultyHandler {
    private static final ResourceLocation EXTRA_DIFF_RL =
            ResourceLocation.fromNamespaceAndPath("l2hostility", "extra_difficulty");


    private static UUID getOrCreateInstanceUuid(ItemStack stack) {
        UUID uuid = stack.get(ModDataComponents.ITEM_INSTANCE_UUID.get());
        if (uuid == null) {
            uuid = UUID.randomUUID();
            stack.set(
                    ModDataComponents.ITEM_INSTANCE_UUID.get(),
                    uuid
            );
        }
        return uuid;
    }

    @SubscribeEvent(priority = EventPriority.LOWEST)
    public static void onStackAttributeModifiers(StackAttributeModifiersEvent event) {
        ItemStack stack = event.getItemStack();
        Holder<Attribute> difficultyHolder = BuiltInRegistries.ATTRIBUTE
                .getHolder(EXTRA_DIFF_RL)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new IllegalStateException(
                        "Attribute not found: " + EXTRA_DIFF_RL
                ));
        List<StackAttributeModifiers.Entry> validModifiers = event.getModifiers()
                .stream()
                .filter(entry -> !difficultyHolder.equals(entry.attribute()))
                .toList();
        long validModifierCount = validModifiers.size();

        if (validModifierCount <= 0) {return;}
        UUID instanceUuid = getOrCreateInstanceUuid(stack);

        ResourceLocation modifierId =
                ResourceLocation.fromNamespaceAndPath(
                        "examplemod",
                        "extra_difficulty/" +
                                instanceUuid.toString().replace("-", "")
                );

        event.replaceModifier(
                difficultyHolder,
                new AttributeModifier(
                        modifierId,
                        validModifierCount,
                        AttributeModifier.Operation.ADD_VALUE
                ),
                validModifiers.getFirst().slots()
        );
    }
}

// ==================================================
// File: event\HostilityLuckHandler.java
// ==================================================

package com.example.examplemod.event;

import dev.xkmc.l2hostility.content.logic.DifficultyLevel;
import net.minecraft.resources.ResourceLocation;
import net.minecraft.server.level.ServerPlayer;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.ai.attributes.AttributeInstance;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.ai.attributes.AttributeModifier;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.ai.attributes.Attributes;
import net.neoforged.bus.api.SubscribeEvent;
import net.neoforged.fml.common.EventBusSubscriber;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.event.tick.PlayerTickEvent;

@EventBusSubscriber
public class HostilityLuckHandler {

    // 1.21.1 不再使用 UUID，使用 ResourceLocation 作为修饰符唯一 ID
    private static final ResourceLocation HOSTILITY_LUCK_ID =
            ResourceLocation.fromNamespaceAndPath("examplemod", "hostility_luck");

    @SubscribeEvent
    public static void onPlayerTick(PlayerTickEvent.Post event) {
        // 仅在服务端处理
        if (event.getEntity().level().isClientSide()) {
            return;
        }

        if (event.getEntity() instanceof ServerPlayer player) {
            // 每秒（20 ticks）节流检查一次
            if (player.tickCount % 20 != 0) {
                return;
            }

            // 1.21.1 Attributes.LUCK 是 Holder<Attribute>，直接传入即可
            AttributeInstance luckAttr = player.getAttribute(Attributes.LUCK);
            if (luckAttr == null) {
                return;
            }

            // 1. 计算目标幸运加成
            int hostilityLevel = DifficultyLevel.ofAny(player);
            double targetBonus = hostilityLevel * 0.1D; // 每 50 级恶意 +1.0 幸运

            // 2. 检查现存修饰符
            AttributeModifier current = luckAttr.getModifier(HOSTILITY_LUCK_ID);

            if (current != null) {
                // 1.21.1 AttributeModifier 变成了 record 风格，取值为 current.amount()
                if (Math.abs(current.amount() - targetBonus) < 1.0E-4) {
                    return; // 数值未变，直接返回
                }
                luckAttr.removeModifier(HOSTILITY_LUCK_ID);
            }

            // 3. 挂载新的基础加成
            if (targetBonus > 0) {
                luckAttr.addTransientModifier(new AttributeModifier(
                        HOSTILITY_LUCK_ID,
                        targetBonus,
                        AttributeModifier.Operation.ADD_VALUE // 1.21.1 中 ADDITION 变成了 ADD_VALUE
                ));
            }
        }
    }
}

// ==================================================
// File: event\MobEquipmentHandler.java
// ==================================================

package com.example.examplemod.event;

import com.example.examplemod.util.WeaponReplacer;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.EquipmentSlot;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.Mob;
import net.minecraft.world.item.ItemStack;
import net.neoforged.bus.api.SubscribeEvent;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.event.entity.EntityJoinLevelEvent;

public class MobEquipmentHandler {

    @SubscribeEvent
    public static void onEntityJoinLevel(EntityJoinLevelEvent event) {
        // 1. 仅在服务端处理，防止客户端与服务端数据不一致
        if (event.getLevel().isClientSide()) return;

        // 2. 仅对新生成的实体生效，排除从存档载入的实体（避免每次读档重复刷新武器）
        if (event.loadedFromDisk()) return;

        // 3. 拦截所有生物（包括原版僵尸/骷髅/猪灵，以及模组生成的怪物）
        if (event.getEntity() instanceof Mob mob) {
            
            EquipmentSlot[] handSlots = new EquipmentSlot[]{EquipmentSlot.MAINHAND, EquipmentSlot.OFFHAND};

            for (EquipmentSlot slot : handSlots) {
                ItemStack currentWeapon = mob.getItemBySlot(slot);
                ItemStack replaced = WeaponReplacer.tryReplace(currentWeapon, mob.getRandom());

                if (replaced != currentWeapon) {
                    mob.setItemSlot(slot, replaced);
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

// ==================================================
// File: event\PlayerDeathExperienceHandler.java
// ==================================================

package com.example.examplemod.event;

import net.minecraft.server.level.ServerPlayer;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.player.Player;
import net.neoforged.bus.api.SubscribeEvent;
import net.neoforged.fml.common.EventBusSubscriber;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.event.entity.living.LivingExperienceDropEvent;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.event.entity.player.PlayerEvent;

@EventBusSubscriber(modid = "examplemod")
public class PlayerDeathExperienceHandler {

    /**
     * 处理玩家重生时的经验继承：扣除 1 级并清空进度条
     */
    @SubscribeEvent
    public static void onPlayerClone(PlayerEvent.Clone event) {
        // 只处理死亡重生的情况（排除从末地传送门返回等非死亡 clone）
        if (!event.isWasDeath()) {
            return;
        }

        ServerPlayer oldPlayer = (ServerPlayer) event.getOriginal();
        ServerPlayer newPlayer = (ServerPlayer) event.getEntity();

        // 计算新等级（最低为 0）
        int newLevel = Math.max(0, oldPlayer.experienceLevel - 1);

        newPlayer.experienceProgress = 0.0F;
        newPlayer.experienceLevel = newLevel;
        
        // 建议：重新计算并校准 totalExperience（可选，但更规范）
        newPlayer.totalExperience = calculateTotalXpForLevel(newLevel);
    }

    /**
     * 阻止玩家死亡时在地上生成经验球，避免刷经验
     */
    @SubscribeEvent
    public static void onLivingExperienceDrop(LivingExperienceDropEvent event) {
        if (event.getEntity() instanceof Player) {
            event.setCanceled(true); // 取消经验球掉落
        }
    }

    /**
     * 辅助方法：计算对应等级的基础总经验值
     */
    private static int calculateTotalXpForLevel(int level) {
        if (level <= 16) {
            return level * level + 6 * level;
        } else if (level <= 31) {
            return (int) (2.5 * level * level - 40.5 * level + 360);
        } else {
            return (int) (4.5 * level * level - 162.5 * level + 2220);
        }
    }
}

// ==================================================
// File: event\SpawnerDrops.java
// ==================================================

package com.example.examplemod.event;

import dev.shadowsoffire.apotheosis.tiers.WorldTier;

import net.minecraft.world.entity.player.Player;
import net.minecraft.world.item.ItemStack;
import net.minecraft.world.item.Items;
import net.minecraft.world.level.block.Blocks;
import net.neoforged.bus.api.SubscribeEvent;
import net.neoforged.fml.common.EventBusSubscriber;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.event.level.BlockDropsEvent;

import static com.example.examplemod.util.Common.*;

@EventBusSubscriber
public final class SpawnerDrops {

    @SubscribeEvent
    public static void onBlockDrops(BlockDropsEvent event) {
        if (
                !(event.getBreaker() instanceof Player player) ||
                        !event.getState().is(Blocks.SPAWNER)) {
            return;
        }

        WorldTier tier = WorldTier.getTier(player);

        if (!roll(event.getLevel().random, tier)) {
            return;
        }
        addDrop(event, createGatewayPearl(tier));

        addDrop(event, createRandomLowestGem(event.getLevel().random)
        );
        addDrop(event, new ItemStack(Items.IRON_INGOT, 5)
        );
    }
}

package com.example.examplemod.event;

import com.example.examplemod.ExampleMod;
import com.example.examplemod.util.ItemGiveHelper;
import net.minecraft.server.level.ServerPlayer;
import net.neoforged.bus.api.SubscribeEvent;
import net.neoforged.fml.common.EventBusSubscriber;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.event.entity.player.PlayerEvent;

import java.util.List;

@EventBusSubscriber(modid = ExampleMod.MODID)
public class StarterKitEvent {

    private static final String GIVEN_TAG = "examplemod:has_received_portable_tools";

    // 你想根据 ID 发放的物品清单
    private static final List<String> STARTER_ITEMS = List.of(
            "examplemod:portable_furnace",
            "minecraft:crafting_table",
            "minecraft:smithing_table",
            "minecraft:enchanting_table",
            "apotheosis:salvaging_table",
            "apotheosis:gem_cutting_table",
            "apotheosis:simple_reforging_table",
            "irons_spellbooks:diamond_spell_book",
            "irons_spellbooks:inscription_table",
            "irons_spellbooks:scroll_forge",
            "l2backpack:dimensional_storage_white",
            "l2backpack:ender_backpack",
            "l2backpack:pickup_tweaker_tool",
            "l2backpack:gem_bag",
            "l2complements:eternal_anvil",
            "l2weaponry:shulkerate_round_shield",
            "irons_spellbooks:wizard_hat",
            "irons_spellbooks:wizard_chestplate",
            "irons_spellbooks:wizard_leggings",
            "irons_spellbooks:wizard_boots",
            "irons_spellbooks:artificer_cane",
            "l2archery:starter_bow",
            "l2backpack:backpack_white",
            "l2weaponry:shulkerate_hammer",
            "l2weaponry:shulkerate_battle_axe",
            "l2hostility:detector_glasses"
    );

    @SubscribeEvent
    public static void onPlayerLogin(PlayerEvent.PlayerLoggedInEvent event) {
        if (event.getEntity() instanceof ServerPlayer player) {
            if (!player.getPersistentData().getBoolean(GIVEN_TAG)) {

                for (String itemId : STARTER_ITEMS) {
                    ItemGiveHelper.giveItemById(player, itemId, 1);
                }

                player.getPersistentData().putBoolean(GIVEN_TAG, true);
            }
        }
    }

}

package com.example.examplemod;

import com.example.examplemod.compat.curios.ModLootCategories;
import com.example.examplemod.event.MobEquipmentHandler;
import com.example.examplemod.registry.ModDataComponents;
import com.example.examplemod.registry.ModEntities;
import com.example.examplemod.registry.ModMenus; // 导入菜单注册
import com.example.examplemod.registry.WeaponReplacementModifier;
import com.example.examplemod.spell.ModSpells;
import com.example.examplemod.spell.SummonedCaveSpider;
import com.example.examplemod.util.WeaponReplacer;
import com.mojang.serialization.MapCodec;
import net.neoforged.bus.api.IEventBus;
import net.neoforged.fml.ModContainer;
import net.neoforged.fml.common.Mod;
import net.neoforged.fml.config.ModConfig;
import net.neoforged.fml.event.lifecycle.FMLCommonSetupEvent;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.common.NeoForge;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.common.loot.IGlobalLootModifier;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.event.RegisterCommandsEvent;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.event.entity.EntityAttributeCreationEvent;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.registries.DeferredHolder;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.registries.DeferredRegister;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.registries.NeoForgeRegistries;
import org.slf4j.Logger;
import org.slf4j.LoggerFactory;

import static com.example.examplemod.registry.ModItems.ITEMS;

@Mod(ExampleMod.MODID)
public final class ExampleMod {
    public static final String MODID = "examplemod";
    private static final Logger LOGGER = LoggerFactory.getLogger(ExampleMod.class);

    public static final DeferredRegister<MapCodec<? extends IGlobalLootModifier>> LOOT_MODIFIERS =
            DeferredRegister.create(NeoForgeRegistries.Keys.GLOBAL_LOOT_MODIFIER_SERIALIZERS, MODID);

    public static final DeferredHolder<MapCodec<? extends IGlobalLootModifier>, MapCodec<WeaponReplacementModifier>> REPLACE_WEAPONS =
            LOOT_MODIFIERS.register("replace_weapons", () -> WeaponReplacementModifier.CODEC);

    public ExampleMod(IEventBus modEventBus, ModContainer modContainer) {
        modContainer.registerConfig(ModConfig.Type.CLIENT, Config.SPEC);
        ModSpells.register(modEventBus);
        ModEntities.register(modEventBus);
        ModDataComponents.COMPONENTS.register(modEventBus);
        ITEMS.register(modEventBus);
        ModMenus.MENUS.register(modEventBus);
        LOOT_MODIFIERS.register(modEventBus);
        modEventBus.addListener(this::registerEntityAttributes);
        modEventBus.addListener(ModLootCategories::onRegister);
        modEventBus.addListener(this::commonSetup);
        NeoForge.EVENT_BUS.addListener(ExampleMod::registerCommands);
        NeoForge.EVENT_BUS.register(MobEquipmentHandler.class);
    }

    private void registerEntityAttributes(EntityAttributeCreationEvent event) {
        event.put(ModEntities.SUMMONED_CAVE_SPIDER.get(), SummonedCaveSpider.createAttributes().build());
    }

    private void commonSetup(FMLCommonSetupEvent event) {
        event.enqueueWork(WeaponReplacer::initWeaponPools);
    }

    private static void registerCommands(RegisterCommandsEvent event) {
    }
}

package com.example.examplemod;

import com.example.examplemod.registry.ModEntities;
import com.example.examplemod.tooltip.*;
import net.minecraft.client.Minecraft;
import net.minecraft.client.gui.screens.inventory.AbstractContainerScreen;
import net.minecraft.client.renderer.entity.CaveSpiderRenderer;
import net.minecraft.network.chat.Component;
import net.minecraft.world.inventory.Slot;
import net.neoforged.api.distmarker.Dist;
import net.neoforged.bus.api.IEventBus;
import net.neoforged.bus.api.SubscribeEvent;
import net.neoforged.fml.ModContainer;
import net.neoforged.fml.common.EventBusSubscriber;
import net.neoforged.fml.common.Mod;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.client.event.*;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.client.gui.ConfigurationScreen;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.client.gui.IConfigScreenFactory;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.common.NeoForge;

@Mod(value = ExampleMod.MODID, dist = Dist.CLIENT)
@EventBusSubscriber(modid = ExampleMod.MODID, value = Dist.CLIENT) // 此注解管理本类中的游戏运行期事件(Key, Tooltip等)
public final class ExampleModClient {

    public ExampleModClient(IEventBus modBus, ModContainer container) {
        container.registerExtensionPoint(IConfigScreenFactory.class, ConfigurationScreen::new);

        // 统一在 MOD 总线上注册生命周期事件
        modBus.addListener(this::registerKeys);
        modBus.addListener(this::registerTooltipComponents);
        modBus.addListener(this::registerReloadListeners);
        modBus.addListener(this::registerRenderers);
        modBus.addListener(this::registerScreens); // 核心添加：绑定工具包界面！

        TooltipHandler.register();
        NeoForge.EVENT_BUS.register(ModelStageRenderer.class);
        NeoForge.EVENT_BUS.register(TooltipInteraction.class);
    }

    private void registerScreens(RegisterMenuScreensEvent event) {
    }

    private void registerRenderers(EntityRenderersEvent.RegisterRenderers event) {
        event.registerEntityRenderer(ModEntities.SUMMONED_CAVE_SPIDER.get(), CaveSpiderRenderer::new);
    }

    private void registerKeys(RegisterKeyMappingsEvent event) {

    }

    private void registerTooltipComponents(RegisterClientTooltipComponentFactoriesEvent event) {
        VisualComponents.registerFactories(event);
    }

    private void registerReloadListeners(RegisterClientReloadListenersEvent event) {
        event.registerReloadListener(TooltipRules.INSTANCE);
    }

    // ================= 下方为游戏运行期事件（GAME Bus） =================

    @SubscribeEvent
    public static void onClientCommandRegister(RegisterClientCommandsEvent event) {
    }

    @SubscribeEvent
    public static void handleKey(InputEvent.Key event) {
    }

    @SubscribeEvent
    public static void renderSlotDebug(ContainerScreenEvent.Render.Foreground event) {
        if (!Config.ENABLED.get() || !Config.SHOW_SLOT_DEBUG.get()) return;
        AbstractContainerScreen<?> screen = event.getContainerScreen();
        Slot slot = screen.getSlotUnderMouse();
        if (slot == null) return;

        boolean empty = !slot.hasItem();
        Component status = Component.translatable(
                empty ? "examplemod.slot_debug.empty" : "examplemod.slot_debug.item",
                slot.index
        );
        int x = screen.getGuiLeft() + slot.x;
        int y = screen.getGuiTop() + slot.y - 10;
        event.getGuiGraphics().drawString(Minecraft.getInstance().font, status, x, y,
                empty ? 0xFFFFAA00 : 0xFF55FF55, true);
    }

    @SubscribeEvent
    public static void tooltipWidth(RenderTooltipEvent.GatherComponents event) {
        if (Config.ENABLED.get()) {
            event.setMaxWidth(Config.MAX_TOOLTIP_WIDTH.get());
        }
    }

    @SubscribeEvent
    public static void tooltipColors(RenderTooltipEvent.Color event) {
        if (!Config.ENABLED.get()) return;

        event.setBackground(Config.getBackgroundColor());
        int border = Config.getBorderColor();

        if (Config.RARITY_THEME.get()) {
            Integer rarityColor = event.getItemStack().getRarity().color().getColor();
            if (rarityColor != null && rarityColor != 0) {
                border = 0xFF000000 | rarityColor;
            }
        }
        event.setBorderStart(border);
        event.setBorderEnd(border);
    }
}

package com.example.examplemod.item;

import net.minecraft.network.chat.Component;
import net.minecraft.world.InteractionHand;

import net.minecraft.world.item.ItemStack;
import net.minecraft.world.level.Level;

public class PortableAnvilItem extends Item {
    public PortableAnvilItem(Properties properties) { super(properties); }

    @Override
    public InteractionResultHolder<ItemStack> use(Level level, Player player, InteractionHand hand) {
        if (!level.isClientSide) {
            player.openMenu(new SimpleMenuProvider(
                (id, inv, p) -> new AnvilMenu(id, inv, ContainerLevelAccess.create(level, p.blockPosition())),
                Component.translatable("container.repair")
            ));
        }
        return InteractionResultHolder.sidedSuccess(player.getItemInHand(hand), level.isClientSide);
    }
}
