package com.example.examplemod.item;

import com.mojang.serialization.Codec;
import com.mojang.serialization.codecs.RecordCodecBuilder;
import net.minecraft.network.RegistryFriendlyByteBuf;
import net.minecraft.network.codec.ByteBufCodecs;
import net.minecraft.network.codec.StreamCodec;
import net.minecraft.world.item.ItemStack;

import java.util.List;

public record PortableBrewingData(
        List<ItemStack> items, // size 5: 0,1,2=药水, 3=材料, 4=烈焰粉
        int brewTime,
        int fuel
) {
    public static final PortableBrewingData EMPTY = new PortableBrewingData(
            List.of(ItemStack.EMPTY, ItemStack.EMPTY, ItemStack.EMPTY, ItemStack.EMPTY, ItemStack.EMPTY), 0, 0
    );

    public static final Codec<PortableBrewingData> CODEC = RecordCodecBuilder.create(inst -> inst.group(
            ItemStack.OPTIONAL_CODEC.listOf().fieldOf("items").forGetter(PortableBrewingData::items),
            Codec.INT.fieldOf("brew_time").forGetter(PortableBrewingData::brewTime),
            Codec.INT.fieldOf("fuel").forGetter(PortableBrewingData::fuel)
    ).apply(inst, PortableBrewingData::new));

    public static final StreamCodec<RegistryFriendlyByteBuf, PortableBrewingData> STREAM_CODEC = StreamCodec.composite(
            ItemStack.OPTIONAL_STREAM_CODEC.apply(ByteBufCodecs.list()), PortableBrewingData::items,
            ByteBufCodecs.VAR_INT, PortableBrewingData::brewTime,
            ByteBufCodecs.VAR_INT, PortableBrewingData::fuel,
            PortableBrewingData::new
    );
}

// ==================================================
// File: item\PortableBrewingStandItem.java
// ==================================================

package com.example.examplemod.item;

import com.example.examplemod.menu.PortableBrewingMenu;
import com.example.examplemod.registry.ModDataComponents;
import net.minecraft.network.chat.Component;
import net.minecraft.server.level.ServerPlayer;
import net.minecraft.world.InteractionHand;
import net.minecraft.world.InteractionResultHolder;
import net.minecraft.world.SimpleContainer;
import net.minecraft.world.SimpleMenuProvider;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.Entity;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.player.Player;
import net.minecraft.world.inventory.SimpleContainerData;
import net.minecraft.world.item.Item;
import net.minecraft.world.item.ItemStack;
import net.minecraft.world.item.Items;
import net.minecraft.world.item.alchemy.PotionBrewing;
import net.minecraft.world.level.Level;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;

public class PortableBrewingStandItem extends Item {
    public PortableBrewingStandItem(Properties properties) { super(properties); }

    @Override
    public InteractionResultHolder<ItemStack> use(Level level, Player player, InteractionHand hand) {
        ItemStack held = player.getItemInHand(hand);
        if (!level.isClientSide && player instanceof ServerPlayer serverPlayer) {
            PortableBrewingData data = held.getOrDefault(ModDataComponents.BREWING_DATA.get(), PortableBrewingData.EMPTY);

            SimpleContainer container = new SimpleContainer(5);
            for (int i = 0; i < 5; i++) {
                if (i < data.items().size()) container.setItem(i, data.items().get(i));
            }

            SimpleContainerData syncData = new SimpleContainerData(2);
            syncData.set(0, data.brewTime());
            syncData.set(1, data.fuel());

            serverPlayer.openMenu(new SimpleMenuProvider((id, inv, p) -> new PortableBrewingMenu(
                    id, inv, container, syncData, p.level(),
                    () -> {
                        List<ItemStack> list = new ArrayList<>();
                        for (int i = 0; i < 5; i++) list.add(container.getItem(i));
                        held.set(ModDataComponents.BREWING_DATA.get(), new PortableBrewingData(list, syncData.get(0), syncData.get(1)));
                    }
            ), Component.translatable("container.brewing")));
        }
        return InteractionResultHolder.sidedSuccess(held, level.isClientSide);
    }

    @Override
    public void inventoryTick(ItemStack stack, Level level, Entity entity, int slotId, boolean isSelected) {
        if (level.isClientSide || !(entity instanceof Player)) return;

        PortableBrewingData data = stack.getOrDefault(ModDataComponents.BREWING_DATA.get(), PortableBrewingData.EMPTY);
        List<ItemStack> items = new ArrayList<>(data.items());
        while (items.size() < 5) items.add(ItemStack.EMPTY);

        int brewTime = data.brewTime();
        int fuel = data.fuel();

        ItemStack fuelItem = items.get(4);
        if (fuel <= 0 && fuelItem.is(Items.BLAZE_POWDER)) {
            fuel = 20;
            fuelItem.shrink(1);
        }

        ItemStack ingredient = items.get(3);
        PotionBrewing potionBrewing = level.potionBrewing();

        boolean canBrew = false;
        if (fuel > 0 && !ingredient.isEmpty()) {
            for (int i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
                ItemStack potion = items.get(i);
                if (!potion.isEmpty() && potionBrewing.hasMix(potion, ingredient)) {
                    canBrew = true;
                    break;
                }
            }
        }

        if (canBrew) {
            brewTime++;
            if (brewTime >= 400) {
                brewTime = 0;
                fuel--;
                for (int i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
                    ItemStack potion = items.get(i);
                    if (!potion.isEmpty() && potionBrewing.hasMix(potion, ingredient)) {
                        items.set(i, potionBrewing.mix(ingredient, potion));
                    }
                }
                ingredient.shrink(1);
            }
        } else {
            brewTime = 0;
        }

        stack.set(ModDataComponents.BREWING_DATA.get(), new PortableBrewingData(items, brewTime, fuel));
    }
}

// ==================================================
// File: item\PortableFurnaceData.java

package com.example.examplemod.item;

import com.mojang.serialization.Codec;
import com.mojang.serialization.codecs.RecordCodecBuilder;
import net.minecraft.network.RegistryFriendlyByteBuf;
import net.minecraft.network.codec.ByteBufCodecs;
import net.minecraft.network.codec.StreamCodec;
import net.minecraft.world.item.ItemStack;

import java.util.List;

public record PortableFurnaceData(
        List<ItemStack> items, // size 3: 0=原料, 1=燃料, 2=产物
        int litTime,
        int litDuration,
        int cookingProgress,
        int cookingTotalTime
) {
    public static final PortableFurnaceData EMPTY = new PortableFurnaceData(
            List.of(ItemStack.EMPTY, ItemStack.EMPTY, ItemStack.EMPTY), 0, 0, 0, 200
    );

    public static final Codec<PortableFurnaceData> CODEC = RecordCodecBuilder.create(inst -> inst.group(
            ItemStack.OPTIONAL_CODEC.listOf().fieldOf("items").forGetter(PortableFurnaceData::items),
            Codec.INT.fieldOf("lit_time").forGetter(PortableFurnaceData::litTime),
            Codec.INT.fieldOf("lit_duration").forGetter(PortableFurnaceData::litDuration),
            Codec.INT.fieldOf("cooking_progress").forGetter(PortableFurnaceData::cookingProgress),
            Codec.INT.fieldOf("cooking_total_time").forGetter(PortableFurnaceData::cookingTotalTime)
    ).apply(inst, PortableFurnaceData::new));

    public static final StreamCodec<RegistryFriendlyByteBuf, PortableFurnaceData> STREAM_CODEC = StreamCodec.composite(
            ItemStack.OPTIONAL_STREAM_CODEC.apply(ByteBufCodecs.list()), PortableFurnaceData::items,
            ByteBufCodecs.VAR_INT, PortableFurnaceData::litTime,
            ByteBufCodecs.VAR_INT, PortableFurnaceData::litDuration,
            ByteBufCodecs.VAR_INT, PortableFurnaceData::cookingProgress,
            ByteBufCodecs.VAR_INT, PortableFurnaceData::cookingTotalTime,
            PortableFurnaceData::new
    );
}

// ==================================================
// File: item\PortableFurnaceItem.java
// ==================================================

package com.example.examplemod.item;

import com.example.examplemod.menu.PortableFurnaceMenu;
import com.example.examplemod.registry.ModDataComponents;
import net.minecraft.network.chat.Component;
import net.minecraft.server.level.ServerPlayer;
import net.minecraft.world.InteractionHand;
import net.minecraft.world.InteractionResultHolder;
import net.minecraft.world.SimpleContainer;
import net.minecraft.world.SimpleMenuProvider;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.Entity;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.player.Player;
import net.minecraft.world.inventory.SimpleContainerData;
import net.minecraft.world.item.Item;
import net.minecraft.world.item.ItemStack;
import net.minecraft.world.item.crafting.RecipeType;
import net.minecraft.world.item.crafting.SingleRecipeInput;
import net.minecraft.world.level.Level;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;

public class PortableFurnaceItem extends Item {
    public PortableFurnaceItem(Properties properties) { super(properties); }

    @Override
    public InteractionResultHolder<ItemStack> use(Level level, Player player, InteractionHand hand) {
        ItemStack held = player.getItemInHand(hand);
        if (!level.isClientSide && player instanceof ServerPlayer serverPlayer) {
            PortableFurnaceData data = held.getOrDefault(ModDataComponents.FURNACE_DATA.get(), PortableFurnaceData.EMPTY);

            SimpleContainer container = new SimpleContainer(3);
            for (int i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
                if (i < data.items().size()) container.setItem(i, data.items().get(i));
            }

            SimpleContainerData syncData = new SimpleContainerData(4);
            syncData.set(0, data.litTime());
            syncData.set(1, data.litDuration());
            syncData.set(2, data.cookingProgress());
            syncData.set(3, data.cookingTotalTime() <= 0 ? 200 : data.cookingTotalTime());

            serverPlayer.openMenu(new SimpleMenuProvider((id, inv, p) -> new PortableFurnaceMenu(
                    id, inv, container, syncData, p.level(),
                    () -> {
                        // 关闭界面保存
                        List<ItemStack> newItems = List.of(container.getItem(0), container.getItem(1), container.getItem(2));
                        held.set(ModDataComponents.FURNACE_DATA.get(), new PortableFurnaceData(
                                newItems, syncData.get(0), syncData.get(1), syncData.get(2), syncData.get(3)
                        ));
                    }
            ), Component.translatable("container.furnace")));
        }
        return InteractionResultHolder.sidedSuccess(held, level.isClientSide);
    }

    // 背包后台离线烧炼
    @Override
    public void inventoryTick(ItemStack stack, Level level, Entity entity, int slotId, boolean isSelected) {
        if (level.isClientSide || !(entity instanceof Player)) return;

        PortableFurnaceData data = stack.getOrDefault(ModDataComponents.FURNACE_DATA.get(), PortableFurnaceData.EMPTY);
        List<ItemStack> items = new ArrayList<>(data.items());
        while (items.size() < 3) items.add(ItemStack.EMPTY);

        ItemStack input = items.get(0);
        ItemStack fuel = items.get(1);
        ItemStack output = items.get(2);

        int litTime = data.litTime();
        int litDuration = data.litDuration();
        int cookingProgress = data.cookingProgress();
        int cookingTotal = data.cookingTotalTime() <= 0 ? 200 : data.cookingTotalTime();

        if (litTime > 0) litTime--;

        if (!input.isEmpty()) {
            SingleRecipeInput recipeInput = new SingleRecipeInput(input);
            var recipeHolder = level.getRecipeManager().getRecipeFor(RecipeType.SMELTING, recipeInput, level).orElse(null);

            if (recipeHolder != null) {
                ItemStack result = recipeHolder.value().assemble(recipeInput, level.registryAccess());
                boolean canInsert = output.isEmpty() || (ItemStack.isSameItemSameComponents(output, result) && output.getCount() + result.getCount() <= output.getMaxStackSize());

                if (canInsert) {
                    if (litTime <= 0 && !fuel.isEmpty()) {
                        int burn = fuel.getBurnTime(RecipeType.SMELTING);
                        if (burn > 0) {
                            litTime = burn;
                            litDuration = burn;
                            ItemStack remainder = fuel.getCraftingRemainingItem();
                            fuel.shrink(1);
                            if (fuel.isEmpty() && !remainder.isEmpty()) {
                                items.set(1, remainder);
                            }
                        }
                    }

                    if (litTime > 0) {
                        cookingProgress++;
                        if (cookingProgress >= cookingTotal) {
                            cookingProgress = 0;
                            input.shrink(1);
                            if (output.isEmpty()) {
                                items.set(2, result.copy());
                            } else {
                                output.grow(result.getCount());
                            }
                        }
                    }
                } else {
                    cookingProgress = 0;
                }
            } else {
                cookingProgress = 0;
            }
        } else {
            cookingProgress = 0;
        }

        stack.set(ModDataComponents.FURNACE_DATA.get(), new PortableFurnaceData(items, litTime, litDuration, cookingProgress, cookingTotal));
    }
}

// ==================================================

package com.example.examplemod.menu;

import net.minecraft.world.Container;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.player.Inventory;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.player.Player;
import net.minecraft.world.inventory.BrewingStandMenu;
import net.minecraft.world.inventory.ContainerData;
import net.minecraft.world.item.ItemStack;
import net.minecraft.world.item.Items;
import net.minecraft.world.item.alchemy.PotionBrewing;
import net.minecraft.world.level.Level;

public class PortableBrewingMenu extends BrewingStandMenu {
    private final Container container;
    private final ContainerData data;
    private final Level level;
    private final Runnable onClose;

    public PortableBrewingMenu(int containerId, Inventory playerInventory, Container container, ContainerData data, Level level, Runnable onClose) {
        super(containerId, playerInventory, container, data);
        this.container = container;
        this.data = data;
        this.level = level;
        this.onClose = onClose;
    }

    @Override
    public void broadcastChanges() {
        if (!level.isClientSide) {
            tickBrewing();
        }
        super.broadcastChanges();
    }

    private void tickBrewing() {
        int brewTime = data.get(0);
        int fuel = data.get(1);

        ItemStack fuelItem = container.getItem(4);
        if (fuel <= 0 && fuelItem.is(Items.BLAZE_POWDER)) {
            fuel = 20;
            fuelItem.shrink(1);
            data.set(1, fuel);
            container.setChanged();
        }

        ItemStack ingredient = container.getItem(3);
        PotionBrewing potionBrewing = level.potionBrewing();

        boolean canBrew = false;
        if (fuel > 0 && !ingredient.isEmpty()) {
            for (int i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
                ItemStack potion = container.getItem(i);
                if (!potion.isEmpty() && potionBrewing.hasMix(potion, ingredient)) {
                    canBrew = true;
                    break;
                }
            }
        }

        if (canBrew) {
            brewTime++;
            if (brewTime >= 400) {
                brewTime = 0;
                fuel--;
                data.set(1, fuel);
                for (int i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
                    ItemStack potion = container.getItem(i);
                    if (!potion.isEmpty() && potionBrewing.hasMix(potion, ingredient)) {
                        container.setItem(i, potionBrewing.mix(ingredient, potion));
                    }
                }
                ingredient.shrink(1);
                container.setChanged();
            }
            data.set(0, brewTime);
        } else {
            if (brewTime > 0) data.set(0, 0);
        }
    }

    @Override
    public void removed(Player player) {
        super.removed(player);
        if (onClose != null) onClose.run();
    }
}

// ==================================================
// File: menu\PortableFurnaceMenu.java
// ==================================================

package com.example.examplemod.menu;

import net.minecraft.server.level.ServerLevel;
import net.minecraft.world.Container;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.player.Inventory;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.player.Player;
import net.minecraft.world.inventory.ContainerData;
import net.minecraft.world.inventory.FurnaceMenu;
import net.minecraft.world.item.ItemStack;
import net.minecraft.world.item.crafting.RecipeType;
import net.minecraft.world.item.crafting.SingleRecipeInput;
import net.minecraft.world.level.Level;

public class PortableFurnaceMenu extends FurnaceMenu {
    private final Container container;
    private final ContainerData data;
    private final Level level;
    private final Runnable onClose;

    public PortableFurnaceMenu(int containerId, Inventory playerInventory, Container container, ContainerData data, Level level, Runnable onClose) {
        super(containerId, playerInventory, container, data);
        this.container = container;
        this.data = data;
        this.level = level;
        this.onClose = onClose;
    }

    // 核心：打开界面期间，服务端每 tick 都会执行这里！
    @Override
    public void broadcastChanges() {
        if (!level.isClientSide && level instanceof ServerLevel serverLevel) {
            tickSmelting(serverLevel);
        }
        super.broadcastChanges(); // 自动向客户端同步槽位物品与火苗、进度条
    }

    private void tickSmelting(ServerLevel level) {
        ItemStack input = container.getItem(0);
        ItemStack fuel = container.getItem(1);
        ItemStack output = container.getItem(2);

        int litTime = data.get(0);
        int litDuration = data.get(1);
        int cookingProgress = data.get(2);
        int cookingTotal = data.get(3) <= 0 ? 200 : data.get(3);

        boolean isLit = litTime > 0;
        if (isLit) {
            litTime--;
            data.set(0, litTime);
        }

        if (!input.isEmpty()) {
            SingleRecipeInput recipeInput = new SingleRecipeInput(input);
            var recipeHolder = level.getRecipeManager().getRecipeFor(RecipeType.SMELTING, recipeInput, level).orElse(null);

            if (recipeHolder != null) {
                ItemStack result = recipeHolder.value().assemble(recipeInput, level.registryAccess());
                boolean canInsert = output.isEmpty() || (ItemStack.isSameItemSameComponents(output, result) && output.getCount() + result.getCount() <= output.getMaxStackSize());

                if (canInsert) {
                    // 火灭了且有燃料，自动点火
                    if (litTime <= 0 && !fuel.isEmpty()) {
                        int burn = fuel.getBurnTime(RecipeType.SMELTING);
                        if (burn > 0) {
                            litTime = burn;
                            litDuration = burn;
                            data.set(0, litTime);
                            data.set(1, litDuration);
                            
                            // 消耗燃料（如岩浆桶返还空桶）
                            ItemStack remainder = fuel.getCraftingRemainingItem();
                            fuel.shrink(1);
                            if (fuel.isEmpty() && !remainder.isEmpty()) {
                                container.setItem(1, remainder);
                            }
                            container.setChanged();
                        }
                    }

                    // 正在燃烧，推进度条
                    if (litTime > 0) {
                        cookingProgress++;
                        if (cookingProgress >= cookingTotal) {
                            cookingProgress = 0;
                            input.shrink(1);
                            if (output.isEmpty()) {
                                container.setItem(2, result.copy());
                            } else {
                                output.grow(result.getCount());
                            }
                            container.setChanged();
                        }
                        data.set(2, cookingProgress);
                    }
                } else {
                    data.set(2, 0);
                }
            } else {
                data.set(2, 0);
            }
        } else {
            data.set(2, 0);
        }
    }

    // 关掉界面时，把最新物品和进度写回 ItemStack
    @Override
    public void removed(Player player) {
        super.removed(player);
        if (onClose != null) {
            onClose.run();
        }
    }
}

// File: mixin\apothic\AllStatsBonusMixin.java
// ==================================================

package com.example.examplemod.mixin.apothic;

import dev.shadowsoffire.apotheosis.socket.gem.bonus.special.AllStatsBonus;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.Mixin;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.At;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.Redirect;

import java.util.Map;

/**
 * 将全属性宝石（AllStatsBonus）的全局属性增幅降低为 1/4
 */
@Mixin(value = AllStatsBonus.class, remap = false)
public class AllStatsBonusMixin {

    @Redirect(
        method = {"addModifiers", "getSocketBonusTooltip"},
        at = @At(
            value = "INVOKE",
            target = "Ljava/util/Map;get(Ljava/lang/Object;)Ljava/lang/Object;"
        )
    )
    private Object scaleAllStats(Map<?, ?> map, Object key) {
        Object val = map.get(key);
        if (val instanceof Float f) {
            return f * 0.25F; // 所有关联属性的加成统一降低为 1/4
        }
        return val;
    }
}

package com.example.examplemod.mixin.apothic;

import dev.shadowsoffire.apotheosis.socket.gem.bonus.AttributeBonus;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.Mixin;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.At;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.ModifyArg;

/**
 * 将神化所有单属性宝石（AttributeBonus）的数值强制降低至原版的 1/4 (0.25x)
 */
@Mixin(value = AttributeBonus.class, remap = false)
public class AttributeBonusMixin {

    @ModifyArg(
        method = "createModifier(Ldev/shadowsoffire/apotheosis/socket/gem/GemView;)Lnet/minecraft/world/entity/ai/attributes/AttributeModifier;",
        at = @At(
            value = "INVOKE",
            target = "Lnet/minecraft/world/entity/ai/attributes/AttributeModifier;<init>(Lnet/minecraft/resources/ResourceLocation;DLnet/minecraft/world/entity/ai/attributes/AttributeModifier$Operation;)V"
        ),
        index = 1
    )
    private double scaleGemAttributeValueToQuarter(double originalValue) {
        // 将宝石提供的属性数值直接缩减为 1/4
        return originalValue * 0.25D;
    }
}

package com.example.examplemod.mixin.apothic;

import dev.shadowsoffire.apotheosis.socket.gem.bonus.DamageReductionBonus;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.Mixin;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.At;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.Redirect;

import java.util.Map;

/**
 * 将神化免伤宝石（DamageReductionBonus）的减伤率降低为 1/4
 */
@Mixin(value = DamageReductionBonus.class, remap = false)
public class DamageReductionBonusMixin {

    @Redirect(
        method = {"onHurt", "getSocketBonusTooltip"},
        at = @At(
            value = "INVOKE",
            target = "Ljava/util/Map;get(Ljava/lang/Object;)Ljava/lang/Object;"
        )
    )
    private Object scaleDamageReduction(Map<?, ?> map, Object key) {
        Object val = map.get(key);
        if (val instanceof Float f) {
            return f * 0.25F; // 免伤率变为原来的 1/4 (如 20% 减免 -> 5% 减免)
        }
        return val;
    }
}

// File: mixin\apothic\LootCategoryMixin.java
// ==================================================

package com.example.examplemod.mixin.apothic;

import com.example.examplemod.compat.curios.ModLootCategories;
import com.example.examplemod.registry.ModDataComponents;
import com.example.examplemod.util.CurioMimicHelper;
import dev.shadowsoffire.apotheosis.Apoth;
import dev.shadowsoffire.apotheosis.loot.LootCategory;
import dev.shadowsoffire.apothic_attributes.modifiers.EntitySlotGroup;
import net.minecraft.resources.ResourceLocation;
import net.minecraft.world.item.ItemStack;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.Mixin;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.Unique;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.At;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.Inject;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.callback.CallbackInfoReturnable;

@Mixin(value = LootCategory.class, remap = false)
public abstract class LootCategoryMixin {

    @Unique
    private static final ThreadLocal<ItemStack> CURRENT_QUERY_STACK = new ThreadLocal<>();

    /**
     * 1. 终结分类随机：
     * 保证同一物品 ID 每次都能随机获得神化分类（武器、胸甲等，继承全部词缀池）
     */
    @Inject(method = "forItem", at = @At("HEAD"), cancellable = true)
    private static void onForItem(ItemStack stack, CallbackInfoReturnable<LootCategory> cir) {
        if (stack.isEmpty() || !CurioMimicHelper.isCurio(stack)) {
            CURRENT_QUERY_STACK.remove();
            return;
        }

        if (!CurioMimicHelper.hasMimicCategory(stack)) {
            CurioMimicHelper.assignRandomCategory(stack);
        }

        CURRENT_QUERY_STACK.set(stack);

        ResourceLocation catKey = stack.get(ModDataComponents.MIMIC_CATEGORY.get());
        if (catKey != null) {
            LootCategory category = Apoth.BuiltInRegs.LOOT_CATEGORY.get(catKey);
            if (category != null && !category.isNone()) {
                cir.setReturnValue(category);
            }
        }
    }

    /**
     * 2. 终结槽位脱绑：
     * 返回我们真正注册好、带有 slotHolder 的合法 Curios 槽位组！
     */
    @Inject(method = "getSlots", at = @At("HEAD"), cancellable = true)
    private void onGetSlots(CallbackInfoReturnable<EntitySlotGroup> cir) {
        ItemStack current = CURRENT_QUERY_STACK.get();
        if (current != null && CurioMimicHelper.isCurio(current)) {
            // 从 ModLootCategories 获取该物品真实的 Curios 槽位组（如项链组、戒指组）
            EntitySlotGroup realCurioGroup = ModLootCategories.getGroupForStack(current);
            if (realCurioGroup != null) {
                cir.setReturnValue(realCurioGroup);
            }
        }
    }
}

package com.example.examplemod.mixin.apothic;

import dev.shadowsoffire.apotheosis.socket.gem.bonus.special.MageSlayerBonus;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.Mixin;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.At;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.Redirect;

import java.util.Map;

/**
 * 将弑法者宝石（MageSlayerBonus）的魔法吸收与回血比例降低为 1/4
 */
@Mixin(value = MageSlayerBonus.class, remap = false)
public class MageSlayerBonusMixin {

    @Redirect(
        method = {"onHurt", "getSocketBonusTooltip"},
        at = @At(
            value = "INVOKE",
            target = "Ljava/util/Map;get(Ljava/lang/Object;)Ljava/lang/Object;"
        )
    )
    private Object scaleMageSlayer(Map<?, ?> map, Object key) {
        Object val = map.get(key);
        if (val instanceof Float f) {
            return f * 0.25F; // 魔法减免与治疗效果同步降低至 1/4
        }
        return val;
    }
}

// ==================================================
// File: mixin\apothic\MixinGemItem.java
// ==================================================

package com.example.examplemod.mixin.apothic;

import dev.shadowsoffire.apotheosis.socket.gem.Gem;
import dev.shadowsoffire.apotheosis.socket.gem.GemItem;
import dev.shadowsoffire.apotheosis.socket.gem.GemRegistry;
import dev.shadowsoffire.apotheosis.socket.gem.Purity;
import net.minecraft.ChatFormatting;
import net.minecraft.core.component.DataComponents;
import net.minecraft.nbt.CompoundTag;
import net.minecraft.network.chat.Component;
import net.minecraft.server.level.ServerPlayer;
import net.minecraft.sounds.SoundEvents;
import net.minecraft.sounds.SoundSource;
import net.minecraft.util.RandomSource;
import net.minecraft.world.InteractionHand;
import net.minecraft.world.InteractionResultHolder;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.player.Player;
import net.minecraft.world.item.Item;
import net.minecraft.world.item.ItemStack;
import net.minecraft.world.item.TooltipFlag;
import net.minecraft.world.item.component.CustomData;
import net.minecraft.world.level.Level;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.Mixin;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.At;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.Inject;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.callback.CallbackInfo;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.callback.CallbackInfoReturnable;

import java.util.List;

@Mixin(value = GemItem.class, remap = false)
public abstract class MixinGemItem extends Item {

    public MixinGemItem(Properties properties) {
        super(properties);
    }

    @Override
    public InteractionResultHolder<ItemStack> use(Level level, Player player, InteractionHand hand) {
        ItemStack heldStack = player.getItemInHand(hand);
        CustomData customData = heldStack.getOrDefault(DataComponents.CUSTOM_DATA, CustomData.EMPTY);
        CompoundTag tag = customData.copyTag();

        if (tag.getBoolean("is_unidentified_gem")) {
            if (level.isClientSide) {
                return InteractionResultHolder.sidedSuccess(heldStack, true);
            }

            // 【防误放锁 1】：前置背包空间检测
            int freeSlot = player.getInventory().getFreeSlot();
            if (freeSlot == -1 && heldStack.getCount() > 1) {
                player.sendSystemMessage(Component.literal("背包已满，无法鉴定！请腾出空间后再试。").withStyle(ChatFormatting.RED));
                return InteractionResultHolder.fail(heldStack);
            }

            // 1. 严格获取箱子生成时锁死的纯度
            Purity purity = GemItem.getPurity(heldStack);
            RandomSource random = player.getRandom();

            // 2. 筛选出支持此纯度的宝石
            List<Gem> validGems = GemRegistry.INSTANCE.getValues().stream()
                    .filter(g -> purity.isAtLeast(g.getMinPurity()))
                    .toList();

            if (validGems.isEmpty()) {
                return InteractionResultHolder.fail(heldStack);
            }

            Gem selectedGem = validGems.get(random.nextInt(validGems.size()));
            ItemStack identifiedGem = selectedGem.toStack(purity);

            // 3. 扣除原石
            heldStack.shrink(1);

            // 【防误放锁 2】：手持位隔离放入 + 双端网络同步
            boolean storedInInventory = false;
            for (int i = 0; i < player.getInventory().items.size(); i++) {
                if (hand == InteractionHand.MAIN_HAND && i == player.getInventory().selected) {
                    continue;
                }
                ItemStack slotStack = player.getInventory().items.get(i);
                if (slotStack.isEmpty()) {
                    player.getInventory().items.set(i, identifiedGem);
                    storedInInventory = true;
                    break;
                }
            }

            if (!storedInInventory) {
                if (heldStack.isEmpty()) {
                    player.setItemInHand(hand, identifiedGem);
                } else {
                    player.drop(identifiedGem, false);
                }
            }

            // 双端广播防止幽灵物品
            if (player instanceof ServerPlayer serverPlayer) {
                serverPlayer.containerMenu.broadcastChanges();
            }

            // 【防误放锁 3】：12 tick 冷却
            player.getCooldowns().addCooldown(this, 12);

            level.playSound(null, player.getX(), player.getY(), player.getZ(),
                    SoundEvents.AMETHYST_BLOCK_CHIME, SoundSource.PLAYERS, 1.0f, 1.2f);
            player.sendSystemMessage(Component.literal("鉴定出宝石: ").withStyle(ChatFormatting.GOLD)
                    .append(identifiedGem.getHoverName()));

            return InteractionResultHolder.consume(heldStack);
        }

        return super.use(level, player, hand);
    }

    // =========================================================================
    // 1. 名字显示：未鉴定的[完美]宝石原石
    // =========================================================================
    @Inject(method = "getName", at = @At("HEAD"), cancellable = true)
    private void overrideUnidentifiedName(ItemStack stack, CallbackInfoReturnable<Component> cir) {
        CustomData data = stack.getOrDefault(DataComponents.CUSTOM_DATA, CustomData.EMPTY);
        if (data.copyTag().getBoolean("is_unidentified_gem")) {
            Purity purity = GemItem.getPurity(stack);
            // 拼接效果：未鉴定的[完美]宝石原石
            cir.setReturnValue(Component.literal("未鉴定的")
                    .append(purity.toComponent())
                    .append(Component.literal("宝石")));
        }
    }

    // =========================================================================
    // 2. Tooltip 显示：纯度放前面，自然句式
    // =========================================================================
    @Inject(method = "appendHoverText", at = @At("HEAD"), cancellable = true)
    private void overrideUnidentifiedTooltip(ItemStack stack, Item.TooltipContext ctx, List<Component> lines, TooltipFlag flag, CallbackInfo ci) {
        CustomData data = stack.getOrDefault(DataComponents.CUSTOM_DATA, CustomData.EMPTY);
        if (data.copyTag().getBoolean("is_unidentified_gem")) {
            lines.add(Component.literal("右键鉴定"));
            ci.cancel();
        }
    }
}

// ==================================================
// File: mixin\apothic\MixinLootTable.java
// ==================================================

package com.example.examplemod.mixin.apothic;

import dev.shadowsoffire.apotheosis.socket.gem.GemItem;
import dev.shadowsoffire.apotheosis.socket.gem.Purity;
import it.unimi.dsi.fastutil.objects.ObjectArrayList;
import net.minecraft.core.component.DataComponents;
import net.minecraft.world.item.ItemStack;
import net.minecraft.world.item.component.CustomData;
import net.minecraft.world.level.storage.loot.LootContext;
import net.minecraft.world.level.storage.loot.LootTable;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.Mixin;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.At;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.Inject;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.callback.CallbackInfoReturnable;

@Mixin(value = LootTable.class)
public class MixinLootTable {

    @Inject(method = "getRandomItems(Lnet/minecraft/world/level/storage/loot/LootContext;)Lit/unimi/dsi/fastutil/objects/ObjectArrayList;", at = @At("RETURN"))
    private void convertAllGemsToUnidentified(LootContext context, CallbackInfoReturnable<ObjectArrayList<ItemStack>> cir) {
        ObjectArrayList<ItemStack> lootList = cir.getReturnValue();
        if (lootList == null || lootList.isEmpty()) return;

        for (int i = 0; i < lootList.size(); i++) {
            ItemStack stack = lootList.get(i);

            // 凡是战利品里生成的神化宝石，且尚未被转化的
            if (stack.getItem() instanceof GemItem) {
                CustomData data = stack.getOrDefault(DataComponents.CUSTOM_DATA, CustomData.EMPTY);
                if (!data.copyTag().getBoolean("is_unidentified_gem")) {
                    
                    // 1. 【核心：直接读取神化官方已经计算好的真实纯度】
                    // 普通箱子神化算出来是碎裂，这里拿到的就绝对是碎裂，绝不会出现低级箱出无瑕！
                    Purity officialPurity = GemItem.getPurity(stack);

                    // 2. 擦除具体的 Gem 属性，只保留纯度，变成未鉴定原石
                    ItemStack unidentified = new ItemStack(stack.getItem());
                    unidentified.set(DataComponents.MAX_STACK_SIZE, 64);
                    GemItem.setPurity(unidentified, officialPurity);

                    CustomData.update(DataComponents.CUSTOM_DATA, unidentified, tag -> {
                        tag.putBoolean("is_unidentified_gem", true);
                        tag.putString("official_purity", officialPurity.name());
                    });

                    // 3. 替换回战利品列表
                    lootList.set(i, unidentified);
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

// File: mixin\apothic\MixinWorldTier.java
// ==================================================

package com.example.examplemod.mixin.apothic;

import dev.shadowsoffire.apotheosis.tiers.WorldTier;
import dev.xkmc.l2hostility.content.logic.DifficultyLevel;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.player.Player;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.Mixin;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.At;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.Inject;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.callback.CallbackInfoReturnable;

@Mixin(WorldTier.class)
public class MixinWorldTier {
    @Inject(
            method = "isUnlocked",
            at = @At("RETURN"),
            cancellable = true
    )
    private static void checkCustomRequirements(
            Player player,
            WorldTier tier,
            CallbackInfoReturnable<Boolean> cir
    ) {
        if (!cir.getReturnValue()) {
            return;
        }

        int hostilityLevel = DifficultyLevel.ofAny(player);

        boolean valid = switch (tier) {
            case HAVEN -> true;
            case FRONTIER ->
                    hostilityLevel >= 60 && player.experienceLevel >= 80;
            case ASCENT ->
                    hostilityLevel >= 120 && player.experienceLevel >= 160;
            case SUMMIT ->
                    hostilityLevel >= 180 && player.experienceLevel >= 240;
            case PINNACLE ->
                    hostilityLevel >= 300 && player.experienceLevel >= 320;
        };
        cir.setReturnValue(valid);
    }
}

package com.example.examplemod.mixin.apothic;

import dev.shadowsoffire.apotheosis.client.WorldTierSelectScreen;
import dev.shadowsoffire.apotheosis.tiers.WorldTier;
import net.minecraft.ChatFormatting;
import net.minecraft.network.chat.Component;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.Mixin;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.At;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.Inject;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.callback.CallbackInfoReturnable;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;

@Mixin(WorldTierSelectScreen.class)
public class MixinWorldTierSelectScreen {

    @Inject(
            method = "tierLocked",
            at = @At("RETURN"),
            cancellable = true
    )
    private static void addLevelRequirements(
            WorldTier tier,
            CallbackInfoReturnable<List<Component>> cir
    ) {
        List<Component> list = new ArrayList<>(cir.getReturnValue());

        int hostilityRequirement = switch (tier) {
            case HAVEN -> 0;
            case FRONTIER -> 30;
            case ASCENT -> 90;
            case SUMMIT -> 150;
            case PINNACLE -> 210;
        };

        int experienceRequirement = switch (tier) {
            case HAVEN -> 0;
            case FRONTIER -> 80;
            case ASCENT -> 160;
            case SUMMIT -> 240;
            case PINNACLE -> 320;
        };

        if (tier != WorldTier.HAVEN) {
            list.add(Component.empty());
            list.add(Component.literal(
                    "需要恶意等级: " + hostilityRequirement
                            + "，玩家等级: " + experienceRequirement
            ).withStyle(ChatFormatting.YELLOW));
        }

        cir.setReturnValue(list);
    }
}

package com.example.examplemod.mixin.apothic;

import dev.shadowsoffire.apotheosis.socket.gem.bonus.MultiAttrBonus;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.Mixin;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.At;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.ModifyArg;

/**
 * 针对神化多属性复合宝石（MultiAttrBonus）内部类 ModifierInst
 * 将构建 AttributeModifier 时的数值缩减为 1/4 (0.25x)
 */
@Mixin(value = MultiAttrBonus.ModifierInst.class, remap = false)
public class MultiAttrBonusMixin {

    @ModifyArg(
        method = "build(Lnet/minecraft/resources/ResourceLocation;Ldev/shadowsoffire/apotheosis/socket/gem/Purity;)Lnet/minecraft/world/entity/ai/attributes/AttributeModifier;",
        at = @At(
            value = "INVOKE",
            target = "Lnet/minecraft/world/entity/ai/attributes/AttributeModifier;<init>(Lnet/minecraft/resources/ResourceLocation;DLnet/minecraft/world/entity/ai/attributes/AttributeModifier$Operation;)V"
        ),
        index = 1
    )
    private double scaleMultiAttributeValueToQuarter(double originalValue) {
        // 将复合宝石提供的每个属性数值都乘以 0.25
        return originalValue * 0.25D;
    }
}

// ==================================================
// File: mixin\ironsspellbooks\ISSMobEffectBalanceMixin.java
// ==================================================

package com.example.examplemod.mixin.ironsspellbooks;

import io.redspace.ironsspellbooks.IronsSpellbooks;
import net.minecraft.core.Holder;
import net.minecraft.core.registries.BuiltInRegistries;
import net.minecraft.resources.ResourceLocation;
import net.minecraft.world.effect.MobEffect;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.ai.attributes.Attribute;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.ai.attributes.AttributeModifier;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.ai.attributes.Attributes;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.Mixin;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.Unique;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.At;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.Inject;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.callback.CallbackInfo;

import java.util.function.BiConsumer;

@Mixin(MobEffect.class)
public abstract class ISSMobEffectBalanceMixin {

    // 预存各效果对应的修饰符 ResourceLocation
    @Unique private static final ResourceLocation FORTIFY_ID = IronsSpellbooks.id("mobeffect_fortify");
    @Unique private static final ResourceLocation VIGOR_ID = IronsSpellbooks.id("mobeffect_vigor");

    @Inject(method = "createModifiers", at = @At("HEAD"), cancellable = true)
    private void irons_spellbooks$rebalanceModifiers(int amplifier, BiConsumer<Holder<Attribute>, AttributeModifier> consumer, CallbackInfo ci) {
        MobEffect self = (MobEffect) (Object) this;
        ResourceLocation effectKey = BuiltInRegistries.MOB_EFFECT.getKey(self);

        if (effectKey == null || !effectKey.getNamespace().equals("irons_spellbooks")) {
            return;
        }

        int level = amplifier + 1; // 药水等级（I 级为 1，II 级为 2 ...）

        switch (effectKey.getPath()) {
            case "fortify" -> {
                // 原版：每级 +1.0 点黄心 (ADD_VALUE)
                // 整合包改动：改为每级固定 +20.0 点（10颗心），或者按需求改成百分比
                consumer.accept(Attributes.MAX_ABSORPTION, new AttributeModifier(
                    FORTIFY_ID,
                    level * 20.0D, 
                    AttributeModifier.Operation.ADD_VALUE
                ));
                ci.cancel();
            }

            case "vigor" -> {
                // 原版：每级 +2.0 点生命 (ADD_VALUE)
                // 整合包改动：直接改成百分比生命上限提升（每级 +25% 基础生命）
                consumer.accept(Attributes.MAX_HEALTH, new AttributeModifier(
                    VIGOR_ID,
                    level * 0.1D,
                    AttributeModifier.Operation.ADD_MULTIPLIED_TOTAL
                ));
                ci.cancel();
            }


            default -> {}
        }
    }
}

// ==================================================
// File: mixin\ironsspellbooks\MixinRandomizeSpellFunction.java
// ==================================================

package com.example.examplemod.mixin.ironsspellbooks;

import io.redspace.ironsspellbooks.api.registry.SpellRegistry;
import io.redspace.ironsspellbooks.api.spells.AbstractSpell;
import io.redspace.ironsspellbooks.api.spells.SchoolType;
import io.redspace.ironsspellbooks.api.spells.SpellRarity;
import io.redspace.ironsspellbooks.item.Scroll;
import io.redspace.ironsspellbooks.loot.RandomizeSpellFunction;
import io.redspace.ironsspellbooks.loot.SpellFilter;
import net.minecraft.core.component.DataComponents;
import net.minecraft.world.item.ItemStack;
import net.minecraft.world.item.component.CustomData;
import net.minecraft.world.level.storage.loot.LootContext;
import net.minecraft.world.level.storage.loot.providers.number.NumberProvider;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.Final;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.Mixin;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.Shadow;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.At;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.Inject;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.callback.CallbackInfoReturnable;

import java.util.List;
import java.util.NavigableMap;
import java.util.TreeMap;

@Mixin(value = RandomizeSpellFunction.class, remap = false)
public class MixinRandomizeSpellFunction {

    @Shadow @Final private NumberProvider qualityRange;
    @Shadow @Final private SpellFilter applicableSpells;

    @Inject(method = "run", at = @At("HEAD"), cancellable = true)
    private void interceptScrollLoot(ItemStack stack, LootContext context, CallbackInfoReturnable<ItemStack> cir) {
        if (stack.getItem() instanceof Scroll) {
            List<AbstractSpell> pool = this.applicableSpells.getApplicableSpells();
            if (pool.isEmpty()) {
                return;
            }

            // 1. 100% 走官方原版的加权算法抽取一次法术与计算等级
            NavigableMap<Integer, AbstractSpell> spellList = new TreeMap<>();
            int total = 0;
            for (AbstractSpell entry : pool) {
                total += switch (SpellRarity.values()[entry.getMinRarity()]) {
                    case COMMON -> 40;
                    case UNCOMMON -> 30;
                    case RARE -> 15;
                    case EPIC -> 8;
                    case LEGENDARY -> 4;
                };
                spellList.put(total, entry);
            }

            AbstractSpell sampleSpell = spellList.higherEntry(context.getRandom().nextInt(total)).getValue();
            if (sampleSpell.equals(SpellRegistry.none())) {
                return;
            }

            // 2. 官方等级公式：计算真实等级
            float quality = this.qualityRange.getFloat(context);
            int spellLevel = 1 + Math.round(quality * (float) (sampleSpell.getMaxLevel() - 1));

            // 3. 【核心】：直接调用官方方法获取该法术在该等级下的真实官方稀有度！
            SpellRarity officialRarity = sampleSpell.getRarity(spellLevel);

            // 4. 强制 64 堆叠
            stack.set(DataComponents.MAX_STACK_SIZE, 64);

            // 5. 写入官方稀有度与学派过滤（如果有）
            CustomData.update(DataComponents.CUSTOM_DATA, stack, tag -> {
                tag.putBoolean("is_unidentified", true);
                tag.putString("spell_rarity", officialRarity.name());

                // 兼容学派箱子
                int totalEnabled = SpellRegistry.getEnabledSpells().size();
                if (pool.size() < totalEnabled && !pool.isEmpty()) {
                    SchoolType firstSchool = pool.get(0).getSchoolType();
                    if (pool.stream().allMatch(s -> s.getSchoolType() == firstSchool)) {
                        tag.putString("school_filter", firstSchool.getId().toString());
                    }
                }
            });

            cir.setReturnValue(stack);
        }
    }
}

// ==================================================
// File: mixin\ironsspellbooks\MixinScroll.java
// ==================================================

package com.example.examplemod.mixin.ironsspellbooks;

import io.redspace.ironsspellbooks.api.registry.SchoolRegistry;
import io.redspace.ironsspellbooks.api.registry.SpellRegistry;
import io.redspace.ironsspellbooks.api.spells.AbstractSpell;
import io.redspace.ironsspellbooks.api.spells.ISpellContainer;
import io.redspace.ironsspellbooks.api.spells.SchoolType;
import io.redspace.ironsspellbooks.api.spells.SpellRarity;
import io.redspace.ironsspellbooks.item.Scroll;
import io.redspace.ironsspellbooks.registries.ItemRegistry;
import net.minecraft.ChatFormatting;
import net.minecraft.core.component.DataComponents;
import net.minecraft.nbt.CompoundTag;
import net.minecraft.network.chat.Component;
import net.minecraft.resources.ResourceLocation;
import net.minecraft.server.level.ServerPlayer;
import net.minecraft.sounds.SoundEvents;
import net.minecraft.sounds.SoundSource;
import net.minecraft.util.RandomSource;
import net.minecraft.world.InteractionHand;
import net.minecraft.world.InteractionResultHolder;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.player.Player;
import net.minecraft.world.item.Item;
import net.minecraft.world.item.ItemStack;
import net.minecraft.world.item.TooltipFlag;
import net.minecraft.world.item.component.CustomData;
import net.minecraft.world.level.Level;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.Mixin;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.Unique;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.At;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.Inject;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.callback.CallbackInfo;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.callback.CallbackInfoReturnable;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Locale;

@Mixin(value = Scroll.class, remap = false)
public abstract class MixinScroll extends Item {

    public MixinScroll(Properties properties) {
        super(properties);
    }

    // =========================================================================
    // 【底层安全盾】：杜绝空串崩溃，保证任何脏数据都不闪退
    // =========================================================================
    @Unique
    private static SpellRarity yourMod$safeGetRarity(CompoundTag tag) {
        if (tag.contains("spell_rarity")) {
            String str = tag.getString("spell_rarity").trim();
            if (!str.isEmpty()) {
                try {
                    return SpellRarity.valueOf(str.toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT));
                } catch (IllegalArgumentException ignored) {}
            }
        }
        return SpellRarity.COMMON; // 遇空值/非法值安全兜底为普通
    }

    @Inject(method = "use", at = @At("HEAD"), cancellable = true)
    private void handleScrollUse(Level level, Player player, InteractionHand hand, CallbackInfoReturnable<InteractionResultHolder<ItemStack>> cir) {
        ItemStack heldStack = player.getItemInHand(hand);
        CustomData customData = heldStack.getOrDefault(DataComponents.CUSTOM_DATA, CustomData.EMPTY);
        CompoundTag tag = customData.copyTag();

        if (tag.getBoolean("is_unidentified")) {
            if (level.isClientSide) {
                cir.setReturnValue(InteractionResultHolder.sidedSuccess(heldStack, true));
                return;
            }

            // 【防误放锁 1】：前置背包空间检测
            int freeSlot = player.getInventory().getFreeSlot();
            if (freeSlot == -1 && heldStack.getCount() > 1) {
                player.sendSystemMessage(Component.literal("背包已满，无法鉴定！请腾出空间后再试。").withStyle(ChatFormatting.RED));
                cir.setReturnValue(InteractionResultHolder.fail(heldStack));
                return;
            }

            // 1. 安全读取官方品质
            SpellRarity targetRarity = yourMod$safeGetRarity(tag);
            String schoolFilter = tag.contains("school_filter") ? tag.getString("school_filter") : null;

            // 2. 根据官方稀有度生成真实卷轴
            ItemStack identifiedScroll = yourMod$createIdentifiedScroll(targetRarity, schoolFilter, player.getRandom());
            if (identifiedScroll.isEmpty()) {
                return;
            }

            // 3. 扣除 1 个未鉴定卷轴
            if (!player.isCreative()) {
                heldStack.shrink(1);
            }

            // 【防误放锁 2】：手持位隔离放入 + 双端网络同步
            boolean inserted = false;
            for (int i = 0; i < player.getInventory().items.size(); i++) {
                if (hand == InteractionHand.MAIN_HAND && i == player.getInventory().selected) {
                    continue; // 跳过当前交互手
                }
                ItemStack slotStack = player.getInventory().items.get(i);
                if (ItemStack.isSameItemSameComponents(slotStack, identifiedScroll) && slotStack.getCount() < slotStack.getMaxStackSize()) {
                    slotStack.grow(1);
                    inserted = true;
                    break;
                } else if (slotStack.isEmpty()) {
                    player.getInventory().items.set(i, identifiedScroll);
                    inserted = true;
                    break;
                }
            }

            if (!inserted) {
                if (heldStack.isEmpty()) {
                    player.setItemInHand(hand, identifiedScroll);
                } else {
                    player.drop(identifiedScroll, false);
                }
            }

            if (player instanceof ServerPlayer serverPlayer) {
                serverPlayer.containerMenu.broadcastChanges();
            }

            // 【防误放锁 3】：12 tick 底层冷却
            player.getCooldowns().addCooldown(this, 12);

            level.playSound(null, player.getX(), player.getY(), player.getZ(),
                    SoundEvents.PLAYER_LEVELUP, SoundSource.PLAYERS, 0.6f, 1.2f);
            player.sendSystemMessage(Component.literal("鉴定出卷轴: ").withStyle(ChatFormatting.GOLD)
                    .append(identifiedScroll.getHoverName()));

            cir.setReturnValue(InteractionResultHolder.consume(heldStack));
        }
    }

    @Unique
    private ItemStack yourMod$createIdentifiedScroll(SpellRarity targetRarity, String schoolFilter, RandomSource random) {
        List<AbstractSpell> validSpells = SpellRegistry.REGISTRY.stream()
                .filter(s -> s.isEnabled() && s != SpellRegistry.none() && s.allowLooting())
                .filter(s -> targetRarity.getValue() >= s.getMinRarity() && targetRarity.getValue() <= s.getMaxRarity())
                .filter(s -> {
                    if (schoolFilter == null) return true;
                    return s.getSchoolType().getId().toString().equals(schoolFilter);
                })
                .toList();

        if (validSpells.isEmpty()) {
            return ItemStack.EMPTY;
        }

        AbstractSpell chosenSpell = validSpells.get(random.nextInt(validSpells.size()));

        List<Integer> matchingLevels = new ArrayList<>();
        for (int lvl = chosenSpell.getMinLevel(); lvl <= chosenSpell.getMaxLevel(); lvl++) {
            if (chosenSpell.getRarity(lvl) == targetRarity) {
                matchingLevels.add(lvl);
            }
        }

        int chosenLevel = matchingLevels.isEmpty()
                ? chosenSpell.getMinLevelForRarity(targetRarity)
                : matchingLevels.get(random.nextInt(matchingLevels.size()));

        ItemStack result = new ItemStack(ItemRegistry.SCROLL.get());
        ISpellContainer.createScrollContainer(chosenSpell, chosenLevel, result);
        return result;
    }

    // =========================================================================
    // 3. 拦截未鉴定名字显示
    // =========================================================================
    @Inject(method = "getName", at = @At("HEAD"), cancellable = true)
    private void overrideUnidentifiedName(ItemStack stack, CallbackInfoReturnable<Component> cir) {
        CustomData data = stack.getOrDefault(DataComponents.CUSTOM_DATA, CustomData.EMPTY);
        if (data.copyTag().getBoolean("is_unidentified")) {
            SpellRarity rarity = yourMod$safeGetRarity(data.copyTag());
            cir.setReturnValue(Component.literal("未鉴定的")
                    .append(rarity.getDisplayName())
                    .append(Component.literal("法术卷轴")));
        }
    }

    // =========================================================================
    // 4. 拦截 Tooltip：保持你喜欢的自然语序（[品质]品质，[学派]学派限定）
    // =========================================================================
    @Inject(method = "appendHoverText", at = @At("HEAD"), cancellable = true)
    private void overrideUnidentifiedTooltip(ItemStack stack, Item.TooltipContext context, List<Component> lines, TooltipFlag flag, CallbackInfo ci) {
        CustomData data = stack.getOrDefault(DataComponents.CUSTOM_DATA, CustomData.EMPTY);
        if (data.copyTag().getBoolean("is_unidentified")) {
            CompoundTag tag = data.copyTag();

            // 你喜欢的语序：[火焰]学派限定
            if (tag.contains("school_filter")) {
                SchoolType school = SchoolRegistry.getSchool(ResourceLocation.parse(tag.getString("school_filter")));
                if (school != null) {
                    lines.add(Component.literal("")
                            .append(school.getDisplayName())
                            .append(Component.literal("学派限定")));
                }
            }

            lines.add(Component.literal("右键解读法术"));
            ci.cancel();
        }
    }
}

package com.example.examplemod.mixin.l2artifacts;


import com.example.examplemod.util.ArtifactStatFormatter;
import dev.xkmc.l2artifacts.content.core.ArtifactStats;
import dev.xkmc.l2artifacts.content.core.StatEntry;
import dev.xkmc.l2artifacts.content.search.upgrade.AugmentMenuScreen;
import net.minecraft.network.chat.Component;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.Mixin;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.Shadow;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.At;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.Inject;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.callback.CallbackInfoReturnable;

import javax.annotation.Nullable;

@Mixin(value = AugmentMenuScreen.class, remap = false)
public class AugmentMenuScreenMixin {

    @Shadow @Nullable private ArtifactStats current;

    @Inject(method = "addEntry", at = @At("RETURN"), cancellable = true)
    private void modifyAddEntry(boolean main, StatEntry entry, StatEntry old, boolean lit_name, boolean lit_stat, CallbackInfoReturnable<Component[]> cir) {
        Component[] ans = cir.getReturnValue();
        if (ans != null && ans.length >= 2 && this.current != null) {
            // ans[1] 是原版显示数值的组件（如 +14.0%）
            ans[1] = ArtifactStatFormatter.format(ans[1], entry, this.current.level(), main);
            cir.setReturnValue(ans);
        }
    }
}

// ==================================================
// File: mixin\l2artifacts\BaseArtifactMixin.java
// ==================================================

package com.example.examplemod.mixin.l2artifacts;

import com.example.examplemod.util.ArtifactStatFormatter;
import dev.xkmc.l2artifacts.content.core.ArtifactStats;
import dev.xkmc.l2artifacts.content.core.BaseArtifact;
import dev.xkmc.l2artifacts.content.core.StatEntry;
import net.minecraft.network.chat.Component;
import net.minecraft.world.item.Item;
import net.minecraft.world.item.ItemStack;
import net.minecraft.world.item.TooltipFlag;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.Mixin;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.At;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.Inject;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.callback.CallbackInfo;

import java.util.List;
import java.util.Optional;

@Mixin(value = BaseArtifact.class, remap = false)
public class BaseArtifactMixin {

    @Inject(method = "appendHoverText", at = @At("TAIL"))
    private void appendPercentageToHoverText(ItemStack stack, Item.TooltipContext ctx, List<Component> list, TooltipFlag flag, CallbackInfo ci) {
        // 1. 按住 Shift 状态下原版展示的是经验等详细信息，不展示词条列表
        if (flag.hasShiftDown()) return;

        Optional<ArtifactStats> statsOpt = BaseArtifact.getStats(stack);
        if (statsOpt.isEmpty()) return;

        ArtifactStats stats = statsOpt.get();
        // 2. 还有待升级未确认时，原版只显示“有可用升级”，不展示词条
        if (stats.level() > stats.old_level()) return;

        int currentLevel = stats.level();

        // 3. 匹配并替换主词条
        if (stats.main_stat() != null) {
            Component mainTip = stats.main_stat().getTooltip(null);
            if (mainTip != null) {
                String targetStr = mainTip.getString();
                for (int i = 0; i < list.size(); i++) {
                    if (list.get(i).getString().equals(targetStr)) {
                        list.set(i, ArtifactStatFormatter.format(list.get(i), stats.main_stat(), currentLevel, true));
                        break;
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        // 4. 匹配并替换副词条
        if (stats.sub_stats() != null) {
            for (StatEntry sub : stats.sub_stats()) {
                Component subTip = sub.getTooltip(null);
                if (subTip != null) {
                    String targetStr = subTip.getString();
                    for (int i = 0; i < list.size(); i++) {
                        if (list.get(i).getString().equals(targetStr)) {
                            list.set(i, ArtifactStatFormatter.format(list.get(i), sub, currentLevel, false));
                            break;
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

// ==================================================

package com.example.examplemod.mixin.l2artifacts;

import dev.xkmc.l2artifacts.compat.L2HostilityCompat;
import dev.xkmc.l2core.capability.attachment.GeneralCapabilityHolder;
import dev.xkmc.l2hostility.content.capability.mob.MobTraitCap;
import dev.xkmc.l2hostility.init.registrate.LHMiscs;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.Mob;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.Mixin;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.Overwrite;

import java.util.Optional;

@Mixin(value = L2HostilityCompat.class, remap = false)
public class L2HostilityCompatMixin {

    /**
     * @author examplemod
     * @reason 装了恶意模组时，只根据恶意等级判断，无视怪物自身血量
     */
    @Overwrite
    private static boolean validForDropForHostility(Mob e, int min, int max) {
        // 获取怪物在 L2Hostility 里的 Capability
        Optional<MobTraitCap> opt = ((GeneralCapabilityHolder) LHMiscs.MOB.type()).getExisting(e);
        if (opt.isEmpty()) {
            return false;
        }

        // 取出怪物的真实等级
        int lv = opt.get().getLevel();

        // 【修改的核心】：只看等级
        // 1. 等级必须 >= min
        // 2. 如果 max <= 0 表示无上限；否则等级必须 < max
        return lv >= min && (max <= 0 || lv < max);
    }
}

// ==================================================
// File: mixin\l2artifacts\UpgradeScreenMixin.java
// ==================================================

package com.example.examplemod.mixin.l2artifacts;

import com.example.examplemod.util.ArtifactStatFormatter;
import dev.xkmc.l2artifacts.content.core.ArtifactStats;
import dev.xkmc.l2artifacts.content.core.StatEntry;
import dev.xkmc.l2artifacts.content.search.upgrade.UpgradeMenuScreen;
import net.minecraft.network.chat.Component;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.Mixin;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.Shadow;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.At;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.Inject;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.callback.CallbackInfoReturnable;

import javax.annotation.Nullable;

@Mixin(value = UpgradeMenuScreen.class, remap = false)
public class UpgradeScreenMixin {

    @Shadow @Nullable private ArtifactStats current;

    @Inject(method = "addEntry", at = @At("RETURN"), cancellable = true)
    private void modifyAddEntry(boolean main, StatEntry entry, @Nullable StatEntry old, CallbackInfoReturnable<Component[]> cir) {
        Component[] ans = cir.getReturnValue();
        if (ans != null && ans.length >= 2 && this.current != null) {
            // ans[1] 是原版显示数值的组件（如 +14.0%）
            ans[1] = ArtifactStatFormatter.format(ans[1], entry, this.current.level(), main);
            cir.setReturnValue(ans);
        }
    }
}

        method = "tick()V",

package com.example.examplemod.mixin.villna;

import net.minecraft.core.BlockPos;
import net.minecraft.sounds.SoundEvent;
import net.minecraft.sounds.SoundSource;
import net.minecraft.world.level.Level;
import net.minecraft.world.level.block.entity.ChestBlockEntity;
import net.minecraft.world.level.block.state.BlockState;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.Mixin;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.Overwrite;

@Mixin(ChestBlockEntity.class)
public class ChestBlockEntityMixin {
    /**
     * @author
     * @reason
     */
    @Overwrite
    public static void playSound(Level level, BlockPos pos, BlockState state, SoundEvent sound) {
        level.playSound(null, pos, sound, SoundSource.BLOCKS, 0.5F, level.random.nextFloat() * 0.1F + 0.9F);

    }
}


// ==================================================
// File: mixin\villna\CompassItemMixin.java
// ==================================================

package com.example.examplemod.mixin.villna;

import net.minecraft.core.BlockPos;
import net.minecraft.core.GlobalPos;
import net.minecraft.core.component.DataComponents;
import net.minecraft.sounds.SoundEvents;
import net.minecraft.sounds.SoundSource;
import net.minecraft.tags.BlockTags;
import net.minecraft.world.InteractionResult;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.player.Player;
import net.minecraft.world.item.CompassItem;
import net.minecraft.world.item.Item;
import net.minecraft.world.item.ItemStack;
import net.minecraft.world.item.Items;
import net.minecraft.world.item.component.LodestoneTracker;
import net.minecraft.world.item.context.UseOnContext;
import net.minecraft.world.level.Level;
import net.minecraft.world.level.block.Blocks;
import net.minecraft.world.level.block.state.BlockState;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.Mixin;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.At;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.Inject;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.callback.CallbackInfoReturnable;

import java.util.Optional;

@Mixin(CompassItem.class)
public abstract class CompassItemMixin extends Item {

    // 只需要继承构造函数，为了不报错
    public CompassItemMixin(Properties properties) {
        super(properties);
    }

    @Inject(method = "useOn", at = @At("HEAD"), cancellable = true)
    private void onUseOnBed(UseOnContext context, CallbackInfoReturnable<InteractionResult> cir) {
        Level level = context.getLevel();
        BlockPos pos = context.getClickedPos();
        BlockState state = level.getBlockState(pos);

        if (state.is(BlockTags.BEDS) && state.is(Blocks.RESPAWN_ANCHOR)) {
            
            // 1. 播放“锁定”音效
            level.playSound(null, pos, SoundEvents.LODESTONE_COMPASS_LOCK, SoundSource.PLAYERS, 1.0F, 1.0F);

            if (!level.isClientSide) {
                Player player = context.getPlayer();
                ItemStack handStack = context.getItemInHand();

                // 2. 创建追踪组件
                // tracked = false 很重要！
                // 这表示“只记录静态坐标，不检查方块是否是磁石”。
                // 这样即使床被挖了，指南针依然会指向那个位置（类似 GPS）。
                LodestoneTracker tracker = new LodestoneTracker(
                        Optional.of(GlobalPos.of(level.dimension(), pos)),
                        false
                );

                // 3. 处理物品堆叠 (如果只有1个，直接变；如果有多个，拆分)
                if (handStack.getCount() == 1) {
                    handStack.set(DataComponents.LODESTONE_TRACKER, tracker);
                } else {
                    ItemStack newStack = handStack.transmuteCopy(Items.COMPASS, 1);
                    handStack.shrink(1);
                    newStack.set(DataComponents.LODESTONE_TRACKER, tracker);

                    if (!player.getInventory().add(newStack)) {
                        player.drop(newStack, false);
                    }
                }
            }

            // 4. 拦截原版逻辑，直接返回成功
            // 这样原版代码就不会运行，防止它因为不是磁石而报错或没反应
            cir.setReturnValue(InteractionResult.sidedSuccess(level.isClientSide));
        }
        // 如果不是床，方法继续向下执行，原版磁石逻辑依然有效
    }
}

// File: mixin\villna\CreeperMixin.java
// ==================================================

package com.example.examplemod.mixin.villna;

import net.minecraft.util.Mth;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.monster.Creeper;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.Mixin;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.At;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.ModifyArg;

@Mixin(Creeper.class)
public abstract class CreeperMixin {

    @ModifyArg(
        method = "explodeCreeper",
        at = @At(
            value = "INVOKE",
            target = "Lnet/minecraft/world/level/Level;explode(" +
                     "Lnet/minecraft/world/entity/Entity;" +
                     "DDDF" +
                     "Lnet/minecraft/world/level/Level$ExplosionInteraction;" +
                     ")Lnet/minecraft/world/level/Explosion;"
        ),
        index = 4
    )
    private float examplemod$scaleExplosionByHealth(float originalRadius) {
        Creeper creeper = (Creeper) (Object) this;

        float healthPercentage = creeper.getHealth() / creeper.getMaxHealth();
        healthPercentage = Mth.clamp(healthPercentage, 0.0F, 1.0F);

        return originalRadius * healthPercentage;
    }
}

// ==================================================
// File: mixin\villna\FoodDataMixin.java
// ==================================================

package com.example.examplemod.mixin.villna;

import net.minecraft.world.entity.player.Player;
import net.minecraft.world.food.FoodData;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.Mixin;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.Unique;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.At;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.Constant;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.ModifyConstant;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.Redirect;

/**
 * Mixin to modify the FoodData class for dynamic natural regeneration.
 * This makes the healing amount scale with the player's max health.
 */
@Mixin(FoodData.class)
public abstract class FoodDataMixin {

    // 定义回血量为最大生命值的百分比。这里是5%，和原版 (1.0 / 20.0) 保持一致。
    // 你可以根据需要调整这个值。
    private static final float HEAL_PERCENTAGE_OF_MAX_HEALTH = 0.05F;

    /**
     * 重定向基于饱和度的快速治疗调用 (foodLevel >= 20)。
     * 原版代码: player.heal(f / 6.0F)，其中 f 最大为 6.0F，所以最多治疗 1.0F。
     * 我们将其修改为按比例缩放。
     * @param player 玩家实例
     * @param originalAmount 原始计算出的治疗量 (f / 6.0F)
     */
    @Redirect(
        method = "tick(Lnet/minecraft/world/entity/player/Player;)V",
        at = @At(
            value = "INVOKE",
            target = "Lnet/minecraft/world/entity/player/Player;heal(F)V",
            ordinal = 0 // 第一个 heal() 调用
        )
    )
    private void redirectSaturationHeal(Player player, float originalAmount) {
        // 计算基于最大生命值的等效治疗量
        // originalAmount 是一个 0 到 1 的系数，我们用它乘以新的基础治疗量
        float scaledHealAmount = (player.getMaxHealth() * HEAL_PERCENTAGE_OF_MAX_HEALTH) * originalAmount;

        // 调用原始的 heal 方法，传入我们计算出的新值。
        // 这将正确触发治疗事件，让“活力迸发”等效果能够生效。
        player.heal(scaledHealAmount);
    }

    @Redirect(
        method = "tick(Lnet/minecraft/world/entity/player/Player;)V",
        at = @At(
            value = "INVOKE",
            target = "Lnet/minecraft/world/entity/player/Player;heal(F)V",
            ordinal = 1 // 第二个 heal() 调用
        )
    )
    private void redirectNaturalRegenHeal(Player player, float originalAmount) {
        // 计算基于玩家最大生命值的治疗量
        float scaledHealAmount = player.getMaxHealth() * HEAL_PERCENTAGE_OF_MAX_HEALTH;

        // 调用原始的 heal 方法，传入我们计算出的新值。
        // 同样，这将确保“活力迸发”等效果能正确应用。
        player.heal(scaledHealAmount);
    }

    // 这里定义默认的新上限，你可以将其连接到你的 Config 文件
    @Unique
    private int maxFoodLevel = 40;

    /**
     * 1. 修改 add 方法中的 clamp 上限
     * 原代码: this.foodLevel = Mth.clamp(foodLevel + this.foodLevel, 0, 20);
     */
    @ModifyConstant(method = "add", constant = @Constant(intValue = 20))
    private int modifyMaxFoodInAdd(int constant) {
        return this.maxFoodLevel;
    }

    /**
     * 2. 修改 needsFood 方法的判断条件
     * 原代码: return this.foodLevel < 20;
     */
    @ModifyConstant(method = "needsFood", constant = @Constant(intValue = 20))
    private int modifyMaxFoodInNeedsFood(int constant) {
        return this.maxFoodLevel;
    }

    /**
     * 3. 修改 tick 方法中的饱和度回血判断
     * 原代码: if (flag && this.saturationLevel > 0.0F && player.isHurt() && this.foodLevel >= 20)
     * 只有当饥饿值达到满值时，饱和度才会快速回血。我们将其修改为新的最大值。
     */
    @ModifyConstant(method = "tick", constant = @Constant(intValue = 20))
    private int modifyMaxFoodInTick(int constant) {
        return this.maxFoodLevel;
    }

    /**
     * 可选：修改普通回血的阈值 (原版是 >= 18)
     * 如果你希望玩家只有在"接近满食"时才回血，应该保留 18 或修改为 Max - 2。
     * 如果你希望玩家在"半饱"状态也能回血，可以不修改。
     *
     * 这里演示如何保持"满值-2"的逻辑（例如上限40，则38以上才回血）。
     */
    @ModifyConstant(method = "tick", constant = @Constant(intValue = 18))
    private int modifyRegenThreshold(int constant) {
        // 保持原版逻辑：最大值 - 2
        return this.maxFoodLevel-4;
    }
}

package com.example.examplemod.mixin.villna;

import com.llamalad7.mixinextras.injector.ModifyReturnValue;
import net.minecraft.client.renderer.GameRenderer;
import net.minecraft.world.effect.MobEffectInstance;
import net.minecraft.world.effect.MobEffects;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.LivingEntity;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.Mixin;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.At;

@Mixin({GameRenderer.class})
public class GameRendererMixin {
    @ModifyReturnValue(
        method = {"getNightVisionScale(Lnet/minecraft/world/entity/LivingEntity;F)F"},
        at = {@At("RETURN")}
    )
    private static float getNightVisionScale(float original, LivingEntity entity, float f) {
        MobEffectInstance effect = entity.getEffect(MobEffects.NIGHT_VISION);
        if (effect != null && effect.endsWithin(240)) {
            return 1;
        } else {
            return original;
        }
    }
}

// ==================================================
// File: mixin\villna\HealOrHarmMobEffectMixin.java
// ==================================================

package com.example.examplemod.mixin.villna;

import dev.shadowsoffire.apothic_attributes.api.ALObjects;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.Entity;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.LivingEntity;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.Final;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.Mixin;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.Shadow;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.At;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.Inject;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.callback.CallbackInfo;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.callback.CallbackInfoReturnable;

import javax.annotation.Nullable;

@Mixin(targets = "net.minecraft.world.effect.HealOrHarmMobEffect")
public abstract class HealOrHarmMobEffectMixin {
    @Shadow @Final private boolean isHarm;

    @Inject(
        method = "applyInstantenousEffect(Lnet/minecraft/world/entity/Entity;Lnet/minecraft/world/entity/Entity;Lnet/minecraft/world/entity/LivingEntity;ID)V",
        at = @At("HEAD"),
        cancellable = true
    )
    private void applyHealingAttributeToInvertedInstant(@Nullable Entity source, @Nullable Entity indirectSource, LivingEntity livingEntity, int amplifier, double health, CallbackInfo ci) {
        if (livingEntity.isInvertedHealAndHarm()) {

            float healingFactor = (float) livingEntity.getAttributeValue(ALObjects.Attributes.HEALING_RECEIVED);

            if (this.isHarm) {
                float baseHeal = (float)((int)(health * (double)(4 << amplifier) + 0.5));
                float finalHeal = baseHeal * healingFactor;
                if (finalHeal > 0) {
                    livingEntity.heal(finalHeal);
                }
            } else {
                float baseDamage = (float)((int)(health * (double)(6 << amplifier) + 0.5));
                float finalDamage = baseDamage * healingFactor;
                if (finalDamage > 0) {
                    if (source == null) {
                        livingEntity.hurt(livingEntity.damageSources().magic(), finalDamage);
                    } else {
                        livingEntity.hurt(livingEntity.damageSources().indirectMagic(source, indirectSource), finalDamage);
                    }
                }
            }

            ci.cancel();
        }
    }
    @Inject(
        method = "applyEffectTick(Lnet/minecraft/world/entity/LivingEntity;I)Z",
        at = @At("HEAD"),
        cancellable = true
    )
    private void applyHealingAttributeToInvertedTick(LivingEntity livingEntity, int amplifier, CallbackInfoReturnable<Boolean> cir) {
        if (livingEntity.isInvertedHealAndHarm()) {

            float healingFactor = (float) livingEntity.getAttributeValue(ALObjects.Attributes.HEALING_RECEIVED);

            if (this.isHarm) {
                float baseHeal = (float)Math.max(4 << amplifier, 0);
                float finalHeal = baseHeal * healingFactor;
                if (finalHeal > 0) {
                    livingEntity.heal(finalHeal);
                }
            } else {
                float baseDamage = (float)(6 << amplifier);
                float finalDamage = baseDamage * healingFactor;
                if (finalDamage > 0) {
                    livingEntity.hurt(livingEntity.damageSources().magic(), finalDamage);
                }
            }
            cir.cancel();
        }
    }
}

// ==================================================
// File: mixin\villna\LootTableMixin.java
// ==================================================

package com.example.examplemod.mixin.villna;

import it.unimi.dsi.fastutil.objects.ObjectArrayList;
import net.minecraft.util.RandomSource;
import net.minecraft.world.Container;
import net.minecraft.world.item.ItemStack;
import net.minecraft.world.level.storage.loot.LootTable;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.Mixin;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.Overwrite;

import java.util.List;

@Mixin(LootTable.class)
public class LootTableMixin
{

    /**
     * @author
     * @reason
     */
    @Overwrite
    public List<Integer> getAvailableSlots(Container inventory, RandomSource random)
    {
        ObjectArrayList<Integer> objectarraylist = new ObjectArrayList<>();
        for (int i = 0; i < inventory.getContainerSize(); i++)
        {
            if (inventory.getItem(i).isEmpty())
            {
                objectarraylist.add(i);
            }
        }
        return objectarraylist;
    }

    /**
     * @author
     * @reason
     */
    @Overwrite
    public void shuffleAndSplitItems(ObjectArrayList<ItemStack> stacks, int emptySlotsCount, RandomSource random)
    {


        for(int i = 0; i < stacks.size(); i++) {
            ItemStack stackA = stacks.get(i);

            for(int j = i + 1; j < stacks.size(); j++) {
                ItemStack stackB = stacks.get(j);

                if(!ItemStack.isSameItemSameComponents(stackA, stackB)) {
                    continue;
                }

                int mergedCount = stackA.getCount() + stackB.getCount();
                int actualMergedCount = Math.min(mergedCount, stackB.getMaxStackSize());
                stackB.setCount(actualMergedCount);
                stackA.setCount(mergedCount - actualMergedCount);
            }
        }

    }
}
