// ==================================================
// File: mixin\villna\MixinPoisonMobEffect.java
// ==================================================

package com.example.examplemod.mixin.villna;

import net.minecraft.world.damagesource.DamageSource;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.LivingEntity;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.Mixin;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.At;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.Redirect;

// 使用 targets 指定包级私有的类
@Mixin(targets = "net.minecraft.world.effect.PoisonMobEffect")
public class MixinPoisonMobEffect {

    @Redirect(
            method = "applyEffectTick",
            at = @At(
                    value = "INVOKE",
                    target = "Lnet/minecraft/world/entity/LivingEntity;hurt(Lnet/minecraft/world/damagesource/DamageSource;F)Z"
            )
    )
    private boolean modifyPoisonDamage(LivingEntity entity, DamageSource source, float amount) {
        // RPG数值：最大生命值的 2%
        // 原版 Poison 的机制是生命值 > 1 才会致死，这里 hurt 依然会被原版的 if 包裹，所以不用担心致死问题
        float newAmount = entity.getMaxHealth() * 0.005F;

        // 建议：保留原版1.0F作为保底，防止打不动低血量生物
        return entity.hurt(source, Math.max(newAmount, 1.0F));
    }
}

// ==================================================
// File: mixin\villna\MixinWitherMobEffect.java
// ==================================================

package com.example.examplemod.mixin.villna;

import net.minecraft.world.damagesource.DamageSource;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.LivingEntity;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.Mixin;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.At;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.Redirect;

@Mixin(targets = "net.minecraft.world.effect.WitherMobEffect")
public class MixinWitherMobEffect {

    @Redirect(
        method = "applyEffectTick",
        at = @At(
            value = "INVOKE",
            target = "Lnet/minecraft/world/entity/LivingEntity;hurt(Lnet/minecraft/world/damagesource/DamageSource;F)Z"
        )
    )
    private boolean modifyWitherDamage(LivingEntity entity, DamageSource source, float amount) {

        float newAmount = entity.getMaxHealth() * 0.01F;
        
        return entity.hurt(source, Math.max(newAmount, 1.0F));
    }
}

// ==================================================
// File: mixin\villna\MobEffectsMixin.java
// ==================================================

package com.example.examplemod.mixin.villna;

import net.minecraft.core.Holder;
import net.minecraft.resources.ResourceLocation;
import net.minecraft.world.effect.MobEffect;
import net.minecraft.world.effect.MobEffects;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.ai.attributes.Attribute;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.ai.attributes.AttributeModifier;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.Mixin;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.At;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.Redirect;

@Mixin(MobEffects.class)
public class MobEffectsMixin {

    /**
     * 拦截 MobEffects 静态初始化期间所有的 addAttributeModifier 调用。
     * 我们通过判断 ResourceLocation 的 ID 来确定当前正在注册哪个效果。
     */
    @Redirect(
            method = "<clinit>", // 目标是静态初始化块
            at = @At(
                    value = "INVOKE",
                    target = "Lnet/minecraft/world/effect/MobEffect;addAttributeModifier(Lnet/minecraft/core/Holder;Lnet/minecraft/resources/ResourceLocation;DLnet/minecraft/world/entity/ai/attributes/AttributeModifier$Operation;)Lnet/minecraft/world/effect/MobEffect;"
            )
    )
    private static MobEffect modifyAttributeModifiers(
            MobEffect instance,
            Holder<Attribute> attribute,
            ResourceLocation id,
            double amount,
            AttributeModifier.Operation operation
    ) {
        // 判断是否是 "力量" (strength) 效果的 ID
        // 原版代码中写的是: ResourceLocation.withDefaultNamespace("effect.strength")
        if (id.getPath().equals("effect.strength")) {
            
            // 在这里修改数值和算法
            
            double newAmount = 0.125;
            
            // 示例 2: 将算法从 ADD_VALUE (直接加算) 改为 ADD_MULTIPLIED_TOTAL (总乘算，即百分比)
            // 如果改为乘算，通常数值要小一点，比如 0.5 代表 +50%
            AttributeModifier.Operation newOperation = AttributeModifier.Operation.ADD_MULTIPLIED_TOTAL; 

            // 返回修改后的调用
            return instance.addAttributeModifier(attribute, id, newAmount, newOperation);
        }

        // 判断是否是 "虚弱" (weakness)
        if (id.getPath().equals("effect.weakness")) {
            return instance.addAttributeModifier(attribute, id, -0.166, AttributeModifier.Operation.ADD_MULTIPLIED_TOTAL);
        }
        

        // 对于其他所有未修改的效果，保持原样返回
        return instance.addAttributeModifier(attribute, id, amount, operation);
    }
}

// File: mixin\villna\ModernContainerScreenMixin.java
// ==================================================

package com.example.examplemod.mixin.villna;

import com.mojang.blaze3d.systems.RenderSystem;
import net.minecraft.client.gui.GuiGraphics;
import net.minecraft.client.gui.screens.inventory.AbstractContainerScreen;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.Mixin;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.Overwrite;

@Mixin(AbstractContainerScreen.class)
public abstract class ModernContainerScreenMixin {

    /**
     * 现代化的槽位高亮
     */
    @Overwrite
    public static void renderSlotHighlight(GuiGraphics guiGraphics, int x, int y, int blitOffset, int color) {
        int highlightColor = 0xFFFFFFFF; // 纯白色高亮，不透明

        // 1. 保存当前矩阵状态
        guiGraphics.pose().pushPose();

        guiGraphics.pose().scale(0.5f, 0.5f, 1.0f);

        int sx = x * 2;
        int sy = y * 2;
        int sSize = 16 * 2;
        int thickness = 1;

        // 上边 (Top)
        guiGraphics.fill(sx, sy, sx + sSize, sy + thickness, blitOffset, highlightColor);

        // 左边 (Left)
        guiGraphics.fill(sx, sy + thickness, sx + thickness, sy + sSize - thickness, blitOffset, highlightColor);

        guiGraphics.pose().popPose();

        RenderSystem.enableBlend();
        RenderSystem.defaultBlendFunc();
    }
}

// File: mixin\villna\PlayerMixin.java
// ==================================================

package com.example.examplemod.mixin.villna;

import net.minecraft.world.damagesource.DamageSource;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.player.Player;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.Mixin;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.At;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.Inject;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.callback.CallbackInfo;

@Mixin(Player.class)
public class PlayerMixin {
    @Inject(method = "die", at = @At("TAIL"))
    private void examplemod$resetExperienceOnDeath(
            DamageSource source,
            CallbackInfo callbackInfo
    ) {
        Player player = (Player) (Object) this;

        // 只让服务端修改，避免客户端和服务端各执行一次
        if (player.level().isClientSide()) {
            return;
        }

        // 被 LivingDeathEvent 取消等情况下，不处理经验
        if (!player.isDeadOrDying()) {
            return;
        }

        // 清空当前等级经验条
        player.experienceProgress = 0.0F;

        // 等级降低一级，但不低于 0
        player.experienceLevel = Math.max(0, player.experienceLevel - 1);
    }
}

// ==================================================
// File: mixin\villna\RangedAttributeMixin.java
// ==================================================

package com.example.examplemod.mixin.villna;

import net.minecraft.world.entity.ai.attributes.RangedAttribute;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.Mixin;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.At;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.ModifyVariable;

@Mixin(RangedAttribute.class)
public class RangedAttributeMixin {

    /**
     * 这个 Mixin 会拦截 RangedAttribute 的构造函数。
     * 它在构造函数的最开始 (HEAD) 注入，修改名为 'max' 的局部变量（即构造函数的第四个参数）。
     * 这样，当游戏创建 Attributes.MAX_HEALTH 时，它的上限就从 1024 变成了 1024000。
     */
    @ModifyVariable(
            method = "<init>",      // 目标是构造函数
            at = @At("HEAD"),       // 在构造函数入口处注入，此时参数刚传入，可以修改
            ordinal = 2,            // 目标是第三个 double 类型的参数 (ordinal=0 是 defaultValue, ordinal=1 是 min)
            argsOnly = true         // 确保我们只在参数中查找变量，而不是所有局部变量
    )
    private static double modifyMaxValueInConstructor(double max) {
        // 如果原来的上限是 1024.0 (最大生命值)，我们就把它放大。
        if (
                max == 1024.0 ||
                max == 2048.0
        ) {
            return Math.min(Double.MAX_VALUE, max*100000000) ;
        }
        // 对于其他属性，保持原样
        return max;
    }
}

package com.example.examplemod.mixin.villna;

import net.minecraft.world.effect.MobEffect;
import net.minecraft.world.effect.MobEffectCategory;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.LivingEntity;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.Mixin;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.Overwrite;

@Mixin(targets = "net.minecraft.world.effect.RegenerationMobEffect")
public abstract class RegenerationMobEffectMixin extends MobEffect {

    protected RegenerationMobEffectMixin(MobEffectCategory p_296242_, int p_294288_) {
        super(p_296242_, p_294288_);
    }
    /**
     * @author
     * @reason
     */
    @Overwrite
    public boolean applyEffectTick(LivingEntity p_295924_, int p_296417_) {
        if (p_295924_.getHealth() < p_295924_.getMaxHealth()) {
            p_295924_.heal(p_295924_.getMaxHealth()*0.05f);
        }

        return true;
    }
}

package com.example.examplemod.network;

import net.minecraft.client.gui.components.Button;
import net.minecraft.client.gui.screens.inventory.InventoryScreen;
import net.minecraft.network.chat.Component;
import net.neoforged.api.distmarker.Dist;
import net.neoforged.bus.api.SubscribeEvent;
import net.neoforged.fml.common.EventBusSubscriber;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.client.event.ScreenEvent;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.network.PacketDistributor;

@EventBusSubscriber(value = Dist.CLIENT)
public class InventoryButton {
    @SubscribeEvent
    public static void onInventoryInit(ScreenEvent.Init.Post event) {
        if (!(event.getScreen() instanceof InventoryScreen screen)) {
            return;
        }

        int width = 32;
        int height = 16;

        int x = screen.getGuiLeft() + (176 - width) / 2;
        int y = screen.getGuiTop() + 168;

        event.addListener(
                Button.builder(
                                Component.translatable(
                                        "examplemod.button.open_shop"
                                ),
                                button -> PacketDistributor.sendToServer(
                                        OpenShopSelectorPayload.INSTANCE
                                )
                        )
                        .bounds(x, y, width, height)
                        .build()
        );
        event.addListener(
                Button.builder(
                                Component.translatable(
                                        "examplemod.button.sleep"
                                ),
                                button -> PacketDistributor.sendToServer(
                                        SleepButtonPayload.INSTANCE
                                )
                        )
                        .bounds(x+width, y, width, height)
                        .build()
        );
    }
}

// File: network\Network.java
// ==================================================

package com.example.examplemod.network;

import com.viscriptshop.util.ViScriptShopServerUtil;
import net.minecraft.core.BlockPos;
import net.minecraft.network.chat.Component;
import net.minecraft.server.level.ServerLevel;
import net.minecraft.server.level.ServerPlayer;
import net.neoforged.bus.api.SubscribeEvent;
import net.neoforged.fml.common.EventBusSubscriber;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.network.event.RegisterPayloadHandlersEvent;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.network.registration.PayloadRegistrar;

@EventBusSubscriber
public final class Network {
    @SubscribeEvent
    public static void registerPayloads(RegisterPayloadHandlersEvent event) {
        PayloadRegistrar registrar = event.registrar("1");
        registrar.playToServer(
                OpenShopSelectorPayload.TYPE,
                OpenShopSelectorPayload.STREAM_CODEC,
                (payload, context) -> {
                    if (context.player() instanceof ServerPlayer serverPlayer) {
                        ViScriptShopServerUtil.serverOpenShopSelector(serverPlayer);
                    }
                }
        );
        registrar.playToServer(
                SleepButtonPayload.TYPE,
                SleepButtonPayload.STREAM_CODEC,
                (payload, context) -> {
                    if (context.player() instanceof ServerPlayer player) {
                        handleSleepButton(player);
                    }
                }
        );

    }
    private static void handleSleepButton(ServerPlayer player) {
        if (!(player.level() instanceof ServerLevel level)) {
            return;
        }

        /*
         * 与原版床相同：
         * 下界、末地等 bed_works=false 的维度不能使用床。
         */
        if (!level.dimensionType().bedWorks()) {
            player.sendSystemMessage(
                    Component.translatable(
                            "examplemod.message.bed_explodes"
                    )
            );
            return;
        }

        long timeOfDay = level.getDayTime() % 24000L;

        /*
         * 原版床只能在夜间使用。
         * 12542：夜晚开始
         * 23460：夜晚结束
         */
        if (timeOfDay < 12542L || timeOfDay >= 23460L) {
            player.sendSystemMessage(
                    Component.translatable(
                            "examplemod.message.cannot_sleep_now"
                    )
            );
            return;
        }

        BlockPos respawnPos = player.blockPosition();

        /*
         * 设置玩家重生点。
         *
         * false：不是强制重生点
         * true：向玩家发送重生点设置提示
         */
        player.setRespawnPosition(
                level.dimension(),
                respawnPos,
                player.getYRot(),
                false,
                true
        );

        /*
         * 推进到第二天早晨。
         *
         * 24000：下一天的开始
         * 1000：原版睡醒后的清晨时间
         */
        long nextMorning =
                ((level.getDayTime() / 24000L) + 1L) * 24000L + 1000L;

        level.setDayTime(nextMorning);

        /*
         * 关闭背包界面，模拟上床后关闭当前界面。
         */
        player.closeContainer();

        player.sendSystemMessage(
                Component.translatable(
                        "examplemod.message.woke_up"
                )
        );
    }
}

package com.example.examplemod.network;

import com.example.examplemod.ExampleMod;
import net.minecraft.network.RegistryFriendlyByteBuf;
import net.minecraft.network.codec.StreamCodec;
import net.minecraft.network.protocol.common.custom.CustomPacketPayload;
import net.minecraft.resources.ResourceLocation;

public record OpenShopSelectorPayload() implements CustomPacketPayload {
    public static final OpenShopSelectorPayload INSTANCE =
            new OpenShopSelectorPayload();

    public static final Type<OpenShopSelectorPayload> TYPE =
            new Type<>(
                    ResourceLocation.fromNamespaceAndPath(
                            ExampleMod.MODID,
                            "open_shop_selector"
                    )
            );

    public static final StreamCodec<
            RegistryFriendlyByteBuf,
            OpenShopSelectorPayload
            > STREAM_CODEC = StreamCodec.unit(INSTANCE);

    @Override
    public Type<? extends CustomPacketPayload> type() {
        return TYPE;
    }
}

// File: registry\DataRegistryHandler.java
// ==================================================

package com.example.examplemod.registry;

import net.minecraft.core.component.DataComponentPatch;
import net.minecraft.core.component.DataComponents;
import net.minecraft.world.item.*;
import net.minecraft.world.level.block.ShulkerBoxBlock;
import net.neoforged.bus.api.EventPriority;
import net.neoforged.bus.api.SubscribeEvent;
import net.neoforged.fml.common.EventBusSubscriber;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.event.ModifyDefaultComponentsEvent;

import java.util.List;
import java.util.Set;
import java.util.function.Consumer;

import static com.example.examplemod.ExampleMod.MODID;

@EventBusSubscriber(modid = MODID)
public class DataRegistryHandler {

    private static final Consumer<DataComponentPatch.Builder> SET_MAX_STACK_64 =
            builder -> builder.set(DataComponents.MAX_STACK_SIZE, 64);

    private static final List<Class<? extends Item>> STACKABLE_TYPES = List.of(
            SaddleItem.class, MinecartItem.class, BoatItem.class, SignItem.class, HangingSignItem.class,
            BucketItem.class, SnowballItem.class, SolidBucketItem.class, MilkBucketItem.class, MobBucketItem.class,
            EggItem.class, BundleItem.class, SpyglassItem.class, BedItem.class, EnderpearlItem.class,
            PotionItem.class, SplashPotionItem.class, LingeringPotionItem.class, EnchantedBookItem.class,
            ArmorStandItem.class, BannerItem.class, BannerPatternItem.class, InstrumentItem.class, HoneyBottleItem.class
    );

    private static final Set<Item> SPECIFIC_ITEMS = Set.of(
            Items.WRITABLE_BOOK, Items.MUSHROOM_STEW, Items.CAKE,
            Items.RABBIT_STEW, Items.BEETROOT_SOUP, Items.KNOWLEDGE_BOOK
    );

    @SubscribeEvent(priority = EventPriority.LOWEST)
    public static void onModifyDefaultComponents(ModifyDefaultComponentsEvent event) {

        // 1. 指定的单个物品
        for (Item item : SPECIFIC_ITEMS) {
            // 同样确保没有被其他奇葩模组魔改出耐久
            if (!item.components().has(DataComponents.MAX_DAMAGE)) {
                event.modify(item, SET_MAX_STACK_64);
            }
        }

        // 2. 批量匹配
        event.modifyMatching(item -> {
            var components = item.components();

            // 核心熔断：原版底层铁律——凡是带耐久度的物品，绝不能堆叠，直接跳过！
            if (components.has(DataComponents.MAX_DAMAGE)) {
                return false;
            }

            // A. 潜影盒
            if (item instanceof BlockItem blockItem && blockItem.getBlock() instanceof ShulkerBoxBlock) {
                return true;
            }

            // B. 类型树匹配
            for (Class<? extends Item> type : STACKABLE_TYPES) {
                if (type.isInstance(item)) {
                    return true;
                }
            }

            // C. 唱片检测（避开单例污染）
            if (components.has(DataComponents.JUKEBOX_PLAYABLE)
                    && components.getOrDefault(DataComponents.RARITY, Rarity.COMMON) == Rarity.RARE) {
                return true;
            }

            return false;
        }, SET_MAX_STACK_64);
    }
}

package com.example.examplemod.registry;

import com.example.examplemod.item.PortableBrewingData;
import com.example.examplemod.item.PortableFurnaceData;
import net.minecraft.core.UUIDUtil;
import net.minecraft.core.component.DataComponentType;
import net.minecraft.core.registries.Registries;
import net.minecraft.resources.ResourceLocation;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.registries.DeferredHolder;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.registries.DeferredRegister;

import java.util.UUID;

public class ModDataComponents {
    public static final DeferredRegister<DataComponentType<?>> COMPONENTS =
            DeferredRegister.create(Registries.DATA_COMPONENT_TYPE, "examplemod");

    // 存储分类的 ResourceLocation（例如 "apotheosis:helmet"）
    public static final DeferredHolder<DataComponentType<?>, DataComponentType<ResourceLocation>> MIMIC_CATEGORY =
            COMPONENTS.register("mimic_cat", () -> DataComponentType.<ResourceLocation>builder()
                    .persistent(ResourceLocation.CODEC)
                    .networkSynchronized(ResourceLocation.STREAM_CODEC)
                    .build());
    public static final DeferredHolder<DataComponentType<?>, DataComponentType<UUID>> ITEM_INSTANCE_UUID =
            COMPONENTS.register("item_instance_uuid",() -> DataComponentType.<UUID>builder()
                    .persistent(UUIDUtil.CODEC)
                    .networkSynchronized(UUIDUtil.STREAM_CODEC)
                    .build());
    public static final DeferredHolder<DataComponentType<?>, DataComponentType<PortableFurnaceData>> FURNACE_DATA =
            COMPONENTS.register("furnace_data",() ->DataComponentType.<PortableFurnaceData>builder()

                    .persistent(PortableFurnaceData.CODEC)
                    .networkSynchronized(PortableFurnaceData.STREAM_CODEC)
                    .build());


    // 3. 酿造台物品与数据组件
    public static final DeferredHolder<DataComponentType<?>, DataComponentType<PortableBrewingData>> BREWING_DATA =
            COMPONENTS.register("brewing_data", () -> DataComponentType.<PortableBrewingData>builder()
                    .persistent(PortableBrewingData.CODEC)
                    .networkSynchronized(PortableBrewingData.STREAM_CODEC)
                    .build());

   }

package com.example.examplemod.registry;

import com.example.examplemod.ExampleMod;
import com.example.examplemod.spell.SummonedCaveSpider;
import net.minecraft.core.registries.Registries;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.EntityType;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.MobCategory;
import net.neoforged.bus.api.IEventBus;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.registries.DeferredHolder;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.registries.DeferredRegister;

public class ModEntities {
    public static final DeferredRegister<EntityType<?>> ENTITIES =
            DeferredRegister.create(Registries.ENTITY_TYPE, ExampleMod.MODID);

    public static final DeferredHolder<EntityType<?>, EntityType<SummonedCaveSpider>> SUMMONED_CAVE_SPIDER =
            ENTITIES.register("summoned_cave_spider", () ->
                    EntityType.Builder.<SummonedCaveSpider>of(SummonedCaveSpider::new, MobCategory.MISC)
                            .sized(0.7F, 0.5F)
                            .clientTrackingRange(8)
                            .build("summoned_cave_spider")
            );

    public static void register(IEventBus modEventBus) {
        ENTITIES.register(modEventBus);
    }
}

// ==================================================
// File: registry\WeaponReplacementModifier.java
// ==================================================

package com.example.examplemod.registry;


import com.example.examplemod.util.WeaponReplacer;
import com.mojang.serialization.MapCodec;
import com.mojang.serialization.codecs.RecordCodecBuilder;
import it.unimi.dsi.fastutil.objects.ObjectArrayList;
import net.minecraft.world.item.ItemStack;
import net.minecraft.world.level.storage.loot.LootContext;
import net.minecraft.world.level.storage.loot.predicates.LootItemCondition;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.common.loot.IGlobalLootModifier;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.common.loot.LootModifier;

public class WeaponReplacementModifier extends LootModifier {

    public static final MapCodec<WeaponReplacementModifier> CODEC = RecordCodecBuilder.mapCodec(
            inst -> codecStart(inst).apply(inst, WeaponReplacementModifier::new)
    );

    public WeaponReplacementModifier(LootItemCondition[] conditionsIn) {
        super(conditionsIn);
    }

    @Override
    protected ObjectArrayList<ItemStack> doApply(ObjectArrayList<ItemStack> generatedLoot, LootContext context) {
        for (int i = 0; i < generatedLoot.size(); i++) {
            ItemStack stack = generatedLoot.get(i);
            ItemStack replaced = WeaponReplacer.tryReplace(stack, context.getRandom());
            if (replaced != stack) {
                generatedLoot.set(i, replaced);
            }
        }
        return generatedLoot;
    }

    @Override
    public MapCodec<? extends IGlobalLootModifier> codec() {
        return CODEC;
    }
}

package com.example.examplemod.spell;

import com.example.examplemod.ExampleMod;
import io.redspace.ironsspellbooks.api.registry.SpellRegistry;
import io.redspace.ironsspellbooks.api.spells.AbstractSpell;

import net.neoforged.neoforge.registries.DeferredHolder;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.registries.DeferredRegister;

public class ModSpells {
    // 创建针对 SpellRegistry 的 DeferredRegister
    public static final DeferredRegister<AbstractSpell> SPELLS = 
            DeferredRegister.create(SpellRegistry.SPELL_REGISTRY_KEY, ExampleMod.MODID);

    // 注册法术
    public static final DeferredHolder<AbstractSpell, AbstractSpell> SUMMON_CAVE_SPIDER =
            SPELLS.register("summon_cave_spider", SummonCaveSpiderSpell::new);

    public static void register(IEventBus modEventBus) {
        SPELLS.register(modEventBus);
    }
}

// ==================================================
// File: spell\SummonCaveSpiderSpell.java
// ==================================================

package com.example.examplemod.spell;

import com.example.examplemod.registry.ModEntities;
import io.redspace.ironsspellbooks.api.config.DefaultConfig;
import io.redspace.ironsspellbooks.api.events.SpellSummonEvent;
import io.redspace.ironsspellbooks.api.magic.MagicData;
import io.redspace.ironsspellbooks.api.registry.SchoolRegistry;
import io.redspace.ironsspellbooks.api.spells.*;
import io.redspace.ironsspellbooks.api.util.Utils;
import io.redspace.ironsspellbooks.capabilities.magic.*;
import net.minecraft.network.chat.Component;
import net.minecraft.network.chat.MutableComponent;
import net.minecraft.resources.ResourceLocation;
import net.minecraft.server.level.ServerPlayer;
import net.minecraft.sounds.SoundEvent;
import net.minecraft.sounds.SoundEvents;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.LivingEntity;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.ai.attributes.Attributes;
import net.minecraft.world.level.Level;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.common.NeoForge;
import org.jetbrains.annotations.Nullable;

import java.util.List;
import java.util.Optional;

public class SummonCaveSpiderSpell extends AbstractSpell {
    private final ResourceLocation spellId = ResourceLocation.fromNamespaceAndPath("examplemod", "summon_cave_spider");
    private final DefaultConfig defaultConfig;

    public SummonCaveSpiderSpell() {
        this.defaultConfig = new DefaultConfig()
                .setMinRarity(SpellRarity.COMMON)
                .setSchoolResource(SchoolRegistry.BLOOD_RESOURCE)
                .setMaxLevel(10)
                .setCooldownSeconds(120.0)
                .build();

        this.baseManaCost = 40;
        this.manaCostPerLevel = 8;
        this.baseSpellPower = 2;
        this.spellPowerPerLevel = 1;
        this.castTime = 30;
    }

    @Override
    public ResourceLocation getSpellResource() {
        return this.spellId;
    }

    @Override
    public DefaultConfig getDefaultConfig() {
        return this.defaultConfig;
    }

    @Override
    public CastType getCastType() {
        return CastType.LONG;
    }

    @Override
    public Optional<SoundEvent> getCastStartSound() {
        return Optional.of(SoundEvents.EVOKER_PREPARE_SUMMON);
    }

    @Override
    public ICastDataSerializable getEmptyCastData() {
        return new SummonedEntitiesCastData();
    }

    @Override
    public int getRecastCount(int spellLevel, @Nullable LivingEntity entity) {
        // 如果想让玩家在召唤持续时间内通过再次按键提前驱散/重新召回，可以像北极熊法术一样设置 recast
        return 1;
    }

    @Override
    public void onRecastFinished(ServerPlayer serverPlayer, RecastInstance recastInstance, RecastResult recastResult, ICastDataSerializable castDataSerializable) {
        if (SummonManager.recastFinishedHelper(serverPlayer, recastInstance, recastResult, castDataSerializable)) {
            super.onRecastFinished(serverPlayer, recastInstance, recastResult, castDataSerializable);
        }
    }

    // ====== 数量与属性阶梯计算 ======
    public int getSpiderCount(int level) {
        if (level <= 0) return 0;
        if (level < 3) return 1;
        if (level < 5) return 2;
        if (level < 8) return 3;
        if (level < 11) return 4;
        if (level < 15) return 5;
        if (level < 19) return 6;
        return 7; // 最多 7 只
    }

    public int getBonusLevels(int level) {
        return Math.max(0, level - 19);
    }

    public float getSpiderHealth(int spellLevel, LivingEntity caster) {
        int bonus = getBonusLevels(spellLevel);
        float base = 12.0F + (spellLevel * 2.0F) + (bonus * 6.0F);
        return base * this.getEntityPowerMultiplier(caster);
    }

    public float getSpiderDamage(int spellLevel, LivingEntity caster) {
        int bonus = getBonusLevels(spellLevel);
        return this.getSpellPower(spellLevel, caster) + (bonus * 1.5F);
    }

    @Override
    public List<MutableComponent> getUniqueInfo(int spellLevel, LivingEntity caster) {
        return List.of(
                Component.literal("Count: " + getSpiderCount(spellLevel)),
                Component.translatable("ui.irons_spellbooks.hp", Utils.stringTruncation(getSpiderHealth(spellLevel, caster), 1)),
                Component.translatable("ui.irons_spellbooks.damage", Utils.stringTruncation(getSpiderDamage(spellLevel, caster), 1))
        );
    }

    @Override
    public void onCast(Level world, int spellLevel, LivingEntity entity, CastSource castSource, MagicData playerMagicData) {
        PlayerRecasts recasts = playerMagicData.getPlayerRecasts();

        if (!recasts.hasRecastForSpell(this)) {
            SummonedEntitiesCastData summonedCastData = new SummonedEntitiesCastData();
            int summonTime = 12000; // 存活 10 分钟 (12000 ticks)
            int count = getSpiderCount(spellLevel);
            float hp = getSpiderHealth(spellLevel, entity);
            float dmg = getSpiderDamage(spellLevel, entity);

            for (int i = 0; i < count; i++) {
                SummonedCaveSpider spider = new SummonedCaveSpider(ModEntities.SUMMONED_CAVE_SPIDER.get(), world);

                // 环绕生成
                double angle = (2 * Math.PI / Math.max(1, count)) * i;
                double offsetX = 1.5 * Math.cos(angle);
                double offsetZ = 1.5 * Math.sin(angle);
                spider.moveTo(entity.getX() + offsetX, entity.getY(), entity.getZ() + offsetZ, entity.getYRot(), 0.0F);

                // 设定基础属性
                if (spider.getAttribute(Attributes.MAX_HEALTH) != null) {
                    spider.getAttribute(Attributes.MAX_HEALTH).setBaseValue(hp);
                    spider.setHealth(hp);
                }
                if (spider.getAttribute(Attributes.ATTACK_DAMAGE) != null) {
                    spider.getAttribute(Attributes.ATTACK_DAMAGE).setBaseValue(dmg);
                }

                // 触发铁魔法召唤事件（支持其他附属模组拦截修改）
                SpellSummonEvent event = new SpellSummonEvent(entity, spider, this.spellId, spellLevel);
                NeoForge.EVENT_BUS.post(event);
                LivingEntity finalCreature = event.getCreature();

                world.addFreshEntity(finalCreature);
                // 接入 SummonManager 自动生命周期与目标追踪管理
                SummonManager.initSummon(entity, finalCreature, summonTime, summonedCastData);
            }

            // 注册 Recast 实例
            RecastInstance recastInstance = new RecastInstance(
                    this.getSpellId(),
                    spellLevel,
                    this.getRecastCount(spellLevel, entity),
                    summonTime,
                    castSource,
                    summonedCastData
            );
            recasts.addRecast(recastInstance, playerMagicData);
        }

        super.onCast(world, spellLevel, entity, castSource, playerMagicData);
    }
}

// ==================================================
// File: spell\SummonedCaveSpider.java
// ==================================================

package com.example.examplemod.spell;

import com.example.examplemod.registry.ModEntities;
import io.redspace.ironsspellbooks.api.spells.AbstractSpell;
import io.redspace.ironsspellbooks.api.util.Utils;
import io.redspace.ironsspellbooks.capabilities.magic.MagicManager;
import io.redspace.ironsspellbooks.capabilities.magic.SummonManager;
import io.redspace.ironsspellbooks.entity.mobs.IMagicSummon;
import io.redspace.ironsspellbooks.entity.mobs.goals.*;
import net.minecraft.core.particles.ParticleTypes;
import net.minecraft.world.damagesource.DamageSource;
import net.minecraft.world.effect.MobEffectInstance;
import net.minecraft.world.effect.MobEffects;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.Entity;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.EntityType;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.LivingEntity;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.Mob;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.ai.attributes.AttributeSupplier;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.ai.attributes.Attributes;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.ai.goal.*;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.monster.CaveSpider;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.player.Player;
import net.minecraft.world.level.Level;

public class SummonedCaveSpider extends CaveSpider implements IMagicSummon {

    public SummonedCaveSpider(EntityType<? extends SummonedCaveSpider> entityType, Level level) {
        super(entityType, level);
        this.xpReward = 0;
    }

    public SummonedCaveSpider(Level level, LivingEntity owner) {
        this(ModEntities.SUMMONED_CAVE_SPIDER.get(), level);
        SummonManager.setOwner(this, owner);
    }

    @Override
    protected void registerGoals() {
        // 动作 AI
        this.goalSelector.addGoal(1, new FloatGoal(this));
        this.goalSelector.addGoal(3, new LeapAtTargetGoal(this, 0.4F)); // 蜘蛛经典扑咬
        this.goalSelector.addGoal(4, new MeleeAttackGoal(this, 1.2, true));
        // 跟随主人：0.9速度，最远20格开始追，5格内停下，瞬移距离25格
        this.goalSelector.addGoal(6, new GenericFollowOwnerGoal(this, this::getSummoner, 1.0, 15.0F, 3.0F, false, 25.0F));
        this.goalSelector.addGoal(7, new WaterAvoidingRandomStrollGoal(this, 0.8));
        this.goalSelector.addGoal(8, new LookAtPlayerGoal(this, Player.class, 6.0F));

        // 目标仇恨 AI（完全受主人控制）
        this.targetSelector.addGoal(1, new GenericOwnerHurtByTargetGoal(this, this::getSummoner));
        this.targetSelector.addGoal(2, new GenericOwnerHurtTargetGoal(this, this::getSummoner));
        this.targetSelector.addGoal(3, new GenericCopyOwnerTargetGoal(this, this::getSummoner));
        this.targetSelector.addGoal(4, (new GenericHurtByTargetGoal(this, (entity) -> entity == this.getSummoner())).setAlertOthers());
        this.targetSelector.addGoal(5, new GenericProtectOwnerTargetGoal(this, this::getSummoner));
    }

    @Override
    public boolean doHurtTarget(Entity target) {
        // 采用法术伤害源（受法强加成，触发主人击杀与法术伤害监听）
        AbstractSpell spell = ModSpells.SUMMON_CAVE_SPIDER.get();
        boolean flag = Utils.doMeleeAttack(this, target, spell.getDamageSource(this, this.getSummoner()));
        if (flag && target instanceof LivingEntity livingTarget) {
            // 继承洞穴蜘蛛特有的毒素攻击
            livingTarget.addEffect(new MobEffectInstance(MobEffects.POISON, 140, 0), this);
        }
        return flag;
    }

    @Override
    public boolean isAlliedTo(Entity other) {
        return super.isAlliedTo(other) || this.isAlliedHelper(other);
    }

    @Override
    public boolean hurt(DamageSource source, float amount) {
        return this.shouldIgnoreDamage(source) ? false : super.hurt(source, amount);
    }

    @Override
    public void die(DamageSource damageSource) {
        this.onDeathHelper();
        super.die(damageSource);
    }

    @Override
    public void onRemovedFromLevel() {
        this.onRemovedHelper(this);
        super.onRemovedFromLevel();
    }

    @Override
    public void onUnSummon() {
        if (!this.level().isClientSide) {
            MagicManager.spawnParticles(this.level(), ParticleTypes.POOF, this.getX(), this.getY() + 0.2, this.getZ(), 15, 0.3, 0.3, 0.3, 0.02, false);
            this.setRemoved(RemovalReason.DISCARDED);
        }
    }

    // 默认基础属性（会被法术等级覆盖）
    public static AttributeSupplier.Builder createAttributes() {
        return Mob.createMobAttributes()
                .add(Attributes.MAX_HEALTH, 12.0)
                .add(Attributes.MOVEMENT_SPEED, 0.35)
                .add(Attributes.ATTACK_DAMAGE, 2.0)
                .add(Attributes.FOLLOW_RANGE, 20.0);
    }
}

// ==================================================
// File: tool\GenerateSpellTraitDataMap.java
// ==================================================

package com.example.examplemod.tool;

import java.io.IOException;
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.nio.file.Files;
import java.nio.file.Path;
import java.nio.file.Paths;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;

public class GenerateSpellTraitDataMap {

    private static final Path DEBUG_FILE =
            Paths.get("run", "spelltrait_debug.txt").toAbsolutePath();

    /**
     * data_maps 目录规则（NeoForge 1.21）：
     *   data/<datapack_namespace>/data_maps/<registry_namespace>/<registry_path>/<任意名>.json
     *
     * LHTraits.DATA 挂的 registry = l2hostility:trait
     * → data/examplemod/data_maps/l2hostility/trait/<name>.json
     */
    private static final Path OUTPUT_DIR =
            Paths.get("src", "main", "resources", "data", "examplemod",
                    "data_maps", "l2hostility", "trait").toAbsolutePath();

    private static final String SUFFIX = "_spelltrait";

    // ============ 每个 trait 的默认值，自己调 ============
    private static final int COST      = 30;   // 选择这个 trait 消耗的等级
    private static final int WEIGHT    = 100;  // 随机权重，越大越常见
    private static final int MAX_RANK  = 5;    // 最大等级（traitLevel 上限）
    private static final int MIN_LEVEL = 20;   // 怪物最低等级才能出这个 trait
    // ====================================================

    public static void main(String[] args) throws IOException {

        if (!Files.exists(DEBUG_FILE)) {
            System.err.println("[Gen] 找不到 " + DEBUG_FILE);
            return;
        }
        Files.createDirectories(OUTPUT_DIR);

        // 1) 从 debug 文件读 item id（每个 id 同时就是 trait 的 id）
        List<String> traitIds = new ArrayList<>();
        for (String line : Files.readAllLines(DEBUG_FILE, StandardCharsets.UTF_8)) {
            int i = line.indexOf("[OK-REG] 注册成功: ");
            if (i < 0) continue;
            String id = line.substring(i + "[OK-REG] 注册成功: ".length()).trim();
            if (id.startsWith("examplemod:") && id.endsWith(SUFFIX)) {
                traitIds.add(id);
            }
        }
        System.out.println("[Gen] 读到 " + traitIds.size() + " 个 trait id");

        // 2) 拼一个包含全部条目的 datapack JSON
        StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
        sb.append("{\n");
        sb.append("  \"replace\": false,\n");
        sb.append("  \"values\": {\n");

        for (int i = 0; i < traitIds.size(); i++) {
            sb.append("    \"").append(traitIds.get(i)).append("\": {\n");
            sb.append("      \"cost\": ").append(COST).append(",\n");
            sb.append("      \"weight\": ").append(WEIGHT).append(",\n");
            sb.append("      \"max_rank\": ").append(MAX_RANK).append(",\n");
            sb.append("      \"min_level\": ").append(MIN_LEVEL).append("\n");
            sb.append("    }");
            if (i < traitIds.size() - 1) sb.append(",");
            sb.append("\n");
        }

        sb.append("  }\n");
        sb.append("}\n");

        Path out = OUTPUT_DIR.resolve("spelltrait.json");
        Files.writeString(out, sb.toString(), StandardCharsets.UTF_8);

        System.out.println("[Gen] 生成 " + traitIds.size() + " 个 TraitConfig");
        System.out.println("[Gen] 输出: " + out);
    }
}

// ==================================================
// File: tool\GenerateSpellTraitModels.java
// ==================================================

package com.example.examplemod.tool;

import java.io.IOException;
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.nio.file.Files;
import java.nio.file.Path;
import java.nio.file.Paths;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;

public class GenerateSpellTraitModels {

    private static final Path DEBUG_FILE =
            Paths.get("run", "spelltrait_debug.txt").toAbsolutePath();

    private static final Path OUTPUT_DIR =
            Paths.get("src", "main", "resources", "assets", "examplemod", "models", "item")
                    .toAbsolutePath();

    private static final String SUFFIX = "_spelltrait";

    /** 你自己的模组 ID（模型/贴图都放这个命名空间下） */
    private static final String MODID = "examplemod";

    /**
     * 纹理复制脚本 TextureCopier 的输出结构：
     *   assets/examplemod/textures/item/<modkeyword>/xxx.png
     * 这里 modkeyword 就是下面 KNOWN_NAMESPACES 里的小写短名。
     *
     * 所以模型里的引用为：
     *   layer0 = examplemod:item/l2hostility/bg
     *   layer1 = examplemod:item/<ns>/<path>
     *
     * 若你当时把 TextureCopier 的子目录关掉了（平铺），
     * 就把下面的 USE_SUBDIR 改成 false。
     */
    private static final boolean USE_SUBDIR = true;

    /** TextureCopier 复制 bg.png 时用的子目录名 */
    private static final String BG_SUBDIR = "l2hostility";
    /** bg 的原始文件名（不含 .png） */
    private static final String BG_NAME = "bg";

    /**
     * 已知法术 namespace。它们的共同点是含下划线，
     * 所以不能靠"第一个下划线"来拆 ns/path。
     * 以后加新 mod 就在这里追加。
     */
    private static final List<String> KNOWN_NAMESPACES = List.of(
            "irons_spellbooks",
            "iss_magicfromtheeast"
    );

    public static void main(String[] args) throws IOException {

        if (!Files.exists(DEBUG_FILE)) {
            System.err.println("[Gen] 找不到 " + DEBUG_FILE);
            return;
        }

        Files.createDirectories(OUTPUT_DIR);

        // ============ 1) 从 debug 文件提取 item id ============
        List<String> itemIds = new ArrayList<>();
        for (String line : Files.readAllLines(DEBUG_FILE, StandardCharsets.UTF_8)) {
            int i = line.indexOf("[OK-REG] 注册成功: ");
            if (i < 0) continue;
            String id = line.substring(i + "[OK-REG] 注册成功: ".length()).trim();
            if (id.startsWith(MODID + ":") && id.endsWith(SUFFIX)) {
                itemIds.add(id);
            }
        }
        System.out.println("[Gen] 读到 " + itemIds.size() + " 个 item id");

        // ============ 2) 逐个生成模型 JSON ============
        int ok = 0, fail = 0;

        // layer0：bg
        String bgRef = USE_SUBDIR
                ? MODID + ":item/" + BG_SUBDIR + "/" + BG_NAME
                : MODID + ":item/" + BG_NAME;

        for (String itemId : itemIds) {
            // examplemod:irons_spellbooks_fireball_spelltrait
            // → rest = irons_spellbooks_fireball
            String rest = itemId.substring((MODID + ":").length());
            rest = rest.substring(0, rest.length() - SUFFIX.length());

            // 用已知 namespace 前缀匹配，取最长的那个（避免前缀互相包含的歧义）
            String ns = null;
            for (String k : KNOWN_NAMESPACES) {
                if (rest.startsWith(k + "_")) {
                    if (ns == null || k.length() > ns.length()) {
                        ns = k;
                    }
                }
            }
            if (ns == null) {
                System.err.println("[Gen] 未知 namespace: " + itemId);
                fail++;
                continue;
            }
            String path = rest.substring(ns.length() + 1);
            if (path.isEmpty()) {
                System.err.println("[Gen] path 为空: " + itemId);
                fail++;
                continue;
            }

            String itemName = ns + "_" + path + SUFFIX;

            // layer1：指向复制到 examplemod 下的贴图
            String layer1 = USE_SUBDIR
                    ? MODID + ":item/" + ns + "/" + path
                    : MODID + ":item/" + path;

            String json = "{\n"
                    + "  \"parent\": \"minecraft:item/generated\",\n"
                    + "  \"textures\": {\n"
                    + "    \"layer0\": \"" + bgRef + "\",\n"
                    + "    \"layer1\": \"" + layer1 + "\"\n"
                    + "  },\n"
                    + "  \"render_type\": \"minecraft:translucent\"\n"
                    + "}\n";

            Path out = OUTPUT_DIR.resolve(itemName + ".json");
            Files.writeString(out, json, StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
            ok++;
        }

        System.out.println("[Gen] 完成：成功 " + ok + "，失败 " + fail);
        System.out.println("[Gen] 输出目录: " + OUTPUT_DIR);
        System.out.println("[Gen] layer0 = " + bgRef);
        System.out.println("[Gen] layer1 示例 = " + (USE_SUBDIR
                ? MODID + ":item/<ns>/<path>"
                : MODID + ":item/<path>"));
    }
}

// ==================================================
// File: tool\ModpackDataDumper.java
// ==================================================

package com.example.examplemod.tool;

import com.google.gson.JsonArray;
import com.google.gson.JsonElement;
import com.google.gson.JsonParser;

import java.io.File;
import java.io.FileWriter;
import java.io.InputStreamReader;
import java.io.PrintWriter;
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.util.Collection;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.TreeMap;
import java.util.TreeSet;
import java.util.jar.JarFile;

public class ModpackDataDumper {

    // 你的 PrismLauncher mods 路径
    private static final String MODS_PATH = "C:/Users/lyj/AppData/Roaming/PrismLauncher/instances/1.21.1/minecraft/mods";
    private static final File OUT_DIR = new File("loot_foundation");

    public static void main(String[] args) {
        File modsDir = new File(MODS_PATH);
        if (!modsDir.exists()) {
            System.err.println("错误：找不到 mods 目录，请检查路径: " + MODS_PATH);
            return;
        }

        OUT_DIR.mkdirs();
        System.out.println(">>> 开始全量抽取 220+ 模组的战利品与装备地基数据...");

        // 1. 战利品表收集
        TreeSet<String> chestTables = new TreeSet<>();
        TreeSet<String> entityTables = new TreeSet<>();
        TreeSet<String> otherLootTables = new TreeSet<>();

        // 2. Curios 槽位映射：slotName -> Set<itemId>
        Map<String, TreeSet<String>> curiosSlots = new TreeMap<>();

        // 3. 核心 RPG 道具按类收集
        TreeSet<String> weapons = new TreeSet<>();
        TreeSet<String> magicScrolls = new TreeSet<>();
        TreeSet<String> gemsAndMats = new TreeSet<>();
        TreeSet<String> artifacts = new TreeSet<>();

        // 4. 全量物品名册：modId -> Set<itemId>
        Map<String, TreeSet<String>> allModItems = new TreeMap<>();

        File[] jars = modsDir.listFiles((d, name) -> name.endsWith(".jar"));
        if (jars == null) return;

        for (File jar : jars) {
            try (JarFile jarFile = new JarFile(jar)) {
                jarFile.stream().forEach(entry -> {
                    String name = entry.getName();

                    // ==========================================
                    // A. 抓取所有战利品表 (Loot Tables)
                    // ==========================================
                    if (name.startsWith("data/") && (name.contains("/loot_table/") || name.contains("/loot_tables/")) && name.endsWith(".json")) {
                        String id = parseResourceLocation(name, "loot_tables?");
                        if (id != null) {
                            if (id.contains("chests/")) {
                                chestTables.add(id);
                            } else if (id.contains("entities/")) {
                                entityTables.add(id);
                            } else {
                                otherLootTables.add(id);
                            }
                        }
                    }

                    // ==========================================
                    // B. 抓取 Curios 槽位与饰品映射 (Curios Tags)
                    // ==========================================
                    if (name.startsWith("data/") && name.matches("^data/[^/]+/tags/items?/curios/.*\\.json$")) {
                        String slotName = name.substring(name.lastIndexOf('/') + 1, name.lastIndexOf('.'));
                        try (var reader = new InputStreamReader(jarFile.getInputStream(entry), StandardCharsets.UTF_8)) {
                            JsonElement json = JsonParser.parseReader(reader);
                            if (json.isJsonObject() && json.getAsJsonObject().has("values")) {
                                JsonArray vals = json.getAsJsonObject().getAsJsonArray("values");
                                for (JsonElement v : vals) {
                                    String item = v.getAsString();
                                    if (!item.startsWith("#")) { // 过滤嵌套tag
                                        curiosSlots.computeIfAbsent(slotName, k -> new TreeSet<>()).add(item);
                                    }
                                }
                            }
                        } catch (Exception ignored) {}
                    }

                    // ==========================================
                    // C. 抓取全量物品名册与 RPG 核心道具 (Item Models)
                    // ==========================================
                    if (name.startsWith("assets/") && name.contains("/models/item/") && name.endsWith(".json")) {
                        String[] parts = name.split("/");
                        if (parts.length >= 5) {
                            String namespace = parts[1];
                            String itemPath = parts[4].substring(0, parts[4].length() - 5);
                            String itemId = namespace + ":" + itemPath;

                            allModItems.computeIfAbsent(namespace, k -> new TreeSet<>()).add(itemId);

                            // 武器类识别 (Simply Swords, Cataclysm, Miracle, Silent Gear)
                            if (namespace.equals("simplyswords") || namespace.equals("weapons_of_miracle") 
                                    || (namespace.equals("cataclysm") && itemId.matches(".*(sword|blade|axe|spear|hammer|bow|shield|claymore).*"))) {
                                weapons.add(itemId);
                            }

                            // 魔法、卷轴与法典 (Iron's Spells, Apprentice's Codex)
                            if ((namespace.equals("irons_spellbooks") || namespace.equals("apprentices_codex")) 
                                    && itemId.matches(".*(scroll|spell_book|ink|tome|focus|staff).*")) {
                                magicScrolls.add(itemId);
                            }

                            // 宝石、词缀材料与强化石 (Apotheosis, Celestial Forge, Iron's Jewelry)
                            if ((namespace.equals("apotheosis") && itemId.matches(".*(gem|scrap|dust|tome).*"))
                                    || (namespace.equals("celestial_forge") && itemId.matches(".*(reinforce|stone|material).*"))
                                    || namespace.equals("irons_jewelry")) {
                                gemsAndMats.add(itemId);
                            }

                            // 背包道具、圣遗物与随身工具 (Reliquary, Waystones, Artifacts)
                            if (namespace.equals("reliquary") || namespace.equals("waystones") || namespace.equals("artifacts")) {
                                artifacts.add(itemId);
                            }
                        }
                    }
                });
            } catch (Exception ignored) {}
        }

        // ==========================================
        // D. 统一写入文件
        // ==========================================
        writeToFile("01_chests_loot_tables.txt", chestTables);
        writeToFile("02_entity_boss_loot_tables.txt", entityTables);
        writeToFile("03_other_loot_tables.txt", otherLootTables);

        // Curios 分槽位输出
        try (PrintWriter pw = new PrintWriter(new FileWriter(new File(OUT_DIR, "04_curios_by_slot.txt")))) {
            curiosSlots.forEach((slot, items) -> {
                pw.println("========== 槽位: [" + slot + "] (" + items.size() + " 件) ==========");
                items.forEach(pw::println);
                pw.println();
            });
        } catch (Exception e) { e.printStackTrace(); }

        // RPG 核心支柱输出
        writeToFile("05_weapons.txt", weapons);
        writeToFile("06_magic_scrolls.txt", magicScrolls);
        writeToFile("07_gems_and_reinforce_mats.txt", gemsAndMats);
        writeToFile("08_inventory_artifacts.txt", artifacts);

        // 全量名册输出（按 Mod 分组）
        try (PrintWriter pw = new PrintWriter(new FileWriter(new File(OUT_DIR, "09_all_items_catalog.txt")))) {
            allModItems.forEach((mod, items) -> {
                pw.println("========== MOD: " + mod + " (" + items.size() + " 个物品) ==========");
                items.forEach(pw::println);
                pw.println();
            });
        } catch (Exception e) { e.printStackTrace(); }

        System.out.println(">>> 导出大功告成！文件均已保存至: " + OUT_DIR.getAbsolutePath());
    }

    private static String parseResourceLocation(String fullPath, String regex) {
        String[] parts = fullPath.split("/");
        if (parts.length >= 4) {
            String namespace = parts[1];
            String sub = fullPath.replaceFirst("^data/[^/]+/" + regex + "/", "");
            return namespace + ":" + sub.substring(0, sub.length() - 5);
        }
        return null;
    }

    private static void writeToFile(String filename, Collection<String> lines) {
        try (PrintWriter pw = new PrintWriter(new FileWriter(new File(OUT_DIR, filename)))) {
            lines.forEach(pw::println);
        } catch (Exception e) { e.printStackTrace(); }
    }
}

// ==================================================
// File: tool\OfflineLootTableDumper.java
// ==================================================

package com.example.examplemod.tool;

import java.io.File;
import java.io.FileWriter;
import java.io.PrintWriter;
import java.util.TreeSet;
import java.util.jar.JarFile;

public class OfflineLootTableDumper {
    public static void main(String[] args) {
        // 你的 mods 路径
        File modsDir = new File("C:/Users/lyj/AppData/Roaming/PrismLauncher/instances/1.21.1/minecraft/mods");
        File outFile = new File("all_chests.txt");

        TreeSet<String> chestTables = new TreeSet<>();
        File[] jars = modsDir.listFiles((dir, name) -> name.endsWith(".jar"));

        if (jars != null) {
            for (File jar : jars) {
                try (JarFile jarFile = new JarFile(jar)) {
                    jarFile.stream().forEach(entry -> {
                        String name = entry.getName();
                        // 匹配 data/<namespace>/loot_table(s)/.../chests/...json
                        if (name.startsWith("data/") && (name.contains("/loot_table/") || name.contains("/loot_tables/"))
                                && name.contains("/chests/") && name.endsWith(".json")) {

                            String[] parts = name.split("/");
                            if (parts.length >= 4) {
                                String namespace = parts[1];
                                // 去掉前缀和 .json 后缀，还原成标准 ResourceLocation
                                String sub = name.replaceFirst("^data/[^/]+/loot_tables?/", "");
                                sub = sub.substring(0, sub.length() - 5);

                                chestTables.add(namespace + ":" + sub);
                            }
                        }
                    });
                } catch (Exception ignored) {
                    // 自动跳过无效或加壳文件
                }
            }
        }

        try (PrintWriter writer = new PrintWriter(new FileWriter(outFile))) {
            for (String table : chestTables) {
                writer.println(table);
            }
            System.out.println("提取完成！共找到 " + chestTables.size() + " 个箱子战利品表，结果已保存至: " + outFile.getAbsolutePath());
        } catch (Exception e) {
            e.printStackTrace();
        }
    }
}

// ==================================================
// File: tool\SpellDataExporter.java
// ==================================================

package com.example.examplemod.tool;

import io.redspace.ironsspellbooks.api.registry.SpellRegistry;
import io.redspace.ironsspellbooks.api.spells.AbstractSpell;
import io.redspace.ironsspellbooks.api.spells.CastType;
import net.minecraft.commands.Commands;
import net.minecraft.core.Registry;
import net.minecraft.network.chat.Component;
import net.neoforged.bus.api.SubscribeEvent;
import net.neoforged.fml.common.EventBusSubscriber;
import net.neoforged.fml.loading.FMLPaths;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.event.RegisterCommandsEvent;

import java.io.BufferedWriter;
import java.io.File;
import java.io.FileWriter;
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.util.*;
import java.util.stream.Collectors;

@EventBusSubscriber(modid = "examplemod")
public class SpellDataExporter {

    @SubscribeEvent
    public static void onRegisterCommands(RegisterCommandsEvent event) {
        event.getDispatcher().register(
                Commands.literal("spell_dump")
                        .requires(source -> source.hasPermission(0))
                        .executes(ctx -> {
                            try {
                                exportSpellList();
                                ctx.getSource().sendSuccess(() -> Component.literal(
                                        "§a[铁魔法法术导出成功！]§f 文件：.minecraft/spell_dump/00_铁魔法法术大表.md"
                                ), false);
                            } catch (Exception e) {
                                ctx.getSource().sendFailure(Component.literal("§c导出失败: " + e.getMessage()));
                                e.printStackTrace();
                            }
                            return 1;
                        })
        );
    }

    public static void exportSpellList() throws Exception {
        Registry<AbstractSpell> registry = SpellRegistry.REGISTRY;
        if (registry == null) throw new IllegalStateException("SpellRegistry.REGISTRY 为 null");

        File dir = FMLPaths.GAMEDIR.get().resolve("spell_dump").toFile();
        if (!dir.exists()) dir.mkdirs();
        File outputFile = new File(dir, "00_铁魔法法术大表.md");

        // 按学派分组
        Map<String, List<AbstractSpell>> bySchool = new TreeMap<>();
        for (AbstractSpell spell : registry) {
            if (spell == null) continue;
            try { if (!spell.isEnabled()) continue; } catch (Throwable t) { continue; }
            String school;
            try {
                school = spell.getSchoolType().getDisplayName().getString();
                if (school.startsWith("school.")) school = "(未本地化) " + school;
            } catch (Throwable t) {
                school = "Unknown";
            }
            bySchool.computeIfAbsent(school, k -> new ArrayList<>()).add(spell);
        }

        try (BufferedWriter w = new BufferedWriter(new FileWriter(outputFile, StandardCharsets.UTF_8))) {
            w.write("# 铁魔法法术大表\n\n");
            w.write("> 描述来源：`spell.<ns>.<name>.guide` 键，从 en_us / zh_cn 任一 lang 读取。\n");
            w.write("> 若某法术无 `.guide` 键，描述显示为 `-`。\n");
            w.write("> **白名单用 ID 列**（反引号内即 ID）。\n\n");
            w.write("> 总法术数：" + registry.size() + "\n\n");

            // 学派索引
            w.write("## 📖 学派索引\n\n");
            for (String school : bySchool.keySet()) {
                w.write("- **" + school + "** （" + bySchool.get(school).size() + " 个）\n");
            }
            w.write("\n---\n\n");

            // 明细表
            for (Map.Entry<String, List<AbstractSpell>> entry : bySchool.entrySet()) {
                String school = entry.getKey();
                List<AbstractSpell> spells = entry.getValue();
                spells.sort(Comparator.comparing(AbstractSpell::getSpellId));

                w.write("## 🔮 " + school + "（" + spells.size() + " 个）\n\n");
                w.write("| 法术名 | ID | 施法类型 | 最高等级 | 基础蓝耗 | 描述 |\n");
                w.write("| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |\n");

                for (AbstractSpell spell : spells) {
                    String id;
                    try { id = spell.getSpellId(); } catch (Throwable t) { id = "?"; }

                    String nameKey = "";
                    try { nameKey = spell.getComponentId(); } catch (Throwable ignored) {}

                    // ⭐ 名称：从 lang 读（en_us 或 zh_cn）
                    String name = nameKey.isEmpty() ? "?" : Component.translatable(nameKey).getString();
                    if (name.equals(nameKey)) name = "⚠️ " + nameKey;  // 没翻译

                    // ⭐ 描述：nameKey + ".guide"
                    String desc = "-";
                    if (!nameKey.isEmpty()) {
                        String guideKey = nameKey + ".guide";
                        String guide = Component.translatable(guideKey).getString();
                        if (!guide.equals(guideKey)) {
                            desc = clean(guide);
                        }
                    }

                    String castType = "?";
                    try {
                        CastType ct = spell.getCastType();
                        if (ct != null) castType = ct.name();
                    } catch (Throwable ignored) {}

                    int maxLevel = 1;
                    try { maxLevel = spell.getMaxLevel(); } catch (Throwable ignored) {}

                    int manaCost = 0;
                    try { manaCost = spell.getManaCost(1); } catch (Throwable ignored) {}

                    w.write("| " + clean(name) + " | `" + id + "` | " + castType + " | "
                            + maxLevel + " | " + manaCost + " | " + desc + " |\n");
                }
                w.write("\n---\n\n");
            }

            // 纯 ID 块
            w.write("## 📋 全部法术 ID（每行一个，直接复制）\n\n```\n");
            List<String> ids = registry.stream()
                    .filter(Objects::nonNull)
                    .filter(s -> { try { return s.isEnabled(); } catch (Throwable t) { return false; } })
                    .map(AbstractSpell::getSpellId)
                    .sorted().collect(Collectors.toList());
            for (String id : ids) { w.write(id); w.write("\n"); }
            w.write("```\n\n");

            // .guide 键块
            w.write("## 🌐 全部 .guide 描述键\n\n```\n");
            List<String> guides = registry.stream()
                    .filter(Objects::nonNull)
                    .filter(s -> { try { return s.isEnabled(); } catch (Throwable t) { return false; } })
                    .sorted(Comparator.comparing(AbstractSpell::getSpellId))
                    .map(s -> {
                        try { return s.getComponentId() + ".guide"; }
                        catch (Throwable t) { return s.getSpellId() + " -> (失败)"; }
                    }).collect(Collectors.toList());
            for (String g : guides) { w.write(g); w.write("\n"); }
            w.write("```\n");
        }
    }

    private static String clean(String input) {
        if (input == null) return "";
        return input.replace("\n", " ").replace("\r", " ").replace("|", "\\|");
    }
}

// ==================================================
// File: tool\TextureCopier.java
// ==================================================

package com.example.examplemod.tool;

import java.io.IOException;
import java.io.InputStream;
import java.nio.file.Files;
import java.nio.file.Path;
import java.nio.file.Paths;
import java.nio.file.StandardCopyOption;
import java.util.*;
import java.util.jar.JarEntry;
import java.util.jar.JarFile;
import java.util.stream.Stream;

/**
 * 从 mod jar 中提取纹理，复制到本模组的资源目录。
 *
 * 运行方式（JDK 11+，单文件直接运行）：
 *     java TextureCopier.java
 * 或先编译再运行：
 *     javac TextureCopier.java && java TextureCopier
 *
 * 请在项目根目录（有 src/ 那一层）运行，否则 OUTPUT_DIR 会落到别处。
 */
public class TextureCopier {

    // ==================== 可配置区 ====================

    /** 你自己的模组 ID */
    private static final String TARGET_MODID = "examplemod";

    /** 输出目录：<项目根>/src/main/resources/assets/<modid>/textures/item */
    private static final Path OUTPUT_DIR = Paths.get(
            "src", "main", "resources", "assets",
            TARGET_MODID, "textures", "item"
    );

    /** 搜索 mod jar 的目录（全部会扫描，去重后使用） */
    private static final List<Path> MODS_SEARCH_PATHS = List.of(
            Paths.get("C:", "Users", "lyj", "AppData", "Roaming",
                    "PrismLauncher", "instances", "hostility", "minecraft", "mods"),
            Paths.get("run", "mods"),
            Paths.get("mods")
    );

    /**
     * 提取规则：
     *   key   = mod jar 文件名里包含的关键词（小写匹配）
     *   value = jar 内部路径匹配规则列表：
     *           - 以 "/" 结尾   => 前缀匹配（该目录下所有 png）
     *           - 以 "~" 开头   => 路径片段匹配（jar 内部路径包含该片段）
     *           - 其他          => 精确匹配单个文件
     *
     *   注意：多个模组命中的文件会被复制到同一个 textures/item 目录，
     *        若有同名文件会互相覆盖。可在 copyEntry 里按模组名建子目录。
     */
    private static final Map<String, List<String>> EXTRACT_RULES = new LinkedHashMap<>();

    static {
        // L2 Hostility 的背景图
        EXTRACT_RULES.put("l2hostility",
                List.of("assets/l2hostility/textures/item/bg.png"));

        // Iron's Spellbooks：所有 spell_icons 下的 png（无论在哪一层）
        EXTRACT_RULES.put("irons_spellbooks",
                List.of("~spell_icons/"));

        // ISS: Magic From The East：同上
        EXTRACT_RULES.put("iss_magicfromtheeast",
                List.of("~spell_icons/"));
    }

    // ==================== 主逻辑 ====================

    public static void main(String[] args) throws IOException {
        Files.createDirectories(OUTPUT_DIR);
        System.out.println("输出目录: " + OUTPUT_DIR.toAbsolutePath());

        List<Path> jars = findModJars();
        if (jars.isEmpty()) {
            System.err.println("未找到任何 mod jar，请检查 MODS_SEARCH_PATHS。");
            return;
        }

        System.out.println("找到 " + jars.size() + " 个 jar：");
        for (Path p : jars) System.out.println("   " + p);

        int total = 0;
        for (Path jarPath : jars) {
            String lower = jarPath.getFileName().toString().toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT);
            for (var rule : EXTRACT_RULES.entrySet()) {
                if (lower.contains(rule.getKey())) {
                    System.out.println("\n>> 处理 " + jarPath.getFileName());
                    total += extractFromJar(jarPath, rule.getKey(), rule.getValue());
                }
            }
        }

        System.out.println("\n全部完成，共复制 " + total + " 个文件到 " + OUTPUT_DIR);
    }

    private static List<Path> findModJars() throws IOException {
        List<Path> out = new ArrayList<>();
        Set<String> seen = new HashSet<>();
        for (Path dir : MODS_SEARCH_PATHS) {
            if (!Files.isDirectory(dir)) continue;
            try (Stream<Path> s = Files.list(dir)) {
                for (Path p : s.toList()) {
                    if (!p.toString().toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT).endsWith(".jar")) continue;
                    String key = p.toAbsolutePath().normalize().toString();
                    if (seen.add(key)) out.add(p);
                }
            }
        }
        return out;
    }

    private static int extractFromJar(Path jarPath, String modKeyword, List<String> rules) throws IOException {
        int count = 0;
        try (JarFile jar = new JarFile(jarPath.toFile())) {
            Enumeration<JarEntry> entries = jar.entries();
            while (entries.hasMoreElements()) {
                JarEntry e = entries.nextElement();
                String entryName = e.getName();
                if (e.isDirectory() || !entryName.toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT).endsWith(".png")) continue;

                for (String rule : rules) {
                    if (matches(entryName, rule)) {
                        copyEntry(jar, e, entryName, modKeyword);
                        count++;
                        break;
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        if (count == 0) System.out.println("   （未找到匹配文件）");
        return count;
    }

    private static boolean matches(String entryName, String rule) {
        if (rule.startsWith("~")) {
            return entryName.contains(rule.substring(1));
        }
        if (rule.endsWith("/")) {
            return entryName.startsWith(rule);
        }
        return entryName.equals(rule);
    }

    private static void copyEntry(JarFile jar, JarEntry entry, String entryName, String modKeyword) throws IOException {
        String fileName = entryName.substring(entryName.lastIndexOf('/') + 1);

        // 若两个模组有同名图标，按模组名建子目录避免覆盖。
        // 想去掉子目录，改成：
        //     Path target = OUTPUT_DIR.resolve(fileName);
        Path target = OUTPUT_DIR.resolve(modKeyword).resolve(fileName);
        Files.createDirectories(target.getParent());

        try (InputStream in = jar.getInputStream(entry)) {
            Files.copy(in, target, StandardCopyOption.REPLACE_EXISTING);
        }
        System.out.println("   复制: " + entryName + "  ->  " + target.getFileName());
    }
}

// ==================================================
// File: tooltip\BuiltinTooltipProviders.java
// ==================================================

package com.example.examplemod.tooltip;

import com.example.examplemod.Config;
import com.mojang.logging.LogUtils;
import net.minecraft.ChatFormatting;
import net.minecraft.advancements.critereon.BlockPredicate;
import net.minecraft.core.BlockPos;
import net.minecraft.core.Holder;
import net.minecraft.core.HolderLookup;
import net.minecraft.core.component.DataComponents;
import net.minecraft.core.registries.BuiltInRegistries;
import net.minecraft.nbt.CompoundTag;
import net.minecraft.network.chat.Component;
import net.minecraft.network.chat.MutableComponent;
import net.minecraft.resources.ResourceLocation;
import net.minecraft.world.LockCode;
import net.minecraft.world.effect.MobEffectInstance;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.EntityType;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.LivingEntity;
import net.minecraft.world.food.FoodProperties;
import net.minecraft.world.item.*;
import net.minecraft.world.item.alchemy.PotionContents;
import net.minecraft.world.item.armortrim.ArmorTrim;
import net.minecraft.world.item.component.*;
import net.minecraft.world.item.enchantment.Enchantment;
import net.minecraft.world.item.enchantment.ItemEnchantments;
import net.minecraft.world.level.block.Block;
import net.minecraft.world.level.block.ComposterBlock;
import net.minecraft.world.level.block.entity.BannerPatternLayers;
import net.minecraft.world.level.block.entity.BeehiveBlockEntity.Occupant;
import net.minecraft.world.level.block.entity.PotDecorations;
import net.minecraft.world.level.saveddata.maps.MapId;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.registries.datamaps.builtin.Compostable;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.registries.datamaps.builtin.NeoForgeDataMaps;
import org.slf4j.Logger;

import java.lang.reflect.Field;
import java.lang.reflect.Modifier;
import java.lang.reflect.ParameterizedType;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.LinkedHashSet;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Set;

public final class BuiltinTooltipProviders {
    private BuiltinTooltipProviders() {}
    private static final List<String> ENTITY_KEYS =
            List.of("entity", "Entity", "CapturedEntity", "mob", "captured_entity");

    private static final Logger LOGGER = LogUtils.getLogger();

    private static final class AdventureAccess {
        private static final Field FIELD = findField();
        private static volatile boolean readFailed;

        private static Field findField() {
            try {
                for (Field field : AdventureModePredicate.class.getDeclaredFields()) {
                    if (Modifier.isStatic(field.getModifiers())
                            || !List.class.isAssignableFrom(field.getType())) continue;

                    if (field.getGenericType() instanceof ParameterizedType type
                            && type.getActualTypeArguments().length == 1
                            && type.getActualTypeArguments()[0] == BlockPredicate.class
                            && field.trySetAccessible()) {
                        return field;
                    }
                }
                LOGGER.warn("无法访问冒险模式方块谓词，将使用文字提示");
            } catch (RuntimeException exception) {
                LOGGER.warn("初始化冒险模式方块谓词访问失败", exception);
            }
            return null;
        }
    }

    public static final List<TooltipContributors.Provider> ALL = List.of(            BuiltinTooltipProviders::appendMapPreview,
            BuiltinTooltipProviders::appendBees,
            BuiltinTooltipProviders::appendEntities,
            BuiltinTooltipProviders::appendAdventureRestrictions,
            BuiltinTooltipProviders::appendArmorTrim,
            BuiltinTooltipProviders::appendDurability,
            BuiltinTooltipProviders::appendFood,
            BuiltinTooltipProviders::appendJukebox,
            BuiltinTooltipProviders::appendEnchantments,
            BuiltinTooltipProviders::appendSuspiciousStew,
            BuiltinTooltipProviders::appendChargedProjectiles,
            BuiltinTooltipProviders::appendContainers,
            BuiltinTooltipProviders::appendPotions,
            BuiltinTooltipProviders::appendFuel,
            BuiltinTooltipProviders::appendToolSpeed,
            BuiltinTooltipProviders::appendCooldown,
            BuiltinTooltipProviders::appendDecorations,
            BuiltinTooltipProviders::appendOminousBottle,
            BuiltinTooltipProviders::appendCompost,
            BuiltinTooltipProviders::appendDyeColor,
            BuiltinTooltipProviders::appendLodestone,
            BuiltinTooltipProviders::appendBookContent,
            BuiltinTooltipProviders::appendInstrument,
            BuiltinTooltipProviders::appendProfile,
            BuiltinTooltipProviders::appendSpecialAttributes,
            BuiltinTooltipProviders::appendMisc
    );

    private static void appendMapPreview(TooltipContext ctx, List<TooltipContribution> sink) {
        MapId mapId = ctx.stack().get(DataComponents.MAP_ID);
        if (mapId != null) {
            sink.add(TooltipContribution.visual(TooltipCategory.IDENTITY, -10, "map_preview", new VisualComponents.MapPreview(mapId)));
        }
    }

    private static void appendBees(TooltipContext ctx, List<TooltipContribution> sink) {
        List<Occupant> bees = ctx.stack().get(DataComponents.BEES);
        if (bees != null && !bees.isEmpty()) {
            List<VisualComponents.EntityEntry> entries = new ArrayList<>(bees.size());
            for (Occupant occupant : bees) {
                CompoundTag tag = occupant.entityData().copyTag();
                String entityId = tag.contains("id") ? tag.getString("id") : "minecraft:bee";
                entries.add(new VisualComponents.EntityEntry(entityId, occupant.ticksInHive(), occupant.minTicksInHive()));
            }
            sink.add(TooltipContribution.visual(TooltipCategory.CUSTOM, 0, "bees_dynamic",
                    new VisualComponents.DynamicEntityPreview(entries, "🐝 居住蜜蜂", new ItemStack(Items.HONEYCOMB))));
        }
    }

    private static void appendEntities(TooltipContext ctx, List<TooltipContribution> sink) {
        ItemStack stack = ctx.stack();

        CustomData bucketData = stack.get(DataComponents.BUCKET_ENTITY_DATA);
        if (bucketData != null) {
            String id = extractEntityId(bucketData.copyTag());
            if (id != null) {
                EntityType<?> type = resolveLivingEntity(id);
                if (type != null) {
                    sink.add(TooltipContribution.visual(TooltipCategory.CUSTOM, 0, "bucket_entity",
                            new VisualComponents.DynamicEntityPreview(
                                    List.of(new VisualComponents.EntityEntry(id, 0, 0)),
                                    "🐟 桶装生物",
                                    new ItemStack(Items.WATER_BUCKET)
                            )));
                    return;
                }
            }
        }

        if (!isEntityPlacerItem(stack)) {
            String capturedId = null;

            CustomData entityData = stack.get(DataComponents.ENTITY_DATA);
            if (entityData != null) {
                capturedId = extractEntityId(entityData.copyTag());
            }

            if (capturedId == null) {
                CustomData customData = stack.get(DataComponents.CUSTOM_DATA);
                if (customData != null) {
                    capturedId = extractEntityIdFromCustomData(customData.copyTag());
                }
            }

            if (capturedId != null) {
                EntityType<?> type = resolveLivingEntity(capturedId);
                if (type != null) {
                    sink.add(TooltipContribution.visual(TooltipCategory.CUSTOM, 0, "captured_entity",
                            new VisualComponents.DynamicEntityPreview(
                                    List.of(new VisualComponents.EntityEntry(capturedId, 0, 0)),
                                    "📦 捕获生物",
                                    new ItemStack(Items.LEAD)
                            )));
                    return;
                }
            }
        }

        CustomData blockEntityData = stack.get(DataComponents.BLOCK_ENTITY_DATA);
        if (blockEntityData != null) {
            CompoundTag tag = blockEntityData.copyTag();
            if (tag.contains("SpawnData", CompoundTag.TAG_COMPOUND)) {
                CompoundTag spawnData = tag.getCompound("SpawnData");
                if (spawnData.contains("entity", CompoundTag.TAG_COMPOUND)) {
                    String spawnerMob = spawnData.getCompound("entity").getString("id");
                    if (!spawnerMob.isEmpty() && isRegisteredEntity(spawnerMob)) {
                        sink.add(TooltipContribution.visual(TooltipCategory.CUSTOM, 0, "spawner_entity",
                                new VisualComponents.DynamicEntityPreview(
                                        List.of(new VisualComponents.EntityEntry(spawnerMob, 0, 0)),
                                        "🔥 刷怪笼核心生物",
                                        new ItemStack(Items.SPAWNER)
                                )));
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }

    private static boolean isEntityPlacerItem(ItemStack stack) {
        Item item = stack.getItem();
        if (item instanceof SpawnEggItem) return true;
        if (item instanceof HangingEntityItem) return true;
        if (item instanceof ArmorStandItem) return true;
        if (item instanceof BoatItem || item instanceof MinecartItem) return true;
        if (item instanceof EndCrystalItem) return true;
        return stack.is(Items.PAINTING) || stack.is(Items.ITEM_FRAME) || stack.is(Items.GLOW_ITEM_FRAME) || stack.is(Items.ARMOR_STAND);
    }

    private static EntityType<?> resolveLivingEntity(String idStr) {
        ResourceLocation id = ResourceLocation.tryParse(idStr);
        if (id == null) return null;

        if (!BuiltInRegistries.ENTITY_TYPE.containsKey(id)) {
            return null;
        }

        EntityType<?> type = BuiltInRegistries.ENTITY_TYPE.get(id);
        if (type == null) return null;

        if (!LivingEntity.class.isAssignableFrom(type.getBaseClass())) {
            return null;
        }
        if (type == EntityType.ARMOR_STAND) {
            return null;
        }

        return type;
    }

    private static boolean isRegisteredEntity(String idStr) {
        ResourceLocation id = ResourceLocation.tryParse(idStr);
        return id != null && BuiltInRegistries.ENTITY_TYPE.containsKey(id);
    }

    private static String extractEntityId(CompoundTag tag) {
        if (tag == null || !tag.contains("id", CompoundTag.TAG_STRING)) return null;
        String id = tag.getString("id");
        return id.isBlank() ? null : id;
    }


    private static String extractEntityIdFromCustomData(CompoundTag tag) {
        if (tag == null) return null;

        String direct = extractEntityId(tag);
        if (direct != null) return direct;

        for (String key : ENTITY_KEYS) {
            String id = null;
            if (tag.contains(key, CompoundTag.TAG_COMPOUND)) {
                id = extractEntityId(tag.getCompound(key));
            } else if (tag.contains(key, CompoundTag.TAG_STRING)) {
                id = tag.getString(key);
