            }

            if (id != null && !id.isBlank()) return id;
        }

        String fallback = tag.getString("entity_id");
        return fallback.isBlank() ? null : fallback;
    }

    private static void appendAdventureRestrictions(
            TooltipContext ctx, List<TooltipContribution> sink
    ) {
        appendAdventureRestriction(
                ctx.stack().get(DataComponents.CAN_BREAK),
                sink, 10, "can_break",
                "⚒ 冒险模式可破坏:",
                "⚒ 包含冒险模式破坏限制",
                ChatFormatting.RED, 0xFFFF5555
        );
        appendAdventureRestriction(
                ctx.stack().get(DataComponents.CAN_PLACE_ON),
                sink, 11, "can_place",
                "🧱 冒险模式可放置于:",
                "🧱 包含冒险模式放置限制",
                ChatFormatting.GREEN, 0xFF55FF55
        );
    }

    private static void appendAdventureRestriction(
            AdventureModePredicate predicate,
            List<TooltipContribution> sink,
            int priority,
            String key,
            String title,
            String fallback,
            ChatFormatting formatting,
            int color
    ) {
        if (predicate == null || !predicate.showInTooltip()) return;

        List<ItemStack> blocks = resolveAdventureBlocks(predicate);
        sink.add(blocks.isEmpty()
                ? TooltipContribution.text(
                TooltipCategory.TOOL, priority, key,
                Component.literal(fallback).withStyle(formatting)
        )
                : TooltipContribution.visual(
                TooltipCategory.TOOL, priority, key + "_grid",
                new VisualComponents.BlockGridPreview(blocks, title, color)
        ));
    }

    private static List<ItemStack> resolveAdventureBlocks(
            AdventureModePredicate predicate
    ) {
        if (AdventureAccess.FIELD == null || AdventureAccess.readFailed) return List.of();

        try {
            Object value = AdventureAccess.FIELD.get(predicate);
            if (!(value instanceof List<?> predicates)) return List.of();

            Set<Item> items = new LinkedHashSet<>();
            for (Object candidate : predicates) {
                if (!(candidate instanceof BlockPredicate blockPredicate)) continue;

                blockPredicate.blocks().ifPresent(blocks -> {
                    for (Holder<Block> holder : blocks) {
                        Item item = holder.value().asItem();
                        if (item != Items.AIR) items.add(item);
                    }
                });
            }

            return items.stream().map(ItemStack::new).toList();
        } catch (IllegalAccessException | RuntimeException exception) {
            AdventureAccess.readFailed = true;
            LOGGER.warn("读取冒险模式方块谓词失败，将使用文字提示", exception);
            return List.of();
        }
    }

    private static void appendArmorTrim(TooltipContext ctx, List<TooltipContribution> sink) {
        ArmorTrim trim = ctx.stack().get(DataComponents.TRIM);
        if (trim != null) {
            sink.add(TooltipContribution.visual(TooltipCategory.ATTRIBUTES, 5, "trim_visual", new VisualComponents.TrimPreview(trim)));
        }
    }



    private static void appendDurability(TooltipContext ctx, List<TooltipContribution> sink) {
        ItemStack stack = ctx.stack();
        if (Config.SHOW_DURABILITY.get() && stack.isDamageableItem()) {
            int remaining = stack.getMaxDamage() - stack.getDamageValue();
            sink.add(TooltipContribution.visual(TooltipCategory.DURABILITY, 0, "durability_bar",
                    new VisualComponents.DurabilityBar(remaining, stack.getMaxDamage())));
        }
    }

    private static void appendFood(TooltipContext ctx, List<TooltipContribution> sink) {
        if (!Config.SHOW_FOOD.get()) return;
        FoodProperties food = ctx.stack().get(DataComponents.FOOD);
        if (food != null) {
            sink.add(TooltipContribution.visual(TooltipCategory.FOOD, 0, "food_visual", new VisualComponents.FoodComponent(food)));
        }
    }

    private static void appendJukebox(TooltipContext ctx, List<TooltipContribution> sink) {
        JukeboxPlayable jukebox = ctx.stack().get(DataComponents.JUKEBOX_PLAYABLE);
        if (jukebox != null) {
            HolderLookup.Provider registries = ctx.vanillaContext() != null ? ctx.vanillaContext().registries() : null;
            if (registries != null) {
                jukebox.song().unwrap(registries).ifPresent(songHolder -> {
                    var song = songHolder.value();
                    sink.add(TooltipContribution.visual(TooltipCategory.CUSTOM, 0, "jukebox_card",
                            new VisualComponents.JukeboxCard(song.description(), (int) song.lengthInSeconds(), song.comparatorOutput())));
                });
            }
        }
    }

    private static void appendEnchantments(TooltipContext ctx, List<TooltipContribution> sink) {
        if (!ctx.detailMode()) return;
        ItemEnchantments enchants = ctx.stack().get(DataComponents.ENCHANTMENTS);
        ItemEnchantments storedEnchants = ctx.stack().get(DataComponents.STORED_ENCHANTMENTS);
        ItemEnchantments targetEnchants = enchants != null && !enchants.isEmpty() ? enchants : storedEnchants;
        if (targetEnchants != null) {
            for (var entry : targetEnchants.entrySet()) {
                int lvl = entry.getIntValue();
                int max = entry.getKey().value().getMaxLevel();
                boolean isMax = lvl >= max;
                MutableComponent line = Component.literal(isMax ? "★ " : "✦ ")
                        .append(Enchantment.getFullname(entry.getKey(), lvl));
                sink.add(TooltipContribution.text(TooltipCategory.ENCHANTMENTS, isMax ? -1 : 1,
                        "ench:" + entry.getKey().getRegisteredName(),
                        line.withStyle(isMax ? ChatFormatting.GOLD : ChatFormatting.AQUA)));
            }
        }
    }

    private static void appendSuspiciousStew(
            TooltipContext ctx, List<TooltipContribution> sink
    ) {
        if (!Config.SHOW_EFFECTS.get()) return;

        SuspiciousStewEffects stew =
                ctx.stack().get(DataComponents.SUSPICIOUS_STEW_EFFECTS);
        if (stew == null || stew.effects().isEmpty()) return;

        List<MobEffectInstance> effects = stew.effects().stream()
                .map(effect -> new MobEffectInstance(effect.effect(), effect.duration()))
                .toList();

        sink.add(TooltipContribution.visual(
                TooltipCategory.EFFECTS, 0, "stew_effects",
                new VisualComponents.PotionEffects(effects, TooltipUtil.tickRate(ctx))
        ));
    }

    private static void appendChargedProjectiles(TooltipContext ctx, List<TooltipContribution> sink) {
        ChargedProjectiles charged = ctx.stack().get(DataComponents.CHARGED_PROJECTILES);
        if (charged != null && !charged.isEmpty()) {
            sink.add(TooltipContribution.visual(TooltipCategory.TOOL, 0, "charged_grid",
                    new VisualComponents.ContainerGrid(charged.getItems())));
        }
    }

    private static void appendContainers(TooltipContext ctx, List<TooltipContribution> sink) {
        if (!Config.SHOW_CONTAINER_PREVIEW.get()) return;
        List<ItemStack> previewItems = new ArrayList<>();
        ItemContainerContents contents = ctx.stack().get(DataComponents.CONTAINER);
        if (contents != null) contents.nonEmptyItems().forEach(previewItems::add);
        BundleContents bundle = ctx.stack().get(DataComponents.BUNDLE_CONTENTS);
        if (bundle != null && !bundle.isEmpty()) bundle.itemsCopy().forEach(previewItems::add);
        if (!previewItems.isEmpty()) {
            sink.add(TooltipContribution.visual(TooltipCategory.CONTAINER_PREVIEW, 0, "container_grid",
                    new VisualComponents.ContainerGrid(previewItems)));
        }

        SeededContainerLoot loot = ctx.stack().get(DataComponents.CONTAINER_LOOT);
        if (loot != null) {
            sink.add(TooltipContribution.text(TooltipCategory.CONTAINER_PREVIEW, -5, "container_loot",
                    Component.literal("🎁 随机战利品容器 (" + loot.lootTable().location().getPath() + ")").withStyle(ChatFormatting.GOLD)));
        }
    }

    private static void appendPotions(TooltipContext ctx, List<TooltipContribution> sink) {
        if (!Config.SHOW_EFFECTS.get()) return;
        PotionContents potion = ctx.stack().get(DataComponents.POTION_CONTENTS);
        if (potion != null && potion.hasEffects()) {
            List<MobEffectInstance> effects = new ArrayList<>();
            potion.getAllEffects().forEach(effects::add);
            float tickRate = TooltipUtil.tickRate(ctx);
            sink.add(TooltipContribution.visual(TooltipCategory.EFFECTS, 0, "potion_visual",
                    new VisualComponents.PotionEffects(effects, tickRate)));
        }
    }

    private static void appendFuel(
            TooltipContext ctx, List<TooltipContribution> sink
    ) {
        if (!Config.SHOW_FUEL.get()) return;

        int burn = ctx.stack().getBurnTime(null);
        if (burn <= 0) return;

        sink.add(TooltipContribution.visual(
                TooltipCategory.FUEL, 0, "fuel_visual",
                new VisualComponents.FuelGauge(burn)
        ));
    }


    private static void appendToolSpeed(
            TooltipContext ctx, List<TooltipContribution> sink
    ) {
        if (!Config.SHOW_TOOL_SPEED.get()) return;

        Tool tool = ctx.stack().get(DataComponents.TOOL);
        if (tool == null) return;

        float maximum = tool.defaultMiningSpeed();
        for (var rule : tool.rules()) {
            maximum = Math.max(maximum, rule.speed().orElse(tool.defaultMiningSpeed()));
        }

        sink.add(TooltipContribution.text(
                TooltipCategory.TOOL, 0, "tool",
                Component.literal(String.format(
                        java.util.Locale.ROOT, "⛏ 挖掘倍率: %.1fx", maximum
                )).withStyle(ChatFormatting.DARK_AQUA)
        ));
    }


    private static void appendCooldown(TooltipContext ctx, List<TooltipContribution> sink) {
        if (Config.SHOW_COOLDOWN.get() && ctx.player() != null && ctx.player().getCooldowns().isOnCooldown(ctx.stack().getItem())) {
            float percent = ctx.player().getCooldowns().getCooldownPercent(ctx.stack().getItem(), 0.0F);
            sink.add(TooltipContribution.visual(TooltipCategory.COOLDOWN, 0, "cooldown_gauge",
                    new VisualComponents.CooldownGauge(percent)));
        }
    }

    private static void appendDecorations(TooltipContext ctx, List<TooltipContribution> sink) {
        PotDecorations pot = ctx.stack().get(DataComponents.POT_DECORATIONS);
        if (pot != null) {
            List<ItemStack> sherds = new ArrayList<>();
            pot.back().ifPresent(i -> sherds.add(new ItemStack(i)));
            pot.left().ifPresent(i -> sherds.add(new ItemStack(i)));
            pot.right().ifPresent(i -> sherds.add(new ItemStack(i)));
            pot.front().ifPresent(i -> sherds.add(new ItemStack(i)));
            if (!sherds.isEmpty()) {
                sink.add(TooltipContribution.visual(TooltipCategory.CUSTOM, 0, "pot_grid", new VisualComponents.ContainerGrid(sherds)));
            }
        }

        BannerPatternLayers banner = ctx.stack().get(DataComponents.BANNER_PATTERNS);
        if (banner != null && !banner.layers().isEmpty()) {
            sink.add(TooltipContribution.text(TooltipCategory.CUSTOM, 0, "banner_layers",
                    Component.literal("🚩 旗帜图案层数: " + banner.layers().size()).withStyle(ChatFormatting.LIGHT_PURPLE)));
        }

        DyeColor baseColor = ctx.stack().get(DataComponents.BASE_COLOR);
        if (baseColor != null) {
            sink.add(TooltipContribution.text(TooltipCategory.CUSTOM, 1, "base_color",
                    Component.literal("🛡 基础底色: " + baseColor.getName()).withStyle(ChatFormatting.GRAY)));
        }
    }

    private static void appendOminousBottle(TooltipContext ctx, List<TooltipContribution> sink) {
        Integer amp = ctx.stack().get(DataComponents.OMINOUS_BOTTLE_AMPLIFIER);
        if (amp != null) {
            sink.add(TooltipContribution.visual(TooltipCategory.EFFECTS, -5, "ominous_badge", new VisualComponents.OminousBottleBadge(amp)));
        }
    }

    private static void appendCompost(TooltipContext ctx, List<TooltipContribution> sink) {
        if (!Config.SHOW_COMPOST.get()) return;
        Compostable compostable = ctx.stack().getItemHolder().getData(NeoForgeDataMaps.COMPOSTABLES);
        float chance = compostable != null ? compostable.chance() : ComposterBlock.getValue(ctx.stack());
        if (chance > 0.0F) {
            sink.add(TooltipContribution.text(TooltipCategory.COMPOST, 0, "compost",
                    Component.literal("🌱 堆肥概率: " + Math.round(chance * 100.0F) + "%").withStyle(ChatFormatting.GREEN)));
        }
    }

    private static void appendDyeColor(TooltipContext ctx, List<TooltipContribution> sink) {
        DyedItemColor dyedColor = ctx.stack().get(DataComponents.DYED_COLOR);
        if (dyedColor != null) {
            sink.add(TooltipContribution.visual(TooltipCategory.CUSTOM, 20, "dyed_color", new VisualComponents.ColorSwatch(dyedColor.rgb())));
        }
    }

    private static void appendLodestone(TooltipContext ctx, List<TooltipContribution> sink) {
        LodestoneTracker lodestone = ctx.stack().get(DataComponents.LODESTONE_TRACKER);
        if (lodestone != null && lodestone.target().isPresent()) {
            var target = lodestone.target().get();
            BlockPos bpos = target.pos();
            double dist = -1.0;
            if (ctx.player() != null && ctx.player().level().dimension().equals(target.dimension())) {
                dist = Math.sqrt(ctx.player().distanceToSqr(bpos.getX(), bpos.getY(), bpos.getZ()));
            }
            sink.add(TooltipContribution.visual(TooltipCategory.CUSTOM, 0, "lodestone_tracker",
                    new VisualComponents.LodestoneCard(target.dimension().location(), bpos, dist)));
        }
    }

    private static void appendBookContent(TooltipContext ctx, List<TooltipContribution> sink) {
        WrittenBookContent written = ctx.stack().get(DataComponents.WRITTEN_BOOK_CONTENT);
        if (written != null) {
            String snippet = written.pages().isEmpty() ? "空" : written.pages().get(0).raw().getString().replace("\n", " ");
            sink.add(TooltipContribution.visual(TooltipCategory.CUSTOM, 0, "written_book",
                    new VisualComponents.BookSummary(written.author(), written.pages().size(), snippet)));
            return;
        }

        WritableBookContent writable = ctx.stack().get(DataComponents.WRITABLE_BOOK_CONTENT);
        if (writable != null) {
            String snippet = writable.pages().isEmpty() ? "空" : writable.pages().get(0).raw().replace("\n", " ");
            sink.add(TooltipContribution.visual(TooltipCategory.CUSTOM, 0, "writable_book",
                    new VisualComponents.BookSummary("未署名 (草稿)", writable.pages().size(), snippet)));
        }
    }

    private static void appendInstrument(TooltipContext ctx, List<TooltipContribution> sink) {
        Holder<Instrument> inst = ctx.stack().get(DataComponents.INSTRUMENT);
        if (inst != null) {
            sink.add(TooltipContribution.text(TooltipCategory.CUSTOM, 0, "instrument",
                    Component.literal("📯 乐器音效: " + inst.getRegisteredName()).withStyle(ChatFormatting.GOLD)));
        }
    }

    private static void appendProfile(TooltipContext ctx, List<TooltipContribution> sink) {
        ResolvableProfile profile = ctx.stack().get(DataComponents.PROFILE);
        if (profile != null && profile.name().isPresent()) {
            sink.add(TooltipContribution.text(TooltipCategory.IDENTITY, 5, "profile_owner",
                    Component.literal("👤 皮肤所有者: " + profile.name().get()).withStyle(ChatFormatting.YELLOW)));
        }
    }

    private static void appendSpecialAttributes(TooltipContext ctx, List<TooltipContribution> sink) {
        ItemStack stack = ctx.stack();

        Integer maxStack = stack.get(DataComponents.MAX_STACK_SIZE);
        if (maxStack != null && maxStack != 64) {
            sink.add(TooltipContribution.text(TooltipCategory.ATTRIBUTES, 35, "max_stack",
                    Component.literal("📦 堆叠上限: " + maxStack).withStyle(ChatFormatting.DARK_GRAY)));
        }

        ResourceLocation noteSound = stack.get(DataComponents.NOTE_BLOCK_SOUND);
        if (noteSound != null) {
            sink.add(TooltipContribution.text(TooltipCategory.CUSTOM, 12, "note_sound",
                    Component.literal("🎵 音符盒发声: " + noteSound.toString()).withStyle(ChatFormatting.AQUA)));
        }

        List<ResourceLocation> recipes = stack.get(DataComponents.RECIPES);
        if (recipes != null && !recipes.isEmpty()) {
            sink.add(TooltipContribution.text(TooltipCategory.CUSTOM, 15, "recipes_unlock",
                    Component.literal("📖 包含配方解锁: " + recipes.size() + " 个").withStyle(ChatFormatting.GREEN)));
        }

        DebugStickState debugStick = stack.get(DataComponents.DEBUG_STICK_STATE);
        if (debugStick != null && !debugStick.properties().isEmpty()) {
            sink.add(TooltipContribution.text(TooltipCategory.DEBUG, 0, "debug_stick",
                    Component.literal("🔧 调试棒已记录状态 (" + debugStick.properties().size() + " 项)").withStyle(ChatFormatting.LIGHT_PURPLE)));
        }
    }

    private static void appendMisc(TooltipContext ctx, List<TooltipContribution> sink) {
        ItemStack stack = ctx.stack();
        Integer repairCost = stack.get(DataComponents.REPAIR_COST);
        if (repairCost != null && repairCost > 0) {
            boolean tooExpensive = repairCost >= 40;
            sink.add(TooltipContribution.text(TooltipCategory.CUSTOM, 30, "repair_cost",
                    Component.literal("🔨 铁砧累加惩罚: +" + repairCost + (tooExpensive ? " (过于昂贵!)" : " 级"))
                            .withStyle(tooExpensive ? ChatFormatting.RED : ChatFormatting.GRAY)));
        }
        if (stack.has(DataComponents.UNBREAKABLE)) {
            sink.add(TooltipContribution.text(TooltipCategory.DURABILITY, -5, "unbreakable",
                    Component.literal("🛡 永久无法破坏").withStyle(ChatFormatting.GOLD)));
        }
        if (stack.has(DataComponents.FIRE_RESISTANT)) {
            sink.add(TooltipContribution.text(TooltipCategory.DURABILITY, -4, "fire_resistant",
                    Component.literal("🔥 免疫岩浆与火焰焚毁").withStyle(ChatFormatting.YELLOW)));
        }
        LockCode lock = stack.get(DataComponents.LOCK);
        if (lock != null && !lock.key().isEmpty()) {
            sink.add(TooltipContribution.text(TooltipCategory.CUSTOM, 0, "lock",
                    Component.literal("🔒 容器已加锁: 需要名为 \"" + lock.key() + "\" 的物品").withStyle(ChatFormatting.DARK_RED)));
        }
    }
}

// ==================================================
// File: tooltip\ModelStageRenderer.java
// ==================================================

package com.example.examplemod.tooltip;

import com.example.examplemod.Config;
import com.example.examplemod.compat.jei.CuriosCompat;
import com.mojang.blaze3d.systems.RenderSystem;
import com.mojang.logging.LogUtils;
import net.minecraft.client.Minecraft;
import net.minecraft.client.gui.GuiGraphics;
import net.minecraft.client.player.RemotePlayer;
import net.minecraft.client.renderer.MultiBufferSource;
import net.minecraft.client.renderer.entity.EntityRenderDispatcher;
import net.minecraft.core.component.DataComponents;
import net.minecraft.util.Mth;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.EntityType;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.EquipmentSlot;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.LivingEntity;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.Pose;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.animal.Wolf;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.animal.horse.Horse;
import net.minecraft.world.item.*;
import net.minecraft.world.item.component.ItemAttributeModifiers;
import net.neoforged.bus.api.SubscribeEvent;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.client.event.ClientPlayerNetworkEvent;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.client.event.RenderTooltipEvent;
import org.joml.Quaternionf;
import org.slf4j.Logger;

import java.util.Collections;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.Set;
import java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap;

public final class ModelStageRenderer {
    private static final int CARD_WIDTH = 100;
    private static final int CARD_HEIGHT = 100;
    private static final int GAP = 8;
    private static final int LEFT_MARGIN = 6;
    private static final int BOTTOM_MARGIN = 30;
    private static final Logger LOGGER = LogUtils.getLogger();

    private static net.minecraft.client.multiplayer.ClientLevel activeLevel;
    private static net.minecraft.client.player.LocalPlayer activePlayer;
    private static final Map<EntityType<?>, LivingEntity> ARMOR_ENTITY_CACHE =
            new ConcurrentHashMap<>();

    private static ItemStack activeHoverStack = ItemStack.EMPTY;
    private static LivingEntity currentTargetEntity = null;

    private static final Set<Item> CRASHED_ITEMS = Collections.newSetFromMap(new ConcurrentHashMap<>());
    private static final Map<EntityType<?>, LivingEntity> ENTITY_CACHE = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();

    private ModelStageRenderer() {}

    @SubscribeEvent
    public static void onClientLogout(ClientPlayerNetworkEvent.LoggingOut event) {
        clearCache();
    }

    public static void clearCache() {
        ENTITY_CACHE.clear();
        CRASHED_ITEMS.clear();
        currentTargetEntity = null;
        activeHoverStack = ItemStack.EMPTY;
        activeLevel = null;
        activePlayer = null;
        ARMOR_ENTITY_CACHE.clear();

    }

    @SubscribeEvent
    public static void onTooltipPre(RenderTooltipEvent.Pre event) {
        // 总开关与防打扰
        if (!Config.ENABLED.get() || !Config.SHOW_MODEL_STAGE.get()) return;

        ItemStack stack = event.getItemStack();
        Minecraft mc = Minecraft.getInstance();
        if (mc.level == null || stack.isEmpty()) return;

        if (stack.has(DataComponents.HIDE_TOOLTIP)) return;
        if (CRASHED_ITEMS.contains(stack.getItem())) return;

        try {
            updateTarget(mc, stack);
        } catch (RuntimeException | LinkageError exception) {
            CRASHED_ITEMS.add(stack.getItem());
            currentTargetEntity = null;
            LOGGER.warn("无法创建物品模型预览: {}", stack.getItem(), exception);
            return;
        }


        if (currentTargetEntity == null) return;
        int screenH = mc.getWindow().getGuiScaledHeight();

        int cardX = LEFT_MARGIN;

        int cardY = screenH - CARD_HEIGHT - BOTTOM_MARGIN;

        cardY = Math.max(6, cardY);

        GuiGraphics graphics = event.getGraphics();
        var pose = graphics.pose();
        pose.pushPose();
        try {
            pose.translate(0, 0, 400);

            graphics.fill(
                    cardX, cardY, cardX + CARD_WIDTH, cardY + CARD_HEIGHT, 0x90101015
            );
            graphics.renderOutline(
                    cardX, cardY, CARD_WIDTH, CARD_HEIGHT, 0x6655FFFF
            );

            LivingEntity target = currentTargetEntity;
            float height = target.getBbHeight();
            float width = target.getBbWidth();

            if (!Float.isFinite(height) || height <= 0.05F) height = 1.0F;
            if (!Float.isFinite(width) || width <= 0.05F) width = 1.0F;

            float maximum = Math.max(height, width * 1.15F);
            float pixels = Math.min(CARD_WIDTH - 24, CARD_HEIGHT - 38);
            int scale = Mth.clamp(Math.round(pixels / maximum), 10, 68);

            float yaw = (System.nanoTime() % 8_000_000_000L)
                    / 8_000_000_000.0F * 360.0F;
            int centerX = cardX + CARD_WIDTH / 2;
            int centerY = cardY + CARD_HEIGHT / 2 + Math.round(height * scale * 0.42F);

            graphics.enableScissor(
                    cardX + 1, cardY + 1,
                    cardX + CARD_WIDTH - 1, cardY + CARD_HEIGHT - 1
            );
            try {
                renderSpinningEntity(graphics, centerX, centerY, scale, yaw, 15.0F, target);
            } finally {
                graphics.disableScissor();
            }
        } catch (RuntimeException | LinkageError exception) {
            CRASHED_ITEMS.add(stack.getItem());
            currentTargetEntity = null;
            LOGGER.warn("无法渲染物品模型预览: {}", stack.getItem(), exception);
        } finally {
            pose.popPose();
        }

    }

    private static void updateTarget(Minecraft mc, ItemStack stack) {
        if (activeLevel != mc.level || activePlayer != mc.player) {
            clearCache();
            activeLevel = mc.level;
            activePlayer = mc.player;
        }
        activeHoverStack = stack.copy();
        currentTargetEntity = null;

        if (stack.getItem() instanceof SpawnEggItem egg) {
            EntityType<?> eggType = egg.getType(stack);
            if (eggType != null) {
                currentTargetEntity = getCachedEntity(mc, eggType);
                return;
            }
        }

        if (stack.getItem() instanceof AnimalArmorItem animalArmor) {
            if (animalArmor.getBodyType() == AnimalArmorItem.BodyType.CANINE) {
                LivingEntity wolf = getCachedEntity(mc, EntityType.WOLF, ARMOR_ENTITY_CACHE);
                if (wolf != null) {
                    for (EquipmentSlot s : EquipmentSlot.values()) wolf.setItemSlot(s, ItemStack.EMPTY);
                    wolf.setItemSlot(EquipmentSlot.BODY, stack.copy());
                    currentTargetEntity = wolf;
                }
                return;
            } else if (animalArmor.getBodyType() == AnimalArmorItem.BodyType.EQUESTRIAN) {
                LivingEntity horse = getCachedEntity(mc, EntityType.HORSE, ARMOR_ENTITY_CACHE);
                if (horse != null) {
                    for (EquipmentSlot s : EquipmentSlot.values()) horse.setItemSlot(s, ItemStack.EMPTY);
                    horse.setItemSlot(EquipmentSlot.BODY, stack.copy());
                    currentTargetEntity = horse;
                }
                return;
            }
        }

        if (mc.level == null || mc.player == null) return;
        RemotePlayer freshDummy = new RemotePlayer(mc.level, mc.player.getGameProfile());
        freshDummy.setPose(Pose.STANDING);

        if (CuriosCompat.isCurio(stack)) {
            if (CuriosCompat.tryEquipCurio(freshDummy, stack.copy())) {
                currentTargetEntity = freshDummy;
                return;
            }
        }

        EquipmentSlot slot = computeHumanoidSlot(stack);
        if (slot != null) {
            freshDummy.setItemSlot(slot, stack.copy());
            currentTargetEntity = freshDummy;
        }
    }

    private static LivingEntity getCachedEntity(Minecraft mc, EntityType<?> type) {
        return getCachedEntity(mc, type, ENTITY_CACHE);
    }

    private static LivingEntity getCachedEntity(
            Minecraft mc,
            EntityType<?> type,
            Map<EntityType<?>, LivingEntity> cache
    ) {
        if (mc.level == null) return null;

        LivingEntity existing = cache.get(type);
        if (existing != null && existing.level() == mc.level) return existing;

        var created = type.create(mc.level);
        if (!(created instanceof LivingEntity living)) return null;

        if (living instanceof Horse horse) {
            horse.setTamed(true);
        } else if (living instanceof Wolf wolf) {
            wolf.setTame(true, false);
        }

        cache.put(type, living);
        return living;
    }


    private static EquipmentSlot computeHumanoidSlot(ItemStack stack) {
        EquipmentSlot directSlot = stack.getEquipmentSlot();
        if (directSlot != null && isHumanoidSlot(directSlot)) return directSlot;

        if (stack.getItem() instanceof ArmorItem armor && isHumanoidSlot(armor.getType().getSlot())) {
            return armor.getType().getSlot();
        }
        if (stack.getItem() instanceof ShieldItem) return EquipmentSlot.OFFHAND;
        if (stack.is(Items.ELYTRA)) return EquipmentSlot.CHEST;
        if (stack.has(DataComponents.PROFILE) || stack.is(Items.PLAYER_HEAD) || stack.is(Items.CARVED_PUMPKIN)) {
            return EquipmentSlot.HEAD;
        }

        ItemAttributeModifiers modifiers = stack.getAttributeModifiers();
        if (!modifiers.modifiers().isEmpty()) {
            for (EquipmentSlot s : new EquipmentSlot[]{EquipmentSlot.HEAD, EquipmentSlot.CHEST, EquipmentSlot.LEGS, EquipmentSlot.FEET}) {
                for (var entry : modifiers.modifiers()) {
                    if (entry.slot().test(s) && !entry.slot().test(EquipmentSlot.MAINHAND)) return s;
                }
            }
            for (var entry : modifiers.modifiers()) {
                if (entry.slot().test(EquipmentSlot.OFFHAND) && !entry.slot().test(EquipmentSlot.MAINHAND)) return EquipmentSlot.OFFHAND;
            }
        }

        if (stack.has(DataComponents.TOOL)
                || stack.getItem() instanceof ProjectileWeaponItem
                || stack.getItem() instanceof TridentItem
                || stack.getItem() instanceof MaceItem
                || stack.getItem() instanceof FishingRodItem
                || stack.getItem() instanceof ShearsItem
                || stack.getItem() instanceof BrushItem
                || stack.getItem() instanceof SpyglassItem) {
            return EquipmentSlot.MAINHAND;
        }
        return null;
    }

    private static boolean isHumanoidSlot(EquipmentSlot slot) {
        return slot == EquipmentSlot.HEAD ||
                slot == EquipmentSlot.CHEST ||
                slot == EquipmentSlot.LEGS ||
                slot == EquipmentSlot.FEET ||
                slot == EquipmentSlot.MAINHAND ||
                slot == EquipmentSlot.OFFHAND;
    }

    private static void renderSpinningEntity(
            GuiGraphics graphics,
            int posX,
            int posY,
            int scale,
            float yaw,
            float pitch,
            LivingEntity entity
    ) {
        var pose = graphics.pose();
        EntityRenderDispatcher dispatcher =
                Minecraft.getInstance().getEntityRenderDispatcher();
        MultiBufferSource.BufferSource buffers = graphics.bufferSource();

        // 先提交之前的 GUI 绘制，避免混入实体渲染状态。
        graphics.flush();

        pose.pushPose();
        try {
            pose.translate(posX, posY, 100.0);
            pose.scale(scale, scale, -scale);
            pose.mulPose(new Quaternionf()
                    .rotateZ((float) Math.PI)
                    .rotateX(pitch * Mth.DEG_TO_RAD)
                    .rotateY(yaw * Mth.DEG_TO_RAD));

            entity.walkAnimation.setSpeed(0.0F);
            entity.walkAnimation.position(0.0F);
            entity.yBodyRot = entity.yBodyRotO = 0.0F;
            entity.yHeadRot = entity.yHeadRotO = 0.0F;
            entity.yRotO = entity.xRotO = 0.0F;
            entity.setYRot(0.0F);
            entity.setXRot(0.0F);

            dispatcher.setRenderShadow(false);
            try {
                RenderSystem.runAsFancy(() -> dispatcher.render(
                        entity, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0F, 1.0F,
                        pose, buffers, 0xF000F0
                ));
            } finally {
                try {
                    buffers.endBatch();
                } finally {
                    dispatcher.setRenderShadow(true);
                }
            }
        } finally {
            pose.popPose();
        }
    }
}

package com.example.examplemod.tooltip;

public enum TooltipCategory {
    IDENTITY(0),
    DURABILITY(10),
    ATTRIBUTES(20),
    TOOL(25),
    ENCHANTMENTS(30),
    EFFECTS(40),
    FOOD(50),
    FUEL(60),
    COMPOST(65),
    COOLDOWN(70),
    TAGS(80),
    COMPONENTS(90),
    CONTAINER_PREVIEW(100),
    CUSTOM(110),
    DEBUG(120);

    private final int order;

    TooltipCategory(int order) {
        this.order = order;
    }

    public int order() {
        return this.order;
    }
}

// ==================================================
// File: tooltip\TooltipContext.java
// ==================================================

package com.example.examplemod.tooltip;

import net.minecraft.client.Minecraft;
import net.minecraft.client.gui.screens.Screen;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.player.Player;
import net.minecraft.world.item.Item;
import net.minecraft.world.item.ItemStack;
import net.minecraft.world.item.TooltipFlag;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.client.event.RenderTooltipEvent;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.event.entity.player.ItemTooltipEvent;

public record TooltipContext(
        ItemStack stack,
        Player player,
        Item.TooltipContext vanillaContext,
        TooltipFlag flags,
        boolean detailMode,
        Screen screen
) {
    public static TooltipContext from(ItemTooltipEvent event) {
        return new TooltipContext(
                event.getItemStack(),
                event.getEntity(),
                event.getContext(),
                event.getFlags(),
                Screen.hasShiftDown(),
                Minecraft.getInstance().screen
        );
    }

    public static TooltipContext from(RenderTooltipEvent.GatherComponents event) {
        Minecraft mc = Minecraft.getInstance();
        Item.TooltipContext vanillaCtx = mc.level != null ? Item.TooltipContext.of(mc.level) : Item.TooltipContext.EMPTY;
        TooltipFlag flags = mc.options.advancedItemTooltips ? TooltipFlag.Default.ADVANCED : TooltipFlag.Default.NORMAL;

        return new TooltipContext(
                event.getItemStack(),
                mc.player,
                vanillaCtx,
                flags,
                Screen.hasShiftDown(),
                mc.screen
        );
    }
}

// ==================================================

// ==================================================

package com.example.examplemod.tooltip;

import com.mojang.datafixers.util.Either;
import net.minecraft.network.chat.Component;
import net.minecraft.world.inventory.tooltip.TooltipComponent;

import java.util.Objects;
import java.util.function.BooleanSupplier;

public record TooltipContribution(
        TooltipCategory category,
        int priority,
        String key,
        Either<Component, TooltipComponent> content,
        BooleanSupplier visible
) {
    public TooltipContribution {
        Objects.requireNonNull(category, "category");
        Objects.requireNonNull(key, "key");
        Objects.requireNonNull(content, "content");
        Objects.requireNonNull(visible, "visible");
    }

    public static TooltipContribution text(
            TooltipCategory category, int priority, String key, Component text
    ) {
        return new TooltipContribution(
                category, priority, key,
                Either.left(Objects.requireNonNull(text, "text")),
                () -> true
        );
    }

    public static TooltipContribution visual(
            TooltipCategory category, int priority, String key, TooltipComponent visual
    ) {
        return new TooltipContribution(
                category, priority, key,
                Either.right(Objects.requireNonNull(visual, "visual")),
                () -> true
        );
    }


    public boolean isVisible() {
        return this.visible.getAsBoolean();
    }
}

// ==================================================
// File: tooltip\TooltipContributors.java
// ==================================================

package com.example.examplemod.tooltip;

import java.util.Collection;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Objects;
import java.util.concurrent.CopyOnWriteArrayList;
import java.util.function.Function;

public final class TooltipContributors {
    private static final CopyOnWriteArrayList<
            Function<TooltipContext, Collection<TooltipContribution>>
            > CONTRIBUTORS = new CopyOnWriteArrayList<>();

    private TooltipContributors() {}

    @FunctionalInterface
    public interface Provider {
        void appendContributions(
                TooltipContext context,
                List<TooltipContribution> sink
        );
    }

    public static void register(
            Function<TooltipContext, Collection<TooltipContribution>> contributor
    ) {
        CONTRIBUTORS.addIfAbsent(Objects.requireNonNull(contributor, "contributor"));
    }

    public static boolean unregister(
            Function<TooltipContext, Collection<TooltipContribution>> contributor
    ) {
        return CONTRIBUTORS.remove(contributor);
    }

    static void contribute(TooltipContext context, List<TooltipContribution> sink) {
        for (var contributor : CONTRIBUTORS) {
            Collection<TooltipContribution> additions = contributor.apply(context);
            if (additions != null) sink.addAll(additions);
        }
    }
}


// ==================================================
// File: tooltip\TooltipHandler.java
// ==================================================

package com.example.examplemod.tooltip;

import com.example.examplemod.Config;
import net.minecraft.core.component.DataComponents;
import net.minecraft.network.chat.Component;
import net.minecraft.world.item.ItemStack;
import net.neoforged.bus.api.SubscribeEvent;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.client.event.RenderTooltipEvent;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.common.NeoForge;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;

public final class TooltipHandler {
    private static boolean registered;

    private TooltipHandler() {}

    public static synchronized void register() {
        if (registered) return;
        NeoForge.EVENT_BUS.register(TooltipHandler.class);
        registered = true;
    }

    @SubscribeEvent
    public static void onGatherComponents(RenderTooltipEvent.GatherComponents event) {
        if (TooltipInteraction.isRenderingPinned()) return;

        ItemStack stack = event.getItemStack();
        if (!Config.ENABLED.get() || stack.isEmpty()
                || stack.has(DataComponents.HIDE_TOOLTIP)) return;

        TooltipContext context = TooltipContext.from(event);
        List<TooltipContribution> contributions = new ArrayList<>(16);

        if (!stack.has(DataComponents.HIDE_ADDITIONAL_TOOLTIP)) {
            for (var provider : BuiltinTooltipProviders.ALL) {
                provider.appendContributions(context, contributions);
            }
        }

        if (Config.SHOW_CUSTOM_RULES.get()) {
            TooltipRules.add(stack, contributions);
        }

        TooltipContributors.contribute(context, contributions);
        event.getTooltipElements().addAll(TooltipUtil.buildElements(contributions));

        List<Component> lines = new ArrayList<>(event.getTooltipElements().size());
        for (var element : event.getTooltipElements()) {
            element.ifLeft(text -> lines.add(
                    text instanceof Component component
                            ? component.copy()
                            : Component.literal(text.getString())
            ));
        }
        TooltipInteraction.capture(stack, lines);
    }
}


// ==================================================
// File: tooltip\TooltipInteraction.java
// ==================================================

package com.example.examplemod.tooltip;

import com.example.examplemod.Config;
import net.minecraft.client.Minecraft;
import net.minecraft.network.chat.Component;
import net.minecraft.world.item.ItemStack;
import net.neoforged.bus.api.SubscribeEvent;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.client.event.ClientPlayerNetworkEvent;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.client.event.InputEvent;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.client.event.RenderGuiEvent;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;

public final class TooltipInteraction {
    private static ItemStack currentStack = ItemStack.EMPTY;
    private static List<Component> currentLines = List.of();

    private static ItemStack pinnedStack = ItemStack.EMPTY;
    private static List<Component> pinnedLines = List.of();
    private static int pageOffset;
    private static boolean renderingPinned;

    private TooltipInteraction() {}

    static boolean isRenderingPinned() {
        return renderingPinned;
    }

    public static void capture(ItemStack stack, List<Component> lines) {
        if (renderingPinned) return;

        if (stack.isEmpty() || lines.isEmpty()) {
            clearCurrent();
            return;
        }

        currentStack = stack.copy();
        currentLines = copyLines(lines);
    }

    public static void reset() {
        clearCurrent();
        clearPinned();
        renderingPinned = false;
    }

    public static void togglePinned() {
        if (!Config.ENABLED.get()) return;

        if (currentStack.isEmpty()) {
            clearPinned();
            return;
        }

        if (ItemStack.matches(currentStack, pinnedStack)) {
            clearPinned();
            return;
        }

        pinnedStack = currentStack.copy();
        pinnedLines = copyLines(currentLines);
        pageOffset = 0;
    }

    @SubscribeEvent
    public static void onFrameStart(RenderGuiEvent.Pre event) {
        clearCurrent();
    }

    @SubscribeEvent
    public static void onLogout(ClientPlayerNetworkEvent.LoggingOut event) {
        reset();
    }

    @SubscribeEvent
    public static void onScroll(InputEvent.MouseScrollingEvent event) {
        if (!canRenderPinned() || event.getScrollDeltaY() == 0.0) return;

        int maximum = maxPageOffset();
        if (maximum == 0) return;

        int direction = event.getScrollDeltaY() < 0.0 ? 1 : -1;
        pageOffset = Math.clamp(pageOffset, 0, maximum);
        pageOffset = Math.clamp(pageOffset + direction, 0, maximum);
        event.setCanceled(true);
    }

    @SubscribeEvent
    public static void renderPinned(RenderGuiEvent.Post event) {
        if (!canRenderPinned()) return;

        int maximum = maxPageOffset();
        pageOffset = Math.clamp(pageOffset, 0, maximum);

        int pageSize = pageSize();
        int from = pageOffset * pageSize;
        int to = from + Math.min(pageSize, pinnedLines.size() - from);

        List<Component> page = new ArrayList<>(to - from + 1);
        page.add(Component.translatable(
                "examplemod.tooltip.pinned", pageOffset + 1, maximum + 1
        ));
        page.addAll(pinnedLines.subList(from, to));

        Minecraft mc = Minecraft.getInstance();
        renderingPinned = true;
        try {
            event.getGuiGraphics().renderTooltip(
                    mc.font,
                    page,
                    pinnedStack.getTooltipImage(),
                    mc.getWindow().getGuiScaledWidth() - 8,
                    8
            );
        } finally {
            renderingPinned = false;
        }
    }

    private static boolean canRenderPinned() {
        Minecraft mc = Minecraft.getInstance();
        return Config.ENABLED.get()
                && mc.level != null
                && mc.screen == null
                && !pinnedStack.isEmpty()
                && !pinnedLines.isEmpty();
    }

    private static int pageSize() {
        return Math.max(1, Config.PINNED_PAGE_SIZE.get());
    }

    private static int maxPageOffset() {
        return Math.max(0, (pinnedLines.size() - 1) / pageSize());
    }

    private static List<Component> copyLines(List<Component> lines) {
        return lines.stream().<Component>map(Component::copy).toList();
    }

    private static void clearCurrent() {
        currentStack = ItemStack.EMPTY;
        currentLines = List.of();
    }

    private static void clearPinned() {
        pinnedStack = ItemStack.EMPTY;
        pinnedLines = List.of();
        pageOffset = 0;
    }
}


// ==================================================
// File: tooltip\TooltipRules.java
// ==================================================

package com.example.examplemod.tooltip;

import com.google.gson.JsonElement;
import com.google.gson.JsonObject;
import com.google.gson.JsonParser;
import com.mojang.logging.LogUtils;
import net.minecraft.ChatFormatting;
import net.minecraft.core.registries.BuiltInRegistries;
import net.minecraft.network.chat.Component;
import net.minecraft.resources.ResourceLocation;
import net.minecraft.server.packs.resources.PreparableReloadListener;
import net.minecraft.server.packs.resources.Resource;
import net.minecraft.server.packs.resources.ResourceManager;
import net.minecraft.util.profiling.ProfilerFiller;
import net.minecraft.world.item.ItemStack;
import org.slf4j.Logger;

import java.io.IOException;
import java.io.InputStreamReader;
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.HashMap;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.concurrent.CompletableFuture;
import java.util.concurrent.Executor;

public final class TooltipRules implements PreparableReloadListener {
    public static final TooltipRules INSTANCE = new TooltipRules();

    private static final Logger LOGGER = LogUtils.getLogger();
    private static final String DIRECTORY = "tooltips/";
    private static final String EXTENSION = ".json";

    private volatile Map<ResourceLocation, List<String>> rules = Map.of();

    private TooltipRules() {}

    @Override
    public CompletableFuture<Void> reload(
            PreparationBarrier barrier,
            ResourceManager manager,
            ProfilerFiller prepProfiler,
            ProfilerFiller reloadProfiler,
            Executor bgExecutor,
            Executor gameExecutor
    ) {
        return CompletableFuture.supplyAsync(() -> load(manager), bgExecutor)
                .thenCompose(barrier::wait)
                .thenAcceptAsync(result -> rules = result, gameExecutor);
    }

    private static Map<ResourceLocation, List<String>> load(ResourceManager manager) {
        Map<ResourceLocation, List<String>> result = new HashMap<>();
        var resources = manager.listResources(
                "tooltips", id -> id.getPath().endsWith(EXTENSION)
        );

        var entries = new ArrayList<>(resources.entrySet());
        entries.sort(Map.Entry.comparingByKey());

        for (Map.Entry<ResourceLocation, Resource> entry : entries) {
            ResourceLocation source = entry.getKey();

            try (var reader = new InputStreamReader(
                    entry.getValue().open(), StandardCharsets.UTF_8
            )) {
                JsonElement root = JsonParser.parseReader(reader);
                if (!root.isJsonObject()) {
                    throw new IllegalArgumentException("规则根节点必须是对象");
                }

                JsonObject object = root.getAsJsonObject();
                ResourceLocation item = itemId(source, object);
                JsonElement lines = object.get("lines");

                if (lines == null) continue;
                if (!lines.isJsonArray()) {
                    throw new IllegalArgumentException("lines 必须是数组");
                }

                List<String> parsed = new ArrayList<>();
                for (JsonElement line : lines.getAsJsonArray()) {
                    if (!line.isJsonPrimitive()) {
                        throw new IllegalArgumentException("lines 元素必须是基本值");
                    }
                    parsed.add(line.getAsString());
                }

                if (result.put(item, List.copyOf(parsed)) != null) {
                    LOGGER.warn("重复的 tooltip 规则，{} 覆盖了物品 {} 的已有规则",
                            source, item);
                }
            } catch (IOException | RuntimeException exception) {
                LOGGER.warn("无法加载 tooltip 规则 {}", source, exception);
            }
        }
        return Map.copyOf(result);
    }

    private static ResourceLocation itemId(ResourceLocation source, JsonObject object) {
        if (object.has("item")) {
            JsonElement value = object.get("item");
            if (!value.isJsonPrimitive() || !value.getAsJsonPrimitive().isString()) {
                throw new IllegalArgumentException("item 必须是资源 ID 字符串");
            }

            ResourceLocation id = ResourceLocation.tryParse(value.getAsString());
            if (id == null) {
                throw new IllegalArgumentException("无效的物品 ID: " + value);
            }
            return id;
        }

        String path = source.getPath();
        return ResourceLocation.fromNamespaceAndPath(
                source.getNamespace(),
                path.substring(DIRECTORY.length(), path.length() - EXTENSION.length())
        );
    }

    public static void add(ItemStack stack, List<TooltipContribution> sink) {
        ResourceLocation id = BuiltInRegistries.ITEM.getKey(stack.getItem());
        List<String> lines = INSTANCE.rules.get(id);
        if (lines == null) return;

        for (int index = 0; index < lines.size(); index++) {
            sink.add(TooltipContribution.text(
                    TooltipCategory.CUSTOM,
                    index,
                    "rule:" + id + ":" + index,
                    Component.literal(lines.get(index)).withStyle(ChatFormatting.GRAY)
            ));
        }
    }
}


// ==================================================
// File: tooltip\TooltipUtil.java
// ==================================================

package com.example.examplemod.tooltip;

import com.example.examplemod.Config;
import com.mojang.datafixers.util.Either;
import net.minecraft.network.chat.FormattedText;
import net.minecraft.util.Mth;
import net.minecraft.world.inventory.tooltip.TooltipComponent;
import net.minecraft.world.item.ItemStack;

import java.util.*;

public final class TooltipUtil {
    private static final Comparator<TooltipContribution> ORDER = Comparator
            .comparingInt((TooltipContribution c) -> c.category().order())
            .thenComparingInt(TooltipContribution::priority)
            .thenComparing(TooltipContribution::key);

    private TooltipUtil() {}

    public static List<Either<FormattedText, TooltipComponent>> buildElements(
            List<TooltipContribution> contributions
    ) {
        int limit = Math.max(0, Config.MAX_LINES.get());
        if (limit == 0 || contributions.isEmpty()) return List.of();

        List<TooltipContribution> visible = new ArrayList<>(contributions.size());
        for (TooltipContribution contribution : contributions) {
            if (contribution.isVisible()) visible.add(contribution);
        }
        visible.sort(ORDER);

        List<Either<FormattedText, TooltipComponent>> result =
                new ArrayList<>(Math.min(limit, visible.size()));
        Set<String> seen = new HashSet<>();

        for (TooltipContribution contribution : visible) {
            if (!seen.add(contribution.key())) continue;

            result.add(contribution.content().mapBoth(
                    text -> (FormattedText) text,
                    visual -> visual
            ));
            if (result.size() == limit) break;
        }
        return result;
    }

    public static float tickRate(TooltipContext context) {
        float rate = context.vanillaContext() == null
                ? 20.0F : context.vanillaContext().tickRate();
        return Float.isFinite(rate) && rate > 0.0F ? rate : 20.0F;
    }

    public static float unit(float value) {
        return Float.isFinite(value) ? Mth.clamp(value, 0.0F, 1.0F) : 0.0F;
    }

    public static float ratio(int value, int maximum) {
        return maximum > 0 ? unit((float) value / maximum) : 0.0F;
    }

    public static List<ItemStack> copyStacks(List<ItemStack> stacks) {
        Objects.requireNonNull(stacks, "stacks");
        return stacks.stream().map(ItemStack::copy).toList();
    }

    public static int lerpColor(int from, int to, float factor) {
        float t = unit(factor);
        int r = Math.round(Mth.lerp(t, from >> 16 & 255, to >> 16 & 255));
        int g = Math.round(Mth.lerp(t, from >> 8 & 255, to >> 8 & 255));
        int b = Math.round(Mth.lerp(t, from & 255, to & 255));
        return 0xFF000000 | r << 16 | g << 8 | b;
    }
}

// ==================================================

package com.example.examplemod.tooltip;

import com.mojang.blaze3d.systems.RenderSystem;
import net.minecraft.client.Minecraft;
import net.minecraft.client.gui.Font;
import net.minecraft.client.gui.GuiGraphics;
import net.minecraft.client.gui.screens.inventory.tooltip.ClientTooltipComponent;
import net.minecraft.client.renderer.texture.TextureAtlasSprite;
import net.minecraft.core.BlockPos;
import net.minecraft.core.registries.BuiltInRegistries;
import net.minecraft.network.chat.Component;
import net.minecraft.resources.ResourceLocation;
import net.minecraft.util.Mth;
import net.minecraft.world.effect.MobEffectInstance;
import net.minecraft.world.effect.MobEffectUtil;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.EntityType;
import net.minecraft.world.food.FoodProperties;
import net.minecraft.world.inventory.tooltip.TooltipComponent;
import net.minecraft.world.item.ItemStack;
import net.minecraft.world.item.Items;
import net.minecraft.world.item.SpawnEggItem;
import net.minecraft.world.item.armortrim.ArmorTrim;
import net.minecraft.world.level.saveddata.maps.MapId;
import net.minecraft.world.level.saveddata.maps.MapItemSavedData;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.client.event.RegisterClientTooltipComponentFactoriesEvent;

import java.util.List;

public final class VisualComponents {

    private VisualComponents() {
    }

    public static void registerFactories(RegisterClientTooltipComponentFactoriesEvent event) {
        event.register(MapPreview.class, ClientMapPreview::new);
        event.register(DynamicEntityPreview.class, ClientDynamicEntityPreview::new);
        event.register(BlockGridPreview.class, ClientBlockGridPreview::new);
        event.register(TrimPreview.class, ClientTrimPreview::new);
        event.register(FoodComponent.class, ClientFoodComponent::new);
        event.register(DurabilityBar.class, ClientDurabilityBar::new);
        event.register(PotionEffects.class, ClientPotionEffects::new);
        event.register(FuelGauge.class, ClientFuelGauge::new);
        event.register(ContainerGrid.class, ClientContainerGrid::new);
        event.register(OminousBottleBadge.class, ClientOminousBottleBadge::new);
        event.register(CooldownGauge.class, ClientCooldownGauge::new);
        event.register(JukeboxCard.class, ClientJukeboxCard::new);
        event.register(ColorSwatch.class, ClientColorSwatch::new);
        event.register(LodestoneCard.class, ClientLodestoneCard::new);
        event.register(BookSummary.class, ClientBookSummary::new);
    }

    // 1. 原生高清地图 (静态渲染)
    public record MapPreview(MapId mapId) implements TooltipComponent {
    }

    public static class ClientMapPreview implements ClientTooltipComponent {
        private final MapPreview data;

        public ClientMapPreview(MapPreview data) {
            this.data = data;
        }

        @Override
        public int getWidth(Font font) {
            return 68;
        }

        @Override
        public int getHeight() {
            return 68;
        }

        @Override
        public void renderImage(Font font, int x, int y, GuiGraphics graphics) {
            Minecraft mc = Minecraft.getInstance();
            if (mc.level == null) return;
            MapItemSavedData mapData = mc.level.getMapData(data.mapId());
            graphics.fill(x, y, x + 68, y + 68, 0xFF2A2A2A);
            graphics.renderOutline(x, y, 68, 68, 0xFF8A6A42);
            if (mapData != null) {
                var pose = graphics.pose();
                pose.pushPose();
                try {
                    pose.translate(x + 2, y + 2, 0.0);
                    pose.scale(0.5F, 0.5F, 1.0F);

                    var buffer = graphics.bufferSource();
                    try {
                        mc.gameRenderer.getMapRenderer().render(
                                pose, buffer, data.mapId(), mapData, false, 0xF000F0
                        );
                    } finally {
                        buffer.endBatch();
                    }
                } finally {
                    pose.popPose();
                }
            } else {
                graphics.drawCenteredString(
                        font, "未探索地图", x + 34, y + 30, 0xFFAAAAAA
                );
            }

        }
    }

    private static String ellipsize(Font font, String text, int width) {
        if (font.width(text) <= width) return text;

        String suffix = "...";
        int remaining = width - font.width(suffix);
        return remaining <= 0
                ? font.plainSubstrByWidth(suffix, Math.max(0, width))
                : font.plainSubstrByWidth(text, remaining) + suffix;
    }

    // 2. 实体捕获 (静态坐标)
    public record EntityEntry(String entityId, int timeInHive, int minTime) {
    }

    public record DynamicEntityPreview(
            List<EntityEntry> entities,
            String title,
            ItemStack fallbackIcon
    ) implements TooltipComponent {
        public DynamicEntityPreview {
            entities = List.copyOf(entities);
            fallbackIcon = fallbackIcon == null ? ItemStack.EMPTY : fallbackIcon.copy();
        }

        public DynamicEntityPreview(List<EntityEntry> entities, String title) {
            this(entities, title, ItemStack.EMPTY);
        }
    }

    public static class ClientDynamicEntityPreview implements ClientTooltipComponent {
        private final DynamicEntityPreview data;

        public ClientDynamicEntityPreview(DynamicEntityPreview data) {
            this.data = data;
        }

        @Override
        public int getWidth(Font font) {
            return 140;
        }

        @Override
        public int getHeight() {
            return 14 + data.entities().size() * 18;
        }

        @Override
        public void renderImage(Font font, int x, int y, GuiGraphics graphics) {
            graphics.drawString(font, data.title() + " (" + data.entities().size() + ")", x, y + 2, 0xFFFFCC00, true);
            int curY = y + 12;
            for (EntityEntry entry : data.entities()) {
                ResourceLocation id = ResourceLocation.tryParse(entry.entityId());
                EntityType<?> type = (id != null && BuiltInRegistries.ENTITY_TYPE.containsKey(id))
                        ? BuiltInRegistries.ENTITY_TYPE.get(id) : null;
                ItemStack icon = ItemStack.EMPTY;

                if (type != null) {
                    SpawnEggItem egg = SpawnEggItem.byId(type);
                    if (egg != null) {
                        icon = new ItemStack(egg);
                    } else if (id != null) {
                        ResourceLocation eggId = ResourceLocation.fromNamespaceAndPath(id.getNamespace(), id.getPath() + "_spawn_egg");
                        if (BuiltInRegistries.ITEM.containsKey(eggId)) {
                            icon = new ItemStack(BuiltInRegistries.ITEM.get(eggId));
                        }
                    }
                }

                if (icon.isEmpty() && data.fallbackIcon() != null && !data.fallbackIcon().isEmpty()) {
                    icon = data.fallbackIcon();
                }

                if (icon.isEmpty()) {
                    icon = new ItemStack(Items.LEAD);
                }

                graphics.renderFakeItem(icon, x, curY);
                String name = type != null ? type.getDescription().getString() : entry.entityId();
                graphics.drawString(font, name, x + 20, curY + 1, 0xFFFFFFFF, false);
                if (entry.minTime() > 0) {
                    int pct = Math.min(100, Math.round(((float) entry.timeInHive() / entry.minTime()) * 100.0F));
                    graphics.drawString(font, "进度: " + pct + "%", x + 20, curY + 9, 0xFF88FF88, false);
                }
                curY += 18;
            }
        }
    }

    // 3. 方块流网格 (静态渲染)
    public record BlockGridPreview(
            List<ItemStack> blocks,
            String label,
            int color
    ) implements TooltipComponent {
        public BlockGridPreview {
            blocks = TooltipUtil.copyStacks(blocks);
        }
    }

    public static class ClientBlockGridPreview implements ClientTooltipComponent {
        private final BlockGridPreview data;
        private static final int MAX_COLS = 8;

        public ClientBlockGridPreview(BlockGridPreview data) {
            this.data = data;
        }

        @Override
        public int getWidth(Font font) {
            return Math.max(font.width(data.label()), Math.min(MAX_COLS, data.blocks().size()) * 18);
        }

        @Override
        public int getHeight() {
            return 12 + ((int) Math.ceil((double) data.blocks().size() / MAX_COLS)) * 18;
        }

        @Override
        public void renderImage(Font font, int x, int y, GuiGraphics graphics) {
            graphics.drawString(font, data.label(), x, y + 1, data.color(), true);
            int startY = y + 11;
            for (int i = 0; i < data.blocks().size(); i++) {
                int col = i % MAX_COLS, row = i / MAX_COLS;
                int bx = x + col * 18, by = startY + row * 18;
                graphics.fill(bx, by, bx + 16, by + 16, 0x33FFFFFF);
                graphics.renderFakeItem(data.blocks().get(i), bx, by);
            }
        }
    }

    // 4. 盔甲纹饰 (静态渲染)

            return 130;
        }

                    ? data.trim().material().value().description().getStyle().getColor().getValue() : 0xAAAAAA;
            graphics.fill(x, y + 2, x + 8, y + 10, 0xFF000000 | trimColor);

            String pattern = data.trim().pattern().value().description().getString();

        }
    }




    // 6. 饱食度 (纯静态图标，绝无弹跳动画)
    public record FoodComponent(FoodProperties food) implements TooltipComponent {
    }

    public static class ClientFoodComponent implements ClientTooltipComponent {
        private static final ResourceLocation FOOD_FULL = ResourceLocation.withDefaultNamespace("hud/food_full");
        private static final ResourceLocation FOOD_HALF = ResourceLocation.withDefaultNamespace("hud/food_half");
        private final FoodComponent data;

        public ClientFoodComponent(FoodComponent data) {
            this.data = data;
        }

        @Override
        public int getWidth(Font font) {
            int hunger = data.food().nutrition();
            int drumsticks = (int) Math.ceil(hunger / 2.0);
            return Math.min(drumsticks, 10) * 9 + font.width("+" + hunger + " (+" + String.format("%.1f", data.food().saturation()) + ")") + 10;
        }

        @Override
        public int getHeight() {
            return 11;
        }

        @Override
        public void renderImage(Font font, int x, int y, GuiGraphics graphics) {
            int hunger = data.food().nutrition();
            int curX = x;
            int fullDrums = hunger / 2;
            boolean half = (hunger % 2) != 0;
            int drawn = 0;
            for (int i = 0; i < fullDrums && drawn < 10; i++, drawn++) {
                graphics.blitSprite(FOOD_FULL, curX, y + 1, 9, 9);
                curX += 9;
            }
            if (half && drawn < 10) {
                graphics.blitSprite(FOOD_HALF, curX, y + 1, 9, 9);
                curX += 9;
            }
            curX += 4;
            String text = "+" + hunger;
            graphics.drawString(font, text, curX, y + 2, 0xFFE67E22, true);
            curX += font.width(text) + 3;
            graphics.drawString(font, "(+" + String.format("%.1f", data.food().saturation()) + ")", curX, y + 2, 0xFFFFCC00, true);
        }
    }

    // 7. 耐久度条
    public record DurabilityBar(int current, int max) implements TooltipComponent {
    }

    public static class ClientDurabilityBar implements ClientTooltipComponent {
        private final DurabilityBar data;

        public ClientDurabilityBar(DurabilityBar data) {
            this.data = data;
        }

        @Override
        public int getWidth(Font font) {
            return 110;
        }

        @Override
        public int getHeight() {
            return 13;
        }

        @Override
        public void renderImage(Font font, int x, int y, GuiGraphics graphics) {
            float ratio = TooltipUtil.ratio(data.current(), data.max());
            int barWidth = Math.round(108 * ratio);

            int color = ratio > 0.5F
                    ? TooltipUtil.lerpColor(0xFFFFB800, 0xFF34C759, (ratio - 0.5F) * 2.0F)
                    : TooltipUtil.lerpColor(0xFFFF3B30, 0xFFFFB800, ratio * 2.0F);


            graphics.fill(x, y, x + 110, y + 11, 0xAA111111);
            graphics.renderOutline(x, y, 110, 11, 0xFF3A3A3C);
            if (barWidth > 0) graphics.fill(x + 1, y + 1, x + 1 + barWidth, y + 10, color | 0xFF000000);
            String text = data.current() + " / " + data.max() + " (" + Math.round(ratio * 100.0F) + "%)";
            graphics.drawString(font, text, x + (110 - font.width(text)) / 2, y + 2, 0xFFFFFFFF, true);
        }

        private static int lerpColor(int from, int to, float factor) {
            int r = (int) Mth.lerp(factor, (from >> 16) & 0xFF, (to >> 16) & 0xFF);
            int g = (int) Mth.lerp(factor, (from >> 8) & 0xFF, (to >> 8) & 0xFF);
            int b = (int) Mth.lerp(factor, from & 0xFF, to & 0xFF);
            return (r << 16) | (g << 8) | b;
        }
    }

    // 8. 药水效果
    public record PotionEffects(
            List<MobEffectInstance> effects,
            float tickRate
    ) implements TooltipComponent {
        public PotionEffects {
            effects = effects.stream().map(MobEffectInstance::new).toList();
            tickRate = Float.isFinite(tickRate) && tickRate > 0.0F ? tickRate : 20.0F;
        }
    }

    public static class ClientPotionEffects implements ClientTooltipComponent {
        private final PotionEffects data;

        public ClientPotionEffects(PotionEffects data) {
            this.data = data;
        }

        @Override
        public int getWidth(Font font) {
            return 140;
        }

        @Override
        public int getHeight() {
            return data.effects().isEmpty() ? 0 : data.effects().size() * 19;
        }

        @Override
        public void renderImage(Font font, int x, int y, GuiGraphics graphics) {
            var tm = Minecraft.getInstance().getMobEffectTextures();
            int curY = y;
            for (MobEffectInstance effect : data.effects()) {
                TextureAtlasSprite sprite = tm.get(effect.getEffect());
                RenderSystem.enableBlend();
                graphics.blit(x, curY + 1, 0, 16, 16, sprite);
                String level = effect.getAmplifier() > 0 ? " " + (effect.getAmplifier() + 1) : "";
                graphics.drawString(font, effect.getEffect().value().getDisplayName().getString() + level, x + 20, curY + 1, 0xFFFFFFFF, true);
                graphics.drawString(font, MobEffectUtil.formatDuration(effect, 1.0F, data.tickRate()).getString(), x + 20, curY + 9, 0xFFAAAAAA, true);
                curY += 19;
            }
        }
    }

    // 9. 燃料量表 (静态煤炭图标)

    }

            graphics.drawString(font, "可烧炼:", x + 18, y + 1, 0xFFAAAAAA, false);
            graphics.drawString(font, String.format("%.1f 份 (%.1fs)", data.burnTicks() / 200.0F, data.burnTicks() / 20.0F), x + 18, y + 9, 0xFFFF793F, true);
        }
    }

    // 10. 容器网格
    public record ContainerGrid(List<ItemStack> items) implements TooltipComponent {
        public ContainerGrid {
            items = TooltipUtil.copyStacks(items);
        }
    }

    public static class ClientContainerGrid implements ClientTooltipComponent {
        private static final int SLOT_SIZE = 18;
        private static final int COLS = 9;
        private final ContainerGrid data;

        public ClientContainerGrid(ContainerGrid data) {
            this.data = data;
        }

        @Override
        public int getWidth(Font font) {
            return Math.min(COLS, Math.max(data.items().size(), 1)) * SLOT_SIZE + 2;
        }

        @Override
        public int getHeight() {
            return ((int) Math.ceil((double) Math.max(1, data.items().size()) / COLS)) * SLOT_SIZE + 2;
        }

        @Override
        public void renderImage(Font font, int x, int y, GuiGraphics graphics) {
            for (int i = 0; i < data.items().size(); i++) {
                int col = i % COLS, row = i / COLS;
                int sx = x + col * SLOT_SIZE, sy = y + row * SLOT_SIZE;
                graphics.fill(sx, sy, sx + SLOT_SIZE, sy + SLOT_SIZE, 0x44000000);
                graphics.renderOutline(sx, sy, SLOT_SIZE, SLOT_SIZE, 0x66FFFFFF);
                ItemStack st = data.items().get(i);
                if (!st.isEmpty()) {
                    graphics.renderItem(st, sx + 1, sy + 1);
                    graphics.renderItemDecorations(font, st, sx + 1, sy + 1);
                }
            }
        }
    }

    // 11. 灾厄等级徽章
    public record OminousBottleBadge(int amplifier) implements TooltipComponent {
    }

    public static class ClientOminousBottleBadge implements ClientTooltipComponent {
        private static final String[] ROMAN = {"I", "II", "III", "IV", "V"};
        private final OminousBottleBadge data;

        public ClientOminousBottleBadge(OminousBottleBadge data) {
            this.data = data;
        }

        @Override
        public int getWidth(Font font) {
            return 80;
        }

        @Override
        public int getHeight() {
            return 14;
        }

        @Override
        public void renderImage(Font font, int x, int y, GuiGraphics graphics) {
            graphics.fill(x, y, x + 75, y + 12, 0x882B003B);
            graphics.renderOutline(x, y, 75, 12, 0xFFAA00AA);
            String label = "☠ 灾厄等级 " + ROMAN[Mth.clamp(data.amplifier(), 0, 4)];
            graphics.drawString(font, label, x + 4, y + 2, 0xFFFF55FF, true);
        }
    }

    // 12. 冷却条
    public record CooldownGauge(float percent) implements TooltipComponent {
    }

    public static class ClientCooldownGauge implements ClientTooltipComponent {
        private final CooldownGauge data;

        public ClientCooldownGauge(CooldownGauge data) {
            this.data = data;
        }

        @Override
        public int getWidth(Font font) {
            return 110;
        }

        @Override
        public int getHeight() {
            return 12;
        }

        @Override
        public void renderImage(Font font, int x, int y, GuiGraphics graphics) {
            graphics.fill(x, y, x + 110, y + 10, 0x88220000);
            graphics.renderOutline(x, y, 110, 10, 0xFFFF5555);
            float percent = TooltipUtil.unit(data.percent());
            int fillW = Math.round(108 * (1.0F - percent));

            if (fillW > 0) graphics.fill(x + 1, y + 1, x + 1 + fillW, y + 9, 0xFFE74C3C);
            String cd = "冷却中: " + Math.round(percent * 100.0F) + "%";
            graphics.drawString(font, cd, x + (110 - font.width(cd)) / 2, y + 1, 0xFFFFFFFF, true);
        }
    }

    // 13. 唱片卡片
    public record JukeboxCard(Component title, int lengthSeconds, int comparatorOutput) implements TooltipComponent {
    }

    public static class ClientJukeboxCard implements ClientTooltipComponent {
        private static final ItemStack RECORD_ICON = new ItemStack(Items.MUSIC_DISC_CAT);
        private final JukeboxCard data;

        public ClientJukeboxCard(JukeboxCard data) {
            this.data = data;
        }

        @Override
        public int getWidth(Font font) {
            return Math.max(140, font.width(data.title()) + 28);
        }

        @Override
        public int getHeight() {
            return 28;
        }

        @Override
        public void renderImage(Font font, int x, int y, GuiGraphics graphics) {
            int w = getWidth(font);
            graphics.fill(x, y, x + w, y + 26, 0x55000000);
            graphics.renderOutline(x, y, w, 26, 0x4455FFFF);
            graphics.renderFakeItem(RECORD_ICON, x + 3, y + 5);
            graphics.drawString(font, data.title(), x + 24, y + 4, 0xFF55FFFF, true);
            String timeStr = String.format("⏱ %02d:%02d", data.lengthSeconds() / 60, data.lengthSeconds() % 60);
            graphics.drawString(font, timeStr, x + 24, y + 14, 0xFFAAAAAA, false);
            if (data.comparatorOutput() > 0) {
                graphics.drawString(font, "⚡ 信号 " + data.comparatorOutput(), x + 32 + font.width(timeStr), y + 14, 0xFFFF5555, false);
            }
        }
    }

    // 14. 染色拾色板
    public record ColorSwatch(int rgb) implements TooltipComponent {
    }

    public static class ClientColorSwatch implements ClientTooltipComponent {
        private final ColorSwatch data;

        public ClientColorSwatch(ColorSwatch data) {
            this.data = data;
        }

        @Override
        public int getWidth(Font font) {
            return 85;
        }

        @Override
        public int getHeight() {
            return 12;
        }

        @Override
        public void renderImage(Font font, int x, int y, GuiGraphics graphics) {
            graphics.fill(x, y + 1, x + 10, y + 11, 0xFF000000 | data.rgb());
            graphics.renderOutline(x, y + 1, 10, 10, 0xFFFFFFFF);
            graphics.drawString(font, String.format("染色: #%06X", (0xFFFFFF & data.rgb())), x + 14, y + 2, 0xFFDDDDDD, true);
        }
    }

    // 15. 磁石卡片
    public record LodestoneCard(ResourceLocation dim, BlockPos pos, double distance) implements TooltipComponent {
    }

    public static class ClientLodestoneCard implements ClientTooltipComponent {
        private static final ItemStack COMPASS = new ItemStack(Items.COMPASS);
        private final LodestoneCard data;

        public ClientLodestoneCard(LodestoneCard data) {
            this.data = data;
        }

        @Override
        public int getWidth(Font font) {
            return 150;
        }

        @Override
        public int getHeight() {
            return 22;
        }

        @Override
        public void renderImage(Font font, int x, int y, GuiGraphics graphics) {
            graphics.renderFakeItem(COMPASS, x, y + 3);
            graphics.drawString(font, String.format("X:%d Y:%d Z:%d", data.pos().getX(), data.pos().getY(), data.pos().getZ()), x + 18, y + 2, 0xFF55FFFF, false);
            String dist = data.distance() >= 0 ? String.format("距离: %.0fm (%s)", data.distance(), data.dim().getPath()) : data.dim().getPath();
            graphics.drawString(font, dist, x + 18, y + 11, 0xFFAAAAAA, false);
        }
    }

    // 16. 成书预览
    public record BookSummary(String author, int pages, String snippet) implements TooltipComponent {
    }

    public static class ClientBookSummary implements ClientTooltipComponent {
        private static final ItemStack BOOK = new ItemStack(Items.WRITTEN_BOOK);
        private final BookSummary data;

        public ClientBookSummary(BookSummary data) {
            this.data = data;
        }

        @Override
        public int getWidth(Font font) {
            return 145;
        }

        @Override
        public int getHeight() {
            return 28;
        }

        @Override
        public void renderImage(Font font, int x, int y, GuiGraphics graphics) {
            graphics.renderFakeItem(BOOK, x, y + 5);

            int available = getWidth(font) - 18;
            String heading = "页数: " + data.pages()
                    + (data.author().isEmpty() ? "" : " | " + data.author());
            String snippet = data.snippet()
                    .replace('\n', ' ')
                    .replace('\r', ' ');

            graphics.drawString(
                    font, ellipsize(font, heading, available),
                    x + 18, y + 3, 0xFFFFAA00, false
            );
            graphics.drawString(
                    font, ellipsize(font, "“" + snippet + "”", available),
                    x + 18, y + 14, 0xFFAAAAAA, false
            );
        }

    }
}

// ==================================================
// File: util\ApothDataExporter.java
