package com.example.examplemod.util;

import dev.shadowsoffire.apotheosis.Apoth;
import dev.shadowsoffire.apotheosis.affix.Affix;
import dev.shadowsoffire.apotheosis.affix.AffixInstance;
import dev.shadowsoffire.apotheosis.affix.AffixRegistry;
import dev.shadowsoffire.apotheosis.loot.LootCategory;
import dev.shadowsoffire.apotheosis.loot.LootRarity;
import dev.shadowsoffire.apotheosis.loot.RarityRegistry;
import dev.shadowsoffire.apotheosis.socket.gem.Gem;
import dev.shadowsoffire.apotheosis.socket.gem.GemInstance;
import dev.shadowsoffire.apotheosis.socket.gem.GemRegistry;
import dev.shadowsoffire.apotheosis.socket.gem.Purity;
import dev.shadowsoffire.placebo.reload.DynamicHolder;
import net.minecraft.commands.Commands;
import net.minecraft.network.chat.Component;
import net.minecraft.resources.ResourceLocation;
import net.minecraft.world.item.Item.TooltipContext;
import net.minecraft.world.item.ItemStack;
import net.neoforged.bus.api.SubscribeEvent;
import net.neoforged.fml.common.EventBusSubscriber;
import net.neoforged.fml.loading.FMLPaths;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.common.util.AttributeTooltipContext;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.event.RegisterCommandsEvent;

import java.io.BufferedWriter;
import java.io.File;
import java.io.FileWriter;
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.util.Collection;

@EventBusSubscriber(modid = "examplemod")
public class ApothDataExporter {

    @SubscribeEvent
    public static void onRegisterCommands(RegisterCommandsEvent event) {
        event.getDispatcher().register(
                Commands.literal("apoth_dump")
                        .requires(source -> source.hasPermission(0))
                        .executes(ctx -> {
                            try {
                                exportSimulatedTooltips();
                                ctx.getSource().sendSuccess(() -> Component.literal("§a[模拟 Tooltip 导出成功！]§f 文件存放在 .minecraft/apoth_dump/00_全槽位可视化对照大表.md"), false);
                            } catch (Exception e) {
                                ctx.getSource().sendFailure(Component.literal("§c导出失败: " + e.getMessage()));
                                e.printStackTrace();
                            }
                            return 1;
                        })
        );
    }

    public static void exportSimulatedTooltips() throws Exception {
        File dir = FMLPaths.GAMEDIR.get().resolve("apoth_dump").toFile();
        if (!dir.exists()) dir.mkdirs();

        File outputFile = new File(dir, "00_全槽位可视化对照大表.md");

        AttributeTooltipContext tooltipCtx = AttributeTooltipContext.of(
                null,
                TooltipContext.EMPTY,
                net.minecraft.world.item.TooltipFlag.NORMAL
        );

        DynamicHolder<LootRarity> mythicHolder = RarityRegistry.INSTANCE.holder(
                ResourceLocation.fromNamespaceAndPath("apotheosis", "mythic")
        );
        LootRarity mythicRarity = mythicHolder.isBound() ? mythicHolder.get() : null;

        Collection<Gem> allGems = GemRegistry.INSTANCE.getValues();
        Collection<Affix> allAffixes = AffixRegistry.INSTANCE.getValues();

        try (BufferedWriter writer = new BufferedWriter(new FileWriter(outputFile, StandardCharsets.UTF_8))) {
            writer.write("# 神化全部位装备 Tooltip 真实渲染大表\n\n");
            writer.write("> 标注带有 `👑[唯一]` 的宝石：在同一件装备上**严禁重复镶嵌**（只能插1颗）；未标注的为普通宝石，可在一个装备的多孔位中**自由堆叠**。\n\n");

            for (LootCategory cat : Apoth.BuiltInRegs.LOOT_CATEGORY) {
                if (cat.isNone()) continue;

                writer.write(String.format("## 📌 部位分类: `%s`\n\n", cat.getKey()));

                // ==========================================
                // 1. 词缀导出
                // ==========================================
                writer.write("### ⚔️ 该部位可洗出的词缀 (Affixes):\n\n");
                writer.write("| 词缀 ID | 类型 | 模拟 Tooltip 描述 (神话阶满值) |\n");
                writer.write("| :--- | :--- | :--- |\n");

                int affixCount = 0;
                for (Affix affix : allAffixes) {
                    if (mythicRarity != null && affix.canApplyTo(ItemStack.EMPTY, cat, mythicRarity)) {
                        affixCount++;
                        AffixInstance mockInst = new AffixInstance(
                                AffixRegistry.INSTANCE.holder(affix),
                                1.0F,
                                mythicHolder,
                                ItemStack.EMPTY
                        );

                        String text;
                        try {
                            Component comp = affix.getAugmentingText(mockInst, tooltipCtx);
                            text = comp.getString();
                        } catch (Exception e) {
                            text = affix.getDescription(mockInst, tooltipCtx).getString();
                        }

                        writer.write(String.format("| `%s` | %s | **%s** |\n", affix.id(), affix.definition().type(), cleanString(text)));
                    }
                }
                if (affixCount == 0) {
                    writer.write("| *(无)* | - | 当前部位没有配置任何专属词缀 |\n");
                }
                writer.write("\n");

                // ==========================================
                // 2. 宝石导出 (带唯一性标志)
                // ==========================================
                writer.write("### 💎 该部位可镶嵌的宝石 (Gems):\n\n");
                writer.write("| 宝石 ID (含唯一性) | 最低纯度效果 | 完美纯度效果 (已受 1/4 Mixin 约束) |\n");
                writer.write("| :--- | :--- | :--- |\n");

                int gemCount = 0;
                for (Gem gem : allGems) {
                    DynamicHolder<Gem> gemHolder = GemRegistry.INSTANCE.holder(gem);

                    GemInstance minInst = new GemInstance(gemHolder, cat, gem.getMinPurity(), ItemStack.EMPTY, 0);
                    GemInstance perfectInst = new GemInstance(gemHolder, cat, Purity.PERFECT, ItemStack.EMPTY, 0);

                    if (perfectInst.getBonus().isPresent()) {
                        gemCount++;
                        String minText = minInst.getSocketBonusTooltip(tooltipCtx).getString();
                        String perfectText = perfectInst.getSocketBonusTooltip(tooltipCtx).getString();

                        // 【关键改动】：如果是唯一宝石，高亮追加 👑[唯一] 标识
                        String uniqueTag = gem.isUnique() ? " 👑`[唯一]`" : "";
                        String gemIdWithTag = String.format("`%s`%s", gem.getId(), uniqueTag);

                        writer.write(String.format("| %s | %s | **%s** |\n",
                                gemIdWithTag,
                                cleanString(minText),
                                cleanString(perfectText)
                        ));
                    }
                }
                if (gemCount == 0) {
                    writer.write("| *(无)* | - | 当前部位没有可用的宝石 |\n");
                }

                writer.write("\n---\n\n");
            }
        }
    }

    private static String cleanString(String input) {
        if (input == null) return "";
        return input.replace("\n", " ").replace("\r", " ").replace("|", "\\|");
    }
}

// ==================================================
// File: util\ApothStudioExporter.java
// ==================================================

package com.example.examplemod.util;

import com.google.gson.*;
import com.mojang.serialization.Codec;
import com.mojang.serialization.DataResult;
import com.mojang.serialization.DynamicOps;
import com.mojang.serialization.JsonOps;
import dev.shadowsoffire.apotheosis.Apoth;
import dev.shadowsoffire.apotheosis.affix.Affix;
import dev.shadowsoffire.apotheosis.affix.AffixRegistry;
import dev.shadowsoffire.apotheosis.loot.LootCategory;
import dev.shadowsoffire.apotheosis.socket.gem.Gem;
import dev.shadowsoffire.apotheosis.socket.gem.GemRegistry;
import net.minecraft.commands.Commands;
import net.minecraft.core.HolderLookup;
import net.minecraft.network.chat.Component;
import net.neoforged.bus.api.SubscribeEvent;
import net.neoforged.fml.common.EventBusSubscriber;
import net.neoforged.fml.loading.FMLPaths;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.event.RegisterCommandsEvent;

import java.io.File;
import java.io.FileWriter;
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.nio.file.Files;
import java.nio.file.Path;

@EventBusSubscriber(modid = "examplemod")
public class ApothStudioExporter {

    private static final Gson GSON = new GsonBuilder().setPrettyPrinting().create();
    private static final Path STUDIO_DIR = FMLPaths.GAMEDIR.get().resolve("apoth_studio");

    @SubscribeEvent
    public static void onRegisterCommands(RegisterCommandsEvent event) {
        event.getDispatcher().register(
                Commands.literal("apoth_studio")
                        .requires(source -> source.hasPermission(0))
                        .then(Commands.literal("export").executes(ctx -> {
                            try {
                                Files.createDirectories(STUDIO_DIR);
                                // 【核心修复点 1】：传入当前世界命令上下文的注册表提供者 (registryAccess)
                                exportStudioPackage(
                                        ctx.getSource().registryAccess(),
                                        STUDIO_DIR.resolve("apoth_studio_data.json").toFile()
                                );
                                ctx.getSource().sendSuccess(() -> Component.literal("§a[ApothStudio]§f 数据底模已全量打包至: §e.minecraft/apoth_studio/apoth_studio_data.json"), false);
                                ctx.getSource().sendSuccess(() -> Component.literal("§b👉 直接在浏览器打开工作台导入此文件即可！"), false);
                            } catch (Exception e) {
                                ctx.getSource().sendFailure(Component.literal("§c导出失败: " + e.getMessage()));
                                e.printStackTrace();
                            }
                            return 1;
                        }))
        );
    }

    private static void exportStudioPackage(HolderLookup.Provider registries, File file) throws Exception {
        JsonObject studioRoot = new JsonObject();

        // 【核心修复点 2】：用当前游戏注册表包装 JsonOps，使其具备方块、实体、附魔等全部注册表访问权！
        DynamicOps<JsonElement> ops = registries.createSerializationContext(JsonOps.INSTANCE);

        // 1. 导出全部合法分类
        JsonArray categoriesArr = new JsonArray();
        for (LootCategory cat : Apoth.BuiltInRegs.LOOT_CATEGORY) {
            if (!cat.isNone()) {
                JsonObject catObj = new JsonObject();
                catObj.addProperty("id", cat.getKey().toString());
                catObj.addProperty("desc", cat.getDescId());
                categoriesArr.add(catObj);
            }
        }
        studioRoot.add("categories", categoriesArr);

        // 2. 导出所有词缀（带有注册表环境的序列化 + 容错机制）
        JsonObject affixesObj = new JsonObject();
        int affixSuccess = 0;
        for (Affix affix : AffixRegistry.INSTANCE.getValues()) {
            try {
                @SuppressWarnings("unchecked")
                Codec<Affix> codec = (Codec<Affix>) affix.getCodec();
                DataResult<JsonElement> result = codec.encodeStart(ops, affix);

                result.ifSuccess(elem -> affixesObj.add(affix.id().toString(), elem))
                        .ifError(err -> System.err.println("[ApothStudio] 词缀序列化警告 (" + affix.id() + "): " + err.message()));

                if (result.isSuccess()) affixSuccess++;
            } catch (Exception e) {
                System.err.println("[ApothStudio] 跳过无法序列化的词缀: " + affix.id());
            }
        }
        studioRoot.add("affixes", affixesObj);

        // 3. 导出所有宝石（带有注册表环境的序列化 + 容错机制）
        JsonObject gemsObj = new JsonObject();
        int gemSuccess = 0;
        for (Gem gem : GemRegistry.INSTANCE.getValues()) {
            try {
                DataResult<JsonElement> result = Gem.CODEC.encodeStart(ops, gem);

                result.ifSuccess(elem -> gemsObj.add(gem.getId().toString(), elem))
                        .ifError(err -> System.err.println("[ApothStudio] 宝石序列化警告 (" + gem.getId() + "): " + err.message()));

                if (result.isSuccess()) gemSuccess++;
            } catch (Exception e) {
                System.err.println("[ApothStudio] 跳过无法序列化的宝石: " + gem.getId());
            }
        }
        studioRoot.add("gems", gemsObj);

        try (FileWriter writer = new FileWriter(file, StandardCharsets.UTF_8)) {
            GSON.toJson(studioRoot, writer);
        }

        System.out.println(String.format("[ApothStudio] 成功导出: %d 个词缀, %d 颗宝石", affixSuccess, gemSuccess));
    }
}

// ==================================================
// File: util\ArtifactStatFormatter.java
// ==================================================

package com.example.examplemod.util;

import dev.xkmc.l2artifacts.content.config.StatType;
import dev.xkmc.l2artifacts.content.core.StatEntry;
import net.minecraft.ChatFormatting;
import net.minecraft.network.chat.Component;
import net.minecraft.network.chat.MutableComponent;
import net.minecraft.network.chat.Style;

public class ArtifactStatFormatter {

    public static Component format(Component originalText, StatEntry entry, int level, boolean isMain) {
        if (originalText == null) return null;

        StatType type = (StatType) entry.type().value();
        double baseVal = type.getBaseValue();
        // entry.value() 返回的是乘了 base 的数值，除以 baseVal 得到真实的倍率 (multiplier)
        double multiplier = baseVal > 0 ? (entry.value() / baseVal) : entry.value();

        double percent;

        if (isMain) {
            // 主词条理论最大倍率：初始 base_high + 当前等级 * main_high
            double maxTheoryMultiplier = type.base_high() + level * type.main_high();
            if (maxTheoryMultiplier <= 0) return originalText;

            percent = (multiplier / maxTheoryMultiplier) * 100.0;
        } else {
            // 副词条单次最大倍率为 base_high
            double singleMax = type.base_high();
            if (singleMax <= 0) return originalText;

            // 向上取整精准推算强化命中次数
            int rolls = Math.max(1, (int) Math.ceil((multiplier - 1e-5) / singleMax));
            double maxTheoryMultiplier = rolls * singleMax;

            percent = (multiplier / maxTheoryMultiplier) * 100.0;
        }

        percent = Math.min(100.0, Math.max(0.0, percent));

        // 颜色评级
        ChatFormatting color;
        boolean isPerfect = false;

        if (percent >= 99.95) {
            color = ChatFormatting.GOLD;
            isPerfect = true;
        } else if (percent >= 90.0) {
            color = ChatFormatting.YELLOW;
        } else if (percent >= 75.0) {
            color = ChatFormatting.GREEN;
        } else if (percent >= 50.0) {
            color = ChatFormatting.WHITE;
        } else {
            color = ChatFormatting.GRAY;
        }

        MutableComponent result = Component.empty();

        // 满 Roll 在最开头添加金色星星
        if (isPerfect) {
            result.append(Component.literal("★ ").withStyle(ChatFormatting.GOLD));
        }

        result.append(originalText);
        result.append(Component.literal(String.format(" (%.0f%%)", percent)).withStyle(Style.EMPTY.withColor(color)));

        return result;
    }
}

// ==================================================
// File: util\Common.java
// ==================================================

package com.example.examplemod.util;

import com.mojang.logging.LogUtils;
import dev.shadowsoffire.apotheosis.socket.gem.Gem;
import dev.shadowsoffire.apotheosis.socket.gem.GemItem;
import dev.shadowsoffire.apotheosis.socket.gem.GemRegistry;
import dev.shadowsoffire.apotheosis.tiers.WorldTier;
import dev.shadowsoffire.gateways.GatewayObjects;
import dev.shadowsoffire.gateways.gate.Gateway;
import dev.shadowsoffire.gateways.gate.GatewayRegistry;
import dev.shadowsoffire.gateways.item.GatePearlItem;
import net.minecraft.core.BlockPos;
import net.minecraft.resources.ResourceLocation;
import net.minecraft.server.level.ServerLevel;
import net.minecraft.util.RandomSource;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.item.ItemEntity;
import net.minecraft.world.item.ItemStack;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.event.level.BlockDropsEvent;
import org.slf4j.Logger;

import java.util.List;
import java.util.Optional;

public class Common {

    private static final Logger LOGGER = LogUtils.getLogger();

    public static void addDrop(BlockDropsEvent event, ItemStack stack) {
        if (stack.isEmpty()) {
            return;
        }

        ServerLevel level = event.getLevel();
        BlockPos pos = event.getPos();

        event.getDrops().add(new ItemEntity(
                level,
                pos.getX() + 0.5D,
                pos.getY() + 0.5D,
                pos.getZ() + 0.5D,
                stack
        ));
    }

    public static ItemStack createGatewayPearl(WorldTier tier) {
        if (tier == null||tier==WorldTier.HAVEN) {
            return ItemStack.EMPTY;
        }

        String gatewayPath = "tiered/" + tier.getSerializedName();

        Optional<ResourceLocation> gatewayId = GatewayRegistry.INSTANCE
                .getKeys()
                .stream()
                .filter(id -> id.getPath().equals(gatewayPath))
                .findFirst();

        if (gatewayId.isEmpty()) {
            LOGGER.warn("No gateway found with path '{}'", gatewayPath);
            return ItemStack.EMPTY;
        }

        ResourceLocation id = gatewayId.get();
        Gateway gateway = GatewayRegistry.INSTANCE.getValue(id);

        if (gateway == null) {
            LOGGER.warn("Gateway registry returned null for '{}'", id);
            return ItemStack.EMPTY;
        }

        ItemStack stack = new ItemStack(GatewayObjects.GATE_PEARL);

        GatePearlItem.setGate(
                stack,
                GatewayRegistry.INSTANCE.holder(gateway)
        );

        return stack;
    }

    public static ItemStack createRandomLowestGem(RandomSource random) {
        List<Gem> gems = GemRegistry.INSTANCE.getValues()
                .stream()
                .toList();

        if (gems.isEmpty()) {
            return ItemStack.EMPTY;
        }

        Gem gem = gems.get(random.nextInt(gems.size()));

        return GemItem.createStack(
                gem,
                gem.getMinPurity()
        );
    }
    /**
     * 枚举越接近中间位置，概率越高。
     * 最低和最高枚举值始终保留非零概率。
     *
     * 权重示例：
     * 3 个枚举：1、2、1
     * 5 个枚举：1、4、5、4、1
     */
    public static <E extends Enum<E>> boolean roll(
            RandomSource random,
            E value
    ) {
        E[] values = value.getDeclaringClass().getEnumConstants();

        int index = value.ordinal();
        int count = values.length;

        int currentWeight = getWeight(index, count);

        /*
         * sum(1 + i * (count - 1 - i))
         * = count + count * (count - 1) * (count - 2) / 6
         */
        int totalWeight =
                count + count * (count - 1) * (count - 2) / 6;

        return random.nextInt(totalWeight) < currentWeight;
    }

    private static int getWeight(int index, int count) {
        return 1 + index * (count - 1 - index);
    }
}

// ==================================================
// File: util\CurioMimicHelper.java
// ==================================================

package com.example.examplemod.util;

import com.example.examplemod.registry.ModDataComponents;
import dev.shadowsoffire.apotheosis.Apoth;
import dev.shadowsoffire.apotheosis.loot.LootCategory;
import net.minecraft.core.Holder;
import net.minecraft.core.registries.BuiltInRegistries;
import net.minecraft.resources.ResourceLocation;
import net.minecraft.tags.TagKey;
import net.minecraft.world.item.Item;
import net.minecraft.world.item.ItemStack;

import java.util.List;
import java.util.concurrent.ThreadLocalRandom;

public class CurioMimicHelper {

    // Curios 官方通用主标签：#curios:curio
    private static final TagKey<Item> CURIOS_TAG =
            TagKey.create(BuiltInRegistries.ITEM.key(), ResourceLocation.fromNamespaceAndPath("curios", "curio"));

    /**
     * 判断物品是否为 Curios 饰品
     */
    public static boolean isCurio(ItemStack stack) {
        if (stack.isEmpty()) return false;

        // 1. 最标准快速判定：是否带有 #curios:curio 标签
        if (stack.is(CURIOS_TAG)) return true;

        // 2. 兜底判定：遍历物品标签，只要属于 "curios" 命名空间（比如 #curios:ring, #curios:charm）即算作饰品
        Holder<Item> holder = stack.getItem().builtInRegistryHolder();
        for (TagKey<Item> tag : holder.tags().toList()) {
            if ("curios".equals(tag.location().getNamespace())) {
                return true;
            }
        }
        return false;
    }

    /**
     * 判断是否已经分配了伪装分类
     */
    public static boolean hasMimicCategory(ItemStack stack) {
        return stack.has(ModDataComponents.MIMIC_CATEGORY.get());
    }

    /**
     * 随机抽取一个神化原有分类并写入物品
     */
    public static void assignRandomCategory(ItemStack stack) {
        // 当场从神化已注册的分类池中筛选（过滤掉 none 和你自己模组的分类）
        List<LootCategory> pool = Apoth.BuiltInRegs.LOOT_CATEGORY.stream()
                .filter(cat -> {
                    ResourceLocation key = cat.getKey();
                    if (key == null) return false;
                    String path = key.getPath();
                    String ns = key.getNamespace();
                    // 排除无效分类、自身模组分类
                    return !"none".equals(path) && !"examplemod".equals(ns);
                })
                .toList();

        if (pool.isEmpty()) return;

        // 随机抽取一个
        LootCategory chosen = pool.get(ThreadLocalRandom.current().nextInt(pool.size()));

        // 写入组件
        stack.set(ModDataComponents.MIMIC_CATEGORY.get(), chosen.getKey());
    }
}

// ==================================================
// File: util\ItemGiveHelper.java
// ==================================================

package com.example.examplemod.util;

import net.minecraft.core.registries.BuiltInRegistries;
import net.minecraft.resources.ResourceLocation;
import net.minecraft.server.level.ServerPlayer;
import net.minecraft.world.item.Item;
import net.minecraft.world.item.ItemStack;
import net.minecraft.world.item.Items;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.items.ItemHandlerHelper;

public class ItemGiveHelper {

    /**
     * 根据物品 ID 给玩家发放物品
     * @param player 目标玩家
     * @param itemId 物品注册名（如 "minecraft:diamond" 或 "examplemod:portable_furnace"）
     * @param count 数量
     * @return 是否发放成功（若 ID 不存在返回 false）
     */
    public static boolean giveItemById(ServerPlayer player, String itemId, int count) {
        // 1. 安全解析命名空间与路径（防止非法字符串崩溃）
        ResourceLocation id = ResourceLocation.tryParse(itemId);
        if (id == null) {
            return false;
        }

        // 2. 从原版物品注册表获取 Item
        Item item = BuiltInRegistries.ITEM.get(id);

        // 如果找不到该 ID，原版注册表会默认返回空气 (Items.AIR)
        if (item == Items.AIR) {
            return false;
        }

        // 3. 构建 ItemStack 并发放
        ItemStack stack = new ItemStack(item, count);
        
        // NeoForge 推荐写法：自动优先填入背包槽位，背包满了自动掉在脚边
        ItemHandlerHelper.giveItemToPlayer(player, stack);
        return true;
    }
}

// ==================================================
// File: util\WeaponReplacer.java
// ==================================================

package com.example.examplemod.util;

import net.minecraft.core.registries.BuiltInRegistries;
import net.minecraft.resources.ResourceLocation;
import net.minecraft.util.RandomSource;
import net.minecraft.world.item.Item;
import net.minecraft.world.item.ItemStack;
import net.minecraft.world.item.Items;

import java.util.*;

public class WeaponReplacer {

    public enum Tier {
        IRON, GOLD, DIAMOND, NETHERITE
    }

    // 缓存材质池
    private static final Map<Tier, List<Item>> TIER_POOLS = new EnumMap<>(Tier.class);
    // 原版剑/斧映射到材质
    private static final Map<Item, Tier> VANILLA_MAPPING = new HashMap<>();
    // 是否已初始化标志位
    private static volatile boolean initialized = false;

    static {
        VANILLA_MAPPING.put(Items.IRON_SWORD, Tier.IRON);
        VANILLA_MAPPING.put(Items.IRON_AXE, Tier.IRON);
        VANILLA_MAPPING.put(Items.GOLDEN_SWORD, Tier.GOLD);
        VANILLA_MAPPING.put(Items.GOLDEN_AXE, Tier.GOLD);
        VANILLA_MAPPING.put(Items.DIAMOND_SWORD, Tier.DIAMOND);
        VANILLA_MAPPING.put(Items.DIAMOND_AXE, Tier.DIAMOND);
        VANILLA_MAPPING.put(Items.NETHERITE_SWORD, Tier.NETHERITE);
        VANILLA_MAPPING.put(Items.NETHERITE_AXE, Tier.NETHERITE);

        for (Tier tier : Tier.values()) {
            TIER_POOLS.put(tier, new ArrayList<>());
        }
    }

    /**
     * 初始化物品池
     * 建议在 FMLCommonSetupEvent 或 FMLLoadCompleteEvent 中调用；
     * 若未手动调用，tryReplace 也会进行懒加载兜底。
     */
    public static synchronized void initWeaponPools() {
        for (Tier tier : Tier.values()) {
            TIER_POOLS.get(tier).clear();
        }

        // L2Weaponry 物品列表
        registerPool(Tier.IRON, "l2weaponry", List.of("iron_dagger", "iron_battleaxe", "iron_hammer", "iron_machete", "iron_spear", "iron_claw", "iron_scythe"));
        registerPool(Tier.GOLD, "l2weaponry", List.of("golden_dagger", "golden_battleaxe", "golden_hammer", "golden_machete", "golden_spear", "golden_claw", "golden_scythe"));
        registerPool(Tier.DIAMOND, "l2weaponry", List.of("diamond_dagger", "diamond_battleaxe", "diamond_hammer", "diamond_machete", "diamond_spear", "diamond_claw", "diamond_scythe"));
        registerPool(Tier.NETHERITE, "l2weaponry", List.of("netherite_dagger", "netherite_battleaxe", "netherite_hammer", "netherite_machete", "netherite_spear", "netherite_claw", "netherite_scythe"));

        // Simply Swords 物品列表
        registerPool(Tier.IRON, "simplyswords", List.of("iron_longsword", "iron_twinblade", "iron_rapier", "iron_katana", "iron_halberd", "iron_glaive", "iron_warglaive", "iron_spear", "iron_claymore", "iron_greathammer", "iron_greataxe", "iron_scythe"));
        registerPool(Tier.GOLD, "simplyswords", List.of("gold_longsword", "gold_twinblade", "gold_rapier", "gold_katana", "gold_halberd", "gold_glaive", "gold_warglaive", "gold_spear", "gold_claymore", "gold_greathammer", "gold_greataxe", "gold_scythe"));
        registerPool(Tier.DIAMOND, "simplyswords", List.of("diamond_longsword", "diamond_twinblade", "diamond_rapier", "diamond_katana", "diamond_halberd", "diamond_glaive", "diamond_warglaive", "diamond_spear", "diamond_claymore", "diamond_greathammer", "diamond_greataxe", "diamond_scythe"));
        registerPool(Tier.NETHERITE, "simplyswords", List.of("netherite_longsword", "netherite_twinblade", "netherite_rapier", "netherite_katana", "netherite_halberd", "netherite_glaive", "netherite_warglaive", "netherite_spear", "netherite_claymore", "netherite_greathammer", "netherite_greataxe", "netherite_scythe"));

        initialized = true;
    }

    private static void registerPool(Tier tier, String modid, List<String> paths) {
        for (String path : paths) {
            ResourceLocation rl = ResourceLocation.fromNamespaceAndPath(modid, path);

            // 使用 getOptional 严格确认该项在注册表中，并且严防获取到空气
            BuiltInRegistries.ITEM.getOptional(rl).ifPresent(holder -> {
                Item item = holder.asItem();
                if (item != Items.AIR) {
                    TIER_POOLS.get(tier).add(item);
                }
            });
        }
    }

    /**
     * 尝试将目标武器替换为随机武器，并保留原武器的组件（附魔、耐久损耗、名字等）
     */
    public static ItemStack tryReplace(ItemStack original, RandomSource random) {
        if (original.isEmpty()) return original;

        // 懒加载防护：防止在游戏开始调用前漏执行 initWeaponPools
        if (!initialized) {
            initWeaponPools();
        }

        Tier tier = VANILLA_MAPPING.get(original.getItem());
        if (tier == null) return original; // 不是目标原版武器

        List<Item> candidates = TIER_POOLS.get(tier);
        if (candidates == null || candidates.isEmpty()) return original;

        Item replacementItem = candidates.get(random.nextInt(candidates.size()));

        // 最终兜底防线：绝对不允许替换为空气
        if (replacementItem == null || replacementItem == Items.AIR) {
            return original;
        }

        // 1.21.1 推荐做法：使用原版 1.20.5+ 提供的 transmuteCopy
        // 它内部会自动处理 ItemStack 实例化并完整平移原有的 ComponentsPatch
        return original.transmuteCopy(replacementItem, original.getCount());
    }
}
